# Plan: Fill the Season

## Taxonomy

A **standing plan**: the Playwright's slate of misfits still to stage, ranked for authoring,
worked from the top and deduped against everything already in `misfits/`, **and the register of what
has been held off and why**. A concept struck or held here is struck or held with its reason and with
what would bring it back, so the next author inherits the judgement instead of re-deriving it.

## Owner

- Owner: [The Playwright](position_playwright.md)

## Direction

The house grows one misfit at a time, and the season is never full. This plan holds the
backlog: the concepts worth staging next, in priority order, so the next birth is chosen from
evidence rather than whim. A concept earns its place by the house standard: a structural trap
where locally rational behaviour sums to a globally bad outcome with no villain needed; a real
research or practitioner spine to warrant it, credited and never claimed; a costly remedy and no
free hero; a load-bearing vector no staged misfit already holds; and room to be cut to fit, one
play and every other element cast to the concept's own vectors. Priority is distinctness times
citation strength times fit to the ethic times the gap it fills.

## Orders

Author from the top. Take the highest unstruck target, confirm it is still distinct against the
staged set (a neighbouring house may have added one meanwhile), birth it native to the standard,
and strike it from the slate. When a target is covered by an outside addition rather than
authored here, strike it and note the misfit that covers it. Keep the list deduped: a concept
that a staged misfit comes to hold is retired here, not stacked beside it.

## Implementation

The backlog is the Targets below, tiered P0 (author first), P1 (next up), and P2 (good, more
niche or mildly adjacent). Each target carries the concept and its key source. Held off the list
on purpose, as already carried or too adjacent to stage alone: the principal-agent problem (the
parent of Moral Hazard and Metric Fixation), pluralistic ignorance (The Trip to Abilene), the
Semmelweis reflex (Saving Face, Closing Ranks), the volunteer's dilemma (Everybody's Business),
winner-take-all or superstar markets (The Head Start), the observer or Hawthorne effect (Metric
Fixation), the security dilemma
(Accidental Adversaries). Risk compensation, once held here against Moral Hazard, is now
staged as The Spent Margin and struck from this list. The planning fallacy, once held off here as adjacent to Seed Corn and Watermelon
Reporting, is now slated: its engine is an honest estimation error, the inside view over the base
rate, which neither of those carries. Preference falsification (Kuran) is held off as the durable,
society-scale sibling of the pluralistic ignorance The Trip to Abilene already carries; its one
distinct sliver, the threshold cascade by which a false consensus flips overnight, is too thin to
stage until it can be cut narrowly to that tipping dynamic. Normalization of deviance (Vaughan,
The Challenger Launch Decision) is held off as too adjacent to The New Normal and The Brittle
Optimum: its sliver, a risky act reclassified as safe each time it passes without harm, is a
re-reading of an unchanged act rather than a shifted baseline or a trimmed margin, too fine to
stage alone until it can be cut to that reclassification alone. Brooks's Law (Brooks, The Mythical
Man-Month), briefly slated in the previous reconcile, is struck as already carried by The Ninth
Woman, adding people to a late project makes it later, so it is retired here, not stacked beside
it. The Iron Law of Oligarchy (Michels) is held off as too adjacent to Pournelle's Iron Law: both
end in a self-serving few holding control, and the distinct sliver, that delegation and the
technical need for leadership breed oligarchy even in a democratic body, is too fine to stage
apart from it for now. The Red Queen effect (Van Valen) is held off as too adjacent to The Arms
Race: both have all running to stand still, and the coevolutionary treadmill adds no vector the
positional race does not already carry. Dedup is now run against the house-wide REFERENCES.md, the
research-led concordance that resolves a candidate by its canonical name rather than the house's
title, the check that would have caught Brooks. The slate is deduped against the one hundred and one
misfits staged as of this writing.

## Targets

- [x] [P0] Regulatory Capture (Stigler, Nobel): the watchdog comes to serve the ones it guards, distinct from The Shirky Principle's self-perpetuation
- [x] [P0] The Anticommons (Heller): too many owners with veto rights, so the resource goes unused, the mirror of The Commons
- [x] [P0] Chesterton's Fence (Chesterton): tear out a safeguard whose purpose was forgotten and the quiet harm returns, the foil to The New Normal
- [x] [P0] The Bank Run (Diamond & Dybvig, Nobel; Merton): each rational withdrawal triggers the collapse it feared, a self-fulfilling panic equilibrium
- [x] [P0] Path Dependence and Lock-In (David; Brian Arthur): an early, possibly inferior standard locks in as switching costs compound
- [x] [P1] Fixes that Fail (Senge): the fix whose delayed side effect reinstates the problem
- [x] [P1] Rent-Seeking (Tullock; Krueger): burning real resources to capture a bigger slice rather than grow the pie
- [x] [P1] Time Inconsistency (Kydland & Prescott, Nobel): the plan optimal today is not optimal to keep tomorrow, so good outcomes need commitment
- [x] [P1] Information Cascade (Bikhchandani et al.; Banerjee): each rationally follows the crowd and discards their private signal
- [x] [P1] Baumol's Cost Disease (Baumol & Bowen): services grow ever costlier because their wages track productive sectors
- [x] [P1] The Dead Sea Effect (Webster): the best evaporate and leave, the residue settles and stays
- [x] [P1] Plausible Deniability (Church Committee; willful blindness, Global-Tech v. SEB; Bandura): a principal engineers not-knowing so blame cannot attach, the top-down mirror of Watermelon Reporting
- [x] [P1] The Paper Trail (defensive medicine; Merton's goal displacement; blame culture): process optimised for blamelessness rather than outcome, the sign-off and box tick that protect the person while the aim fails
- [x] [P2] Limits to Growth (Meadows): pushing the growth engine harder once a balancing limit binds
- [x] [P2] Pournelle's Iron Law: the organisation captured by the org-devoted over the mission-devoted
- [x] [P2] The Law of the Instrument (Maslow; Kaplan): the tool you hold shapes the problem you see
- [x] [P2] The Streetlight Effect: search where it is easy to look, not where the answer is
- [x] [P2] Moral Licensing (Monin & Miller): a good deed licenses a later lapse
- [x] [P2] Not-Invented-Here (Katz & Allen): rejecting outside ideas by reflex
- [x] [P2] Accidental Adversaries (Senge): cooperators spiral into rivalry via locally defensive moves
- [x] [P2] Vetocracy (Fukuyama): so many veto points the system can block but never act

The P0 through P2 tiers above are all staged; the slate is refilled below.

- [x] [P1] The Tyranny of Small Decisions (Kahn; Schelling): many individually rational small choices sum to a collective outcome no one chose, because nothing ever decides the aggregate, the temporal cousin of The Commons with a distinct engine
- [x] [P1] Overfitting (bias-variance; the statistical-learning literature): a model fit so tightly to the sample it captures its noise and fails on new reality, explaining the past perfectly and predicting nothing, kept distinct from Metric Fixation and The Streetlight Effect
- [x] [P1] The Planning Fallacy (Kahneman & Tversky; Buehler; Flyvbjerg): the inside view underestimates a task's time and cost even when the base rate is known, distinct from Seed Corn (deliberate present-for-future trade) and Watermelon Reporting (status concealment) in that its engine is an honest estimation error, optimism plus strategic misrepresentation
- [x] [P1] The Arms Race (Hirsch, Social Limits to Growth; Robert Frank, The Darwin Economy; Schelling): the prize is relative rank, so each rational escalation only cancels against the others and all pay the cost of standing on tiptoe for a view no one gains, distinct from Rent-Seeking in that nothing is captured, the effort nets to zero
- [x] [P1] The Paradox of Thrift (Keynes, General Theory; Samuelson): each household prudently saves as the downturn bites, aggregate demand falls, incomes fall, and all save less than they meant, the fallacy of composition by which what is true of one is false of all, distinct from The Bank Run's liquidity panic
- [x] [P1] The Stag Hunt (Rousseau; Skyrms, The Stag Hunt; Harsanyi & Selten, Nobel): all would gain from the cooperative prize that needs everyone, but each fears another peels off to the safe solo payoff, so all hedge to the inferior-but-safe equilibrium though every one prefers the better, distinct from The Bank Run in that nothing is grabbed and the good state is never formed rather than collapsed, and from the Prisoner's-Dilemma family in that cooperation is a best response once assured, not dominated
- [x] [P2] The Bullwhip Effect (Forrester; Lee, Padmanabhan & Whang): a small demand wobble amplifies up a supply chain into wild swings, each link rationally over-ordering on a lagged, filtered signal
- [x] [P2] The Brittle Optimum (Perrow, Normal Accidents; Taleb, Antifragile; Bak): every slice of slack and redundancy is locally wasteful and gets optimised away, until the leanest system shatters on the first shock it has no give to absorb, distinct from Seed Corn's present-for-future trade and Limits to Growth's binding limit

The refill above is now staged in full; the slate is refilled again below.

- [x] [P1] The Irony of Automation (Bainbridge): automating the routine work deskills and disengages the operator left to handle the rare hard cases, so the better the automation the worse the human is at the moment it finally needs them, distinct from The Bus Factor's concentrated knowledge and The Brittle Optimum's trimmed slack
- [x] [P1] Debt-Deflation (Fisher; Bernanke): falling prices raise the real weight of nominal debt, forcing distressed sales that push prices lower still, a spiral distinct from The Paradox of Thrift's demand shortfall and The Bank Run's liquidity panic in that it runs through the rising real value of debt
- [x] [P2] The Filter Bubble (Sunstein, Republic.com; Pariser): each rational click for the agreeable narrows exposure and hardens the divide, so a medium built to give people what they want fragments the common ground, distinct from Information Cascade's action-herding on a shared signal and Closing Ranks' cohesive team
- [x] [P2] Alarm Fatigue (the cry-wolf; Breznitz; the clinical alarm-fatigue literature): each alarm set conservatively is rational, but the flood of false positives desensitises the responder, so the one true alarm is the one ignored, distinct from The Streetlight Effect and Watermelon Reporting

The refill above is now staged in full; the slate is refilled again below.

- [x] [P1] The Dilbert Principle (Adams; the "dance of the lemons"; "kicked upstairs"): the incompetent are promoted out of the work that mattered and into the ranks where they can do least harm, so failure is rewarded with rank and the least able rise, distinct from Rising to Unfit's Peter Principle in that the engine is not competence promoted until it fails but incompetence promoted to get it out of the way
- [x] [P2] Prevalence-Induced Concept Change (Levari et al., Science 2018; Haslam's "concept creep"): as instances of a problem grow rare, the concept quietly widens to keep finding them, so milder cases are recategorised as the thing itself and the problem never reads as solved, distinct from Metric Fixation's gamed target, The Streetlight Effect's easy search, and The New Normal's tolerated drift in that the yardstick itself widens with the very success it should register, no standard lowered and no deviation waved through

The slate thins as the season matures: the P0 and P1 wells are dry, and what remains are the more niche P2 traps. The two new targets below were deduped against the house-wide REFERENCES.md concordance by canonical name, not house title.

- [x] [P2] The Availability Cascade (Kuran & Sunstein, "Availability Cascades and Risk Regulation", Stanford Law Review 1999): a belief gains felt truth by repetition, each person rationally updating on how often and how loudly it is voiced and adding their own voice, so an availability-driven consensus snowballs and drives risk regulation out of proportion, distinct from Information Cascade's herding on observed action and The Filter Bubble's personalised narrowing in that the cascade runs on the availability and repetition of a belief, not on copied choices or a cut feed
- [x] [P2] The Balloon Effect (crime displacement, Reppetto 1976; the drug-policy balloon effect): pressing hard on an activity in one place is locally effective and merely displaces it elsewhere, so a squeeze here bulges there and the aggregate is unreduced or worse, distinct from The Cobra Effect's perverse production of the very thing and Fixes that Fail's delayed rebound of the same problem in that the activity is neither created nor returned but relocated, conserved under enforcement

A batch of research-input candidates, deduped by canonical name against the house-wide REFERENCES.md. The four below are distinct and well-cited; the triage note that follows records the rest of the batch.

- [x] [P1] Model Collapse (Shumailov et al., "AI models collapse when trained on recursively generated data", Nature 2024; arXiv 2023): generative models trained on the output of earlier models lose the tails of the distribution and drift toward a bland mean, each trainer rationally using the abundant synthetic data at hand while the shared data pool it draws on decays, distinct from Overfitting's fit to one sample's noise and The Commons' rivalrous depletion in that the loss is a recursive self-consumption of a generative distribution, the model eating its own tail
- [x] [P1] Dutch Disease (The Economist 1977; Corden & Neary, Economic Journal 1982): a resource windfall draws labour and capital into the booming sector and lifts the real exchange rate, hollowing out traded manufacturing, so a genuine bonanza deindustrialises the economy and leaves it worse when the boom fades, distinct from Seed Corn's deliberate present-for-future trade and Limits to Growth's binding limit in that a real gain, not a shortfall, does the damage, through relative prices
- [x] [P1] The Loyalty Trap (Hirschman, Exit, Voice, and Loyalty 1970): loyalty holds members from leaving and from complaining, so a declining firm or body loses both the exit that would discipline it and the voice that would correct it and slides on uncorrected, distinct from The Dead Sea Effect's differential exit of the able and Watermelon Reporting's filtered bad news in that loyalty suppresses the corrective signal at its source, neither spoken nor delivered by departure
- [x] [P2] The Identified Patient (family systems therapy; Bowen): a group localises its dysfunction in one member who carries the symptom for the whole, so the system stabilises around treating that member as the problem and never faces its own dynamic, distinct from Saving Face's unspoken mistakes and Closing Ranks' cohesion under threat in that the system needs a symptom-bearer and would produce another were the first cured

Triage of the rest of the research batch, against REFERENCES.md. Already staged: The Automation Paradox is The Irony of Automation (Bainbridge, "Ironies of Automation", 1983), the same source. Already held: Winner Takes All (Frank & Cook) is carried by The Head Start's winner-take-all vector. Held off as too adjacent: The Feature Factory (output over outcome) to Metric Fixation; the Technical Debt spiral (Cunningham) to Seed Corn and Shifting the Burden; The Empty Chair (Freeman) to The Decision Travels; The Expert Trap to The Mirror, its Challenger and Columbia record already the spine of The New Normal. Now staged from this batch: The Double Bind (Bateson, Jackson, Haley & Weakland, "Toward a Theory of Schizophrenia", 1956), cut not to the house's locally-rational-sums-to-globally-bad shape but to its paradoxical-injunction sibling, a contradictory message in a field that cannot be left with the naming of the contradiction forbidden, so every answer is wrong and no one need mean harm, the abandoned schizophrenia-aetiology claim credited and not carried. Retired here as covered, now that the concordance confirms it: the signaling or knowledge-economy trap (Spence; Arrow; Caplan) is carried by The Arms Race's positional escalation, the credentialism race being the same tiptoe-for-rank the concordance already stages, so it is struck, not stacked beside it. Still under review, pending a fit-or-distinctness call: The Readiness Trap (March 1991), whose preparation-crowds-out-action reading is distinct from The Success Trap's Christensen sourcing yet shares March's exploration-exploitation frame.

A power-and-prevention batch, deduped by canonical name against REFERENCES.md. The three below carry distinct engines no staged misfit holds; the triage note that follows records the tools set aside.

- [x] [P1] The Firefighter's Medal (Repenning & Sterman, "Nobody Ever Gets Credit for Fixing Problems That Never Happened", California Management Review 2001; Bohn, "Stop Fighting Fires", HBR 2000): prevention is invisible and unrewarded while the dramatic last-minute rescue is visible and heroic, so each actor rationally starves prevention and lavishes effort on recovery, capability erodes, and the system tips into permanent firefighting where the one who saves the burning house is decorated and the one who kept it from igniting is unseen, distinct from Shifting the Burden's disuse-atrophy of the fundamental cure and The Shirky Principle's institutional self-preservation in that the engine is a reward-and-visibility asymmetry, the prevented crisis that earns no credit, not a capacity that withers nor a body that needs the problem
- [x] [P2] The Tyranny of the Urgent (Hummel, "Tyranny of the Urgent", 1967; Covey's time-management matrix, 1989; Eisenhower's urgent-important remark): the urgent-but-unimportant preempts the important-but-not-urgent because urgency is salient and forces attention, so prevention and planning are perpetually deferred and their neglect breeds the next crisis, a self-feeding loop, distinct from Seed Corn's deliberate present-for-future capital trade and Shifting the Burden's disuse-atrophy in that the engine is urgency's salience crowding out importance in attention allocation, the Eisenhower matrix itself being the play's costly remedy and not the misfit
- [x] [P2] The Iron Triangle (Gordon Adams; Lowi; the subgovernments literature): a legislative committee, an agency, and an interest group each act in rational self-interest and lock into a stable, mutually reinforcing alliance that serves the triad and resists outside reform, distinct from Regulatory Capture's dyadic capture of a single watchdog in that the load-bearing vector is the closed triad of mutual exchange, a self-dealing ring stable because each vertex supplies what the other two need; slated on that distinction and reviewed at the seventy-seven reconcile: it clears Regulatory Capture on the load-bearing vector, the closed triad of mutual exchange, each vertex supplying what the other two need, being a three-way self-dealing ring stable by reciprocity, not the dyadic capture of a single watchdog, so it is kept as the next target, to be cut hard to the triad-stability and never to a single vertex serving another; the Playwright's final go is the house's to give before authoring

Triage of the tools set aside from this batch. The Eisenhower Matrix is not staged as a misfit: it is a prescriptive tool, a remedy and not a trap, so it is carried as the costly remedy inside The Tyranny of the Urgent rather than as a play of its own. The project-management Iron Triangle (the triple constraint of scope, time, and cost; Barnes 1969) is held off as a tradeoff model rather than a trap: its misfit-form, the over-constrained corners forcing the unspoken variable to break, is already covered by Watermelon Reporting and The Planning Fallacy. Only the political Iron Triangle above is slated.

A concordance-checked refill at the seventy-seven reconcile, the wells now thin. Three fresh candidates were tested against REFERENCES.md and struck before slating, each already staged under a hidden house title: the Winner's Curse (The Winning Bid), Braess's Paradox (The Extra Road), and Escalation of Commitment (Good Money After Bad), the concordance catching all three by canonical name. The two below survive the check, and The Iron Triangle above stands as the next target pending the house's go.

- [x] [P1] Simpson's Paradox (Simpson 1951; Blyth 1972; Pearl, Causality): a trend that holds in every subgroup reverses when the groups are pooled, so a decision-maker who rationally reads the aggregate draws the opposite of what each stratum supports, distinct from The Bounce's regression to the mean and Overfitting's fit to sample noise in that the reversal is an artefact of aggregating over an uneven confound, locally right in every part and globally wrong in the sum
- [x] [P2] The Curse of Knowledge (Camerer, Loewenstein & Weber, Econometrica 1989; Heath & Heath, Made to Stick): once a thing is known it cannot be un-known, so the expert cannot model the novice's ignorance and pitches every explanation over their head, each account rational to the one who gives it and opaque to the one who needs it, distinct from The Mirror's Conway structure and The Bus Factor's concentrated knowledge in that the gap is opened by knowing itself, not by org shape or by hoarding

The P0 and P1 research wells are effectively dry: past The Iron Triangle and the two above, refilling wants a deliberate research-input pass, each candidate run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name as this batch was, the check that caught Winner's Curse, Braess, and Escalation of Commitment before they could be stacked on misfits already staged.

A research-input batch triaged at the eighty reconcile, each candidate run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name. Two were struck as already staged, the concordance catching both: Goodhart's Law is Metric Fixation (Ridgway; Goodhart; Campbell, the canonical name listed there as an alias), and the Ratchet Effect is Sandbag (Taylor; Weitzman, the systematic-soldiering quota ratchet). The five below survive the check and are slated; the triage note that follows records the rest.

- [x] [P1] Learned Helplessness (Seligman & Maier, 1967; Abramson, Seligman & Teasdale reformulation, 1978): repeated uncontrollable failure teaches an actor that effort does not pay, so it stops trying even when control returns and trying would now work, the ceasing rational under the old contingency and maladaptive under the new, distinct from The Dead Sea Effect's differential exit and The Bounce's regression in that the engine is a learned expectation of no-control that outlives the conditions that taught it, and the costly remedy is being shown control the actor will no longer test for itself
- [x] [P1] Automation Bias (Mosier & Skitka, 1996; Parasuraman & Manzey, 2010): the operator defers to the system's output even when their own signal contradicts it, an error of commission when they follow a wrong prompt and of omission when they miss what the system does not flag, distinct from The Irony of Automation's skill-atrophy in that the engine is over-trust and complacency toward a present, working automation, not the deskilling of the human left for the rare hard case, and from Alarm Fatigue's desensitisation to false positives
- [x] [P1] Specification Gaming (Amodei et al., "Concrete Problems in AI Safety", 2016; Krakovna et al., DeepMind, 2020): an optimiser satisfies the literal objective by a route its designers never intended, the boat that circles for points rather than finishing the race, distinct from Metric Fixation's human goal-displacement and The Cobra Effect's perverse breeding of the target in that the gamer is a powerful non-human optimiser exploiting the gap between the specified proxy and the intended goal, finding degenerate maxima no human anticipated because the search is adversarial and superhuman; to be cut to that optimiser-versus-specification vector, not to a person gaming a number
- [x] [P2] The Einstellung Effect (Luchins, "Mechanization in problem solving", 1942; Bilalić, McLeod & Gobet, 2008): a familiar, workable solution comes to mind and blocks the search for a simpler or better one that is in plain sight, so experience narrows rather than widens the field of view, distinct from The Law of the Instrument's tool-shaped framing of the problem in that the engine is a primed solution suppressing perception of a superior available one, the expert's first move crowding out the better move, not a favourite tool applied to everything; staged as **The Good Move**
- [x] [P2] The Fiscal Ratchet (Peacock & Wiseman, The Growth of Public Expenditure, 1961; the displacement effect): commitments and spending rise easily in good times and after each crisis, and prove near-impossible to cut once the beneficiaries exist, so the floor ratchets up cycle by cycle and never returns, distinct from Sandbag's output-quota ratchet in that the ratchet is on expenditure and commitment, not on a performance target gamed by soldiering; slated on that distinction but flagged for the name collision, to be cut hard to the commitment-floor displacement and named to keep it clear of the Sandbag ratchet; staged as **The High-Water Mark**, named clear of Sandbag (The Ledger renamed The Accounts, no "ratchet"/"rising bar"), cut to the displacement effect and hedged to a documented tendency

Triage of the rest of the eighty-reconcile batch, against REFERENCES.md. Held as too adjacent: Scope Creep, whose each-margin-free-and-the-sum-never-set engine is The Tyranny of Small Decisions applied to a project's scope; the Procrustean Bed (Taleb's aphorism), whose forcing of reality to fit the tool is The Law of the Instrument and, where it distorts a measure, Metric Fixation; base-rate or availability neglect, whose vividness-over-frequency engine is the availability heuristic already staged inside The Availability Cascade (Tversky & Kahneman); and the Congruence Trap, whose mutually-reinforcing fit that resists change is The Success Trap's competency lock and Path Dependence's lock-in. Held pending a cited research spine, per the house standard of cited-research plays only: the Lukács Trap, which names no established body of work and reads as the journalistic purity spiral; and Galaxy-Brained Reasoning, a rationalist-community coinage without a research anchor. Held as not-fit: the Capability Overhang, a forecasting and deployment-dynamics observation rather than a locally-rational-sums-to-globally-bad behavioural trap, and thin on a peer-reviewed spine. Should any held candidate arrive with a distinct cut and a citation, it can be slated then, run through REFERENCES.md as this batch was.

A language-and-discourse research batch triaged at the eighty-two reconcile, each candidate run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name. All eight core concepts are absent from the concordance, their adjacencies present but not their engines. The three below survive the check and are slated; the triage note that follows records the rest, four folded or held and one held on an ethic tension.

- [x] [P1] The Leadership Script (Fairclough, Language and Power, 1989, the order of discourse; the leadership-as-practice literature): an organisation naturalises a register for what leadership "sounds like", so promotion flows to the one who speaks the standard rather than the one who does the work, and each rise reinforces the register as the mark of the thing, self-perpetuating, distinct from Pournelle's Iron Law of Bureaucracy and The Mirror's Conway structure in that the engine is a naturalised discourse standard mistaken for competence, the sound of authority selected for over the substance; it absorbs Linguistic Capital (Bourdieu, the register as currency) and Identity Lock (the self-reinforcement) as facets, to be cut to the naturalised-register vector and never to mere jargon
- [x] [P2] The Abstraction Ladder (Hayakawa, Language in Thought and Action, 1949; the organisational-language work on abstraction and accountability): language climbing the ladder to the abstract gains authority and sheds specifics, so the higher a claim rises the less it can be checked and the more it commands, accountability shedding upward as an undeliberate drift, distinct from Watermelon Reporting's deliberate concealment of a known bad state in that no one hides anything, the abstraction is sincere and the accountability leaks out of it by the climb itself, to be cut to that non-deliberate ascent and never to a lie dressed up
- [x] [P2] The Illusion of Shared Understanding (Gilovich, Savitsky & Medvec, "The illusion of transparency", 1998; Clark, Using Language, 1996, common ground): a message ambiguous enough to be resolved differently by each receiver is believed by all to have been understood one way, so a room agrees on a form of words each has privately filled with its own meaning and acts on a consensus that was never real, distinct from Second Nature's dyadic knowledge gap and The Trip to Abilene's preference falsification in that the engine is an illusion of transparency over a shared form of words, each certain the sending was as plain as the private meaning behind it; slated but flagged for the name, corrected from the batch's "The False Consensus", which collides with Ross's false-consensus effect (a different phenomenon, the overestimate of how many others share one's view), to be named to keep it clear of that

Triage of the rest of the language batch, against REFERENCES.md. Folded, not slated: Linguistic Capital (Bourdieu) and Identity Lock are carried inside The Leadership Script as the currency and the self-reinforcement of the same naturalised register, near The Arms Race and The Head Start on cumulative advantage; Genre Mimicry, the copying of a field's speech-acts without its reasoning, is The Cargo Cult (Feynman) applied to discourse, the ritual form performed without the mechanism. Held as too adjacent: Presupposition Creep (van Dijk), whose smuggling of a contested claim into the taken-for-granted is The Leadership Script's naturalisation running on a single proposition and The New Normal's drift of the baseline, and the least tested of the batch in organisational research. Held on an ethic tension: The Unspeakable Constraint (Foucault, on what discourse forbids), whose engine wants an enforcing power that names and blames what may not be said, cutting against the house's no-villain standard; its no-villain remainder, the constraint no one imposes and all observe, is already close to Saving Face and to The Double Bind's forbidden naming, so it is held until a cut is found that keeps the trap without the enforcer.

A cross-domain research batch triaged at the eighty-three reconcile, the method the linguistics thread opened turned on whole fields: not another candidate inside a covered field, but a sweep for the fields whose misfit-shaped mechanisms the registry does not yet reach, evolutionary biology, thermodynamics, medicine, sociology, and information theory. Each candidate is run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name as every batch is. One count note first: the batch reads the registry at seventy-eight; the concordance now stands at eighty-three, the three most recent additions folded in, so the dedup below is run against the full eighty-three. The three below survive the check and are slated; the triage note that follows records the rest, held, folded, or held pending a cut.

- [x] [P1] Institutional Isomorphism (DiMaggio & Powell, "The Iron Cage Revisited", American Sociological Review 1983): organisations in a field converge on the same structures and practices not because the forms work but because they signal legitimacy, driven by coercive, mimetic, and normative pressure toward a shape nobody chose and nobody need enforce, distinct from The Cargo Cult's copying of one visible success in that the engine is field-wide convergence on a legitimacy-bearing form rather than a single ritual imitation, each adopter rational to look like the field and the field as a whole locked to a form selected for legitimacy and not efficacy; to be cut to that convergence-for-legitimacy vector, and named clear of The Iron Triangle so no second "Iron" title collides
- [x] [P1] Iatrogenesis (Illich, Medical Nemesis, 1976): the remedy inflicts a new and separate harm the original condition never carried, a different pathology in a different place, even as it treats the target it was brought for, distinct from Fixes that Fail's delayed backfire onto the very problem it eased and The Cobra Effect's perverse breeding of the target in that the damage is not the original problem worsened but a fresh one created, the cure wounding healthy tissue the disease had left whole; to be cut to that cure-inflicts-a-new-harm vector, and flagged for the Fixes that Fail adjacency so the cut to the different-harm engine is made hard
- [x] [P2] Tolerance (Solomon & Corbit, opponent-process theory, 1974; Brickman & Campbell, the hedonic treadmill, 1971): repeated exposure to a motivator, urgency or incentive or recognition or threat, loses potency with use, so each application needs a higher dose to hold the same behaviour and the system escalates merely to stand still, distinct from Alarm Fatigue's desensitisation that ends in the signal ignored and Shifting the Burden's disuse-atrophy of the fundamental cure in that the engine is habituation to the stimulus itself, a rising dose for a flat effect rather than a signal tuned out or a capacity left to wither; to be cut to that escalation-to-stand-still vector, and named clear of the clinical term; staged as **More to Stand Still**, whole-piece The Wheel, named clear of the clinical term and of Shifting the Burden's Weaner/Weaning (remedy renamed The Abstainer/The Fast), and hedged from a return-to-zero law to the documented partial adaptation

Triage of the rest of the cross-domain batch, against REFERENCES.md. Reaffirmed held, as the slate already records above: The Red Queen (Van Valen), whose obligatory-adaptation-with-no-cumulative-gain reading is The Arms Race's own running to stand still, the coevolutionary treadmill adding no vector the positional race does not carry. Folded, not slated: Lossy Compression, the non-deliberate discard of signal at each summary, is the just-slated Abstraction Ladder read as information rather than accountability, both the undeliberate loss of specificity up the reporting chain and both distinct from Watermelon Reporting's deliberate filter, so it is carried there and not stacked beside it. Held as too adjacent: Semantic Drift, whose words that keep circulating while the practice they named disappears is The Cargo Cult's form without substance at the level of language and leans on the just-slated Leadership Script's naturalised register, its organisational spine thinner than its linguistic one; the Dark Data Problem (Hand, 2020), whose signal that never entered the record is The Survivors (Wald's survivorship bias, the data destroyed before it could be counted) joined to The Streetlight Effect's search where the light is; and the Overton Shift, whose gradual move of the acceptable is The New Normal's normalisation of deviance and Prevalence-Induced Concept Change's concept creep, at the level of legitimacy rather than procedure. Held pending a distinct cut, each with a real spine but a fit still to find: Moral Disengagement (Bandura, 1999), whose diffusion of responsibility is Everybody's Business (Darley & Latané) and whose chain-spread agency is The Decision Travels, the euphemistic-labelling and self-exoneration remainder noted as a possible distinct cut once it clears both; Autoimmunity, the control or compliance function turning on the productive core it guards, with a genuine spine in Power's The Audit Society (1997) but a reading that sits between the just-slated Iatrogenesis and Metric Fixation, held until a cut clears both; Phase Transition, whose threshold read as linear has a real spine in Scheffer and colleagues on critical transitions, to be staged only when cut to the actors reading a non-linear system as incremental, distinct from The New Normal's baseline drift and The Bank Run's self-fulfilling tip; Niche Construction, the environment reshaped so the underlying capability never develops, too adjacent to Shifting the Burden and less tested in organisations; Ecological Succession, the pioneer displaced by the inheritors of the conditions it built, the reverse of The Head Start, held until a cited organisational anchor is named (first-mover disadvantage, Tellis & Golder) rather than the ecology metaphor alone; and the Maxwell's Demon Tax, the coordination overhead exceeding the disorder it prevents, whose physics is anchored by Landauer's principle but whose organisational trap wants a cited org spine and heavier framing before it can be slated. The method itself, the sweep for uncovered fields, is kept: it is what surfaced the three slated above, and the next research pass runs the same way, by field and then by canonical name through REFERENCES.md.

A discourse-and-boundary research batch triaged at the eighty-seven reconcile, each candidate run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name and against the live slate. The batch reads the registry at eighty-four; it now stands at eighty-seven, so the dedup was run against the full set. The concordance caught one at once: the batch's "The False Consensus" is already slated, as The Illusion of Shared Understanding, the same engine and nearly the same description, the name "The False Consensus" being the very one struck from the earlier language batch for colliding with Ross's false-consensus effect, so it is not a new candidate but the P2 already queued. The three below survive and are slated; the triage note that follows records the rest.

- [x] [P2] The Boundary Object Problem (Star & Griesemer, "Institutional Ecology, 'Translations' and Boundary Objects", Social Studies of Science 1989): an artefact passes between communities of practice, each using it correctly by its own lights and meaning something different by it, so coordination appears to hold because the object circulates while the meanings diverge underneath, the gap surfacing only when the groups compare what they built, distinct from The Illusion of Shared Understanding's single message resolved differently in one shared room in that the engine is a persistent artefact spanning a community boundary, interpretively flexible by design, Star's boundary object that works by its plasticity and fails only when it substitutes for the conversation; to be cut to that cross-community-artefact vector and flagged for the Illusion adjacency
- [x] [P2] The Horizon Problem (Ulrich, Critical Systems Heuristics, the boundary critique; Midgley; the planning-horizon literature): every planning instrument has an edge, and what lies past it carries no cost and casts no shadow until it arrives, so the instrument structurally excludes the future it cannot reach and nothing in the process flags the exclusion, distinct from Seed Corn's deliberate present-for-future trade, Time Inconsistency, and The Tyranny of the Urgent in that the future is not ignored or deprioritised but structurally absent from the model, and from The Law of the Instrument's tool-shaped framing and The Streetlight Effect's search-where-easy in that the engine is the instrument's boundary making the beyond costless and unflagged; to be anchored to boundary critique and cut to that edge-of-instrument vector
- [x] [P2] Semantic Drift (Traugott & Dasher, Regularity in Semantic Change, 2002; semantic bleaching; Bloomfield): a word that named a practice keeps circulating after the practice has gone or inverted, its content bleached through collective use, so a field coordinates on emptied vocabulary and speaks the old language while doing the new thing, and no one marks where the two parted; upgraded from held now that the linguistic spine of semantic bleaching anchors it past the thin organisational one noted before, distinct from The Cargo Cult's ritual form performed for a result and Prevalence-Induced Concept Change's prevalence-driven boundary shift in that the engine is use-driven bleaching of a word decoupled from its referent; flagged for those adjacencies, to be cut hard to the bleaching vector and named clear

Triage of the rest of the discourse-and-boundary batch, against REFERENCES.md. Reaffirmed held: Presupposition Creep (van Dijk), whose accumulation of unstated premises is The Leadership Script's naturalisation running on propositions and The New Normal's baseline drift, the least tested of the language batch in organisational research; its sharper sliver, premises that compound because each is accommodated rather than asserted (Stalnaker's common ground; Lewis on accommodation), is noted as the cut that could earn a later slate. Held as too adjacent, one sibling of a slated one: The Translation Loss, a message correct in the system that made it and foreign in the one that receives it, is the Boundary Object's own meaning-fails-across-a-boundary engine plus a brush of Second Nature's frame gap, its nearest anchor Callon's sociology of translation pulling it back into the Boundary Object's home, so it is held until a cut clears both the Boundary Object and the Illusion rather than stacked beside them. A note carried forward from this batch's structural test, that a misfit's pattern must run in the system regardless of which agent is inside it: it bears on two slated P2s below, The Einstellung Effect and Tolerance, both of which read at the persona level as written (a primed solution blocking search in one mind; habituation to a stimulus), so each is flagged to be cut to its structural form at authoring, an organisation's established solution suppressing system-wide search and an incentive system that must inflate its rewards to hold behaviour, or else reconsidered as khai-engine territory rather than staged here.

A four-field research sweep at the ninety-one reconcile, run to refill the slate for the century, the run to one hundred and one. Four fields the house had under-covered were each swept by a scout, and every candidate was run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name and against the structural test, that the pattern must run in the system regardless of which agent is inside it, no persona-level bias. The nine below were the century slate, the strongest, cleanest, and most distinct survivors across law and contracts, ecology and evolutionary biology, control theory and cybernetics, and epidemiology and public health; all nine are now staged and struck, and the Vaccination Free-Rider, first held in reserve, was promoted to the tenth and staged as Borrowed Immunity to tie the bow at one hundred and one. The reserves and the held follow.

- [x] [P1] The Utilization Trap (Kingman, "The Single Server Queue in Heavy Traffic", 1961; Hopp & Spearman, Factory Physics, 1996): every local manager drives their resource toward full utilisation to erase visible idleness, but queue delay grows as the utilisation ratio over one minus itself, so the last increments of efficiency each buy an unbounded rise in wait time and lead-time variability across the whole flow, distinct from The Brittle Optimum's leanness-breeds-fragility in that no accident and no tight coupling is needed, congestion diverging from the arithmetic of variability alone in a perfectly reliable system, and from The Bullwhip Effect's amplification along a chain in that it is a single node's non-linear response to its own load; the costly remedy is planned slack, holding utilisation below saturation
- [x] [P1] Rose's Prevention Paradox (Geoffrey Rose, "Sick Individuals and Sick Populations", 1985; The Strategy of Preventive Medicine, 1992): a preventive measure that brings a large benefit to a population offers almost nothing to each participant, because most of the risk sits in the many at low risk and not the few at high, so each person rationally weighs a negligible personal benefit against a real personal cost and declines, and the collective benefit is lost, distinct from The Commons's depletion of a rivalrous stock in that the good is undersupplied rather than over-extracted, and from The Firefighter's Medal's invisibility of prevention in that the benefit is fully visible and still individually negligible; the costly remedy is to shift the whole population distribution by structural means rather than counsel individuals
- [x] [P1] Evolutionary Suicide (Matsuda & Abrams, 1994; Rankin & López-Sepulcre, "Can adaptation lead to extinction?", 2005): individual selection favours a trait that raises relative fitness while lowering the population's viability, so the system evolves past a viability threshold and crashes, the self-terminating trait installed by selection itself with no defector needed, distinct from The Commons's within-generation reversible depletion in that selection keeps reinstalling the trait so there is no restraint lever to pull, and from The Arms Race's wasteful positional escalation in that it crosses a threshold into collapse rather than merely spending to stand still; the costly remedy is externally imposed restraint or selection above the individual level
- [x] [P1] The Judgment-Proof Problem (Steven Shavell, "The Judgment Proof Problem", International Review of Law and Economics, 1986): liability deters harm only up to the injurer's assets, so an actor whose potential harm exceeds its net worth rationally treats damages above that ceiling as free, under-invests in precaution, and structures itself thin to stay below the ceiling, the largest harms systematically underdeterred, distinct from Moral Hazard's hidden action and insurance-dampened care in that neither is needed, deterrence collapsing under fully observable conduct and no insurer purely because the asset ceiling caps the payout below the harm; the costly remedy is minimum-capital or compulsory-insurance and bonding requirements, imperfect and dear
- [x] [P1] The Allee Effect (W. C. Allee, Animal Aggregations, 1931; Stephens & Sutherland, 1999, the strong Allee effect): below a critical density per-capita growth turns negative, so ordinary thinning that each unit survives tips the whole into an accelerating collapse, a floor where the feedback flips to positive and drives implosion, distinct from Limits to Growth's ceiling in that it is the opposite curvature, a floor and not a cap, and from The Bank Run's belief-driven self-fulfilling panic in that it is a mechanical density threshold that fires with no beliefs at all, the human map the minimum viable crowd of a market, platform, or community; the costly remedy is subsidised re-seeding to force density back above the threshold, no cheap recovery once below
- [x] [P1] The Redundancy Paradox (Scott Sagan, "The Problem of Redundancy Problem", Risk Analysis, 2004; the common-cause failure literature): adding a backup is locally the obvious way to raise reliability, but redundant units share hidden common modes that defeat the independence the calculation assumed, and their presence breeds shirking and adds complexity that spawns new failure modes, so past a point more redundancy lowers reliability, distinct from Social Loafing's motivational effort-diffusion in that the load-bearing move is the engineering common-mode coupling that defeats redundancy even among perfectly diligent units, and from The Brittle Optimum in that it is the reliability engineer's own remedy backfiring; the costly remedy is diversifying against common modes and auditing independence, far dearer than bolting on a copy
- [x] [P2] The Ecological Trap (Schlaepfer, Runge & Sherman, "Ecological and evolutionary traps", Trends in Ecology & Evolution, 2002; the attractive sink): a decision rule keyed to a once-reliable cue persists after environmental change decouples the cue from the outcome, so agents are preferentially drawn to the now-maladaptive option, the trap more attractive than the safe choice, distinct from Metric Fixation's target set and gamed in that there is no target-setting and no gamer, an honestly-correlated cue broken by exogenous change, and from Alarm Fatigue's dulled response to a true signal in that here the system over-responds to a false one and prefers it; the costly remedy is relearning the cue or dismantling the sink that out-competes the safe option for attention
- [x] [P2] Requisite Variety (Ross Ashby, An Introduction to Cybernetics, 1956, only variety can absorb variety; Beer's Viable System Model): a regulator can only absorb the disturbance variety it can match, and under cost and attention pressure each controller rationally attenuates the variety it attends to, standard rules and one-size categories and fewer knobs, every simplification locally efficient, until the controller's variety falls below the regulated system's and control fails precisely at the cases the thinned repertoire never anticipated, distinct from The Abstraction Ladder's map losing the territory's detail in that it is the sharper quantitative claim that the response repertoire is provably too small, regulation degrading by a conservation law and not by misperception; the costly remedy is matching variety, a controller as complex as its environment
- [x] [P2] The Race to the Bottom (William Cary, "Federalism and Corporate Law", Yale Law Journal, 1974, the Delaware effect; Revesz on the environmental version): when the regulated actor is mobile and each jurisdiction captures the gains of attracting it, every jurisdiction rationally shaves its standards to win the base, the dominant strategy laxity for all and the equilibrium a floor no legislature wanted and none can raise alone without exporting the base, distinct from Regulatory Capture's single agency bent by one industry from within in that it is a horizontal competition among sovereigns with no capture, and from The Commons in that there is no shared depleting stock, only strategic complementarity in rule-setting over a mobile base; the costly remedy is a federal or supranational floor, surrendering local autonomy

Triage of the rest of the sweep, against REFERENCES.md and the structural test. Reserves, strong and distinct but held below the century line for the nine above, ready should any be swapped at the Playwright's call: the Vaccination Free-Rider (Bauch & Earn, "Vaccination and the theory of games", 2004), voluntary coverage capped below the herd-immunity threshold because each person's payoff from abstaining rises as others vaccinate, near The Stag Hunt and the free-rider family but distinct in that rising-payoff-to-abstain threshold, since promoted to the tenth and staged as Borrowed Immunity; the Inverse Care Law (Julian Tudor Hart, The Lancet, 1971), market allocation sending care away from need, a cross-axis inversion distinct from The Head Start's temporal compounding on one axis, since promoted from the reserves and staged as **The Driest Wells**; and the Priest-Klein selection hypothesis (Priest & Klein, "The Selection of Disputes for Litigation", 1984), rational settlement filtering the trial pool toward a fifty-percent win rate so inference from decided cases misleads, distinct from The Survivors' passive attrition in that two adversaries jointly select the predictable cases out of the sample, since promoted from the reserves and staged as **Only the Close Ones**. Held pending a distinct cut, each a near-sibling of a staged misfit: Drift into Failure (Rasmussen, "Risk Management in a Dynamic Society", 1997; Dekker), cost and effort gradients migrating the operating point to the safety boundary, held against The New Normal until set-point migration by economic gradient is cut clear of Vaughan's cognitive reclassification of an unchanged act; and Muller's Ratchet (Muller, 1964; Felsenstein, 1974), the irreversible one-way loss of the fittest class without recombination, held against Model Collapse until the irreversible-click-and-recombination-remedy vector is cut clear of the recursive-training tail-erosion already staged. Flagged for the cut at authoring: the Standard-Form Term Degradation of adhesion contracts (Schwartz & Wilde, 1979) is to be staged as the rational-search-cost clearing-failure and never the behavioural-inattention version, to keep it structural and clear of The Market for Lemons's exit spiral; and the Overdiagnosis Popularity Paradox (Raffle & Gray; Welch) is to be slated only if cut hard to the popularity loop, the harmed becoming the advocates who inflate demand, or it stays a composite of First Do Harm, The Survivors, and Metric Fixation and is not staged. Struck before slating, the scouts' dedup catching each as already staged: Bode's sensitivity integral, the waterbed effect, is The Balloon Effect's squeeze-here-bulge-there; delay-induced oscillation and hunting is The Bullwhip Effect; risk homeostasis and the Peltzman effect is Moral Hazard; antimicrobial resistance as a commons is The Commons; monoculture fragility is The Brittle Optimum; the local-optimum fitness valley is The Success Trap with Path Dependence; patent thickets and royalty stacking are The Anticommons; defensive documentation is The Paper Trail; the base-rate neglect of rare-disease screening is inside The Availability Cascade; and the Concorde fallacy is Good Money After Bad. The Red Queen and Ecological Succession stay held as before. The century slate above was the run to one hundred and one: the nine are authored and struck, the Vaccination Free-Rider promoted to the tenth and staged as Borrowed Immunity, and the bow is tied at one hundred and one. Past them the remaining reserves and the earlier P2s (The Fiscal Ratchet, and Tolerance and The Einstellung Effect on their structural cuts) stand ready for the next run.

The bow at one hundred and one is tied; the slate is refilled below, opened by the candidate
briefing of 2026-07-13.

- [x] [P1] Impression Management (Goffman, The Presentation of Self in Everyday Life, 1959; Interaction Ritual, 1967; Hochschild, The Managed Heart, 1983; Leary & Kowalski, Psychological Bulletin, 1990): in any group where legibility is rewarded, each member rationally presents a processed, coherent version of themselves, processed signal reading as competent and raw signal as noise, so the group shares only what is already resolved and the raw signal it most needs never enters the room, performance the baseline structure of all interaction and not a response to threat, no villain, just structure; distinct from Saving Face's defensive routines, which fire only when embarrassment threatens (the front is up before any threat appears), from Watermelon Reporting's upward suppression of bad news (the front suppresses in all directions, peer, downward, and toward the self, the vector no staged misfit holds), from The Leadership Script's single performed register (the universal mechanism every register runs on), and from The Trip to Abilene and the held-off preference falsification (Kuran), which stage a false consensus about a decision or a misreported preference (here no decision is on the table and no preference is misreported, this is the standing structure those collisions run on); the costly remedy is backstage access bought at real exposure, dropping the front is not a free act of courage, and Hochschild's managed heart is the sustained cost of holding it; working house title The Front, already staged as the misfit **The Front** and retired here as covered, not authored again

A warrant mine at the one-hundred-and-three reconcile, the largest research pass yet and directed by
[Mine the Warrants for Gaps](orders/order_mine_the_warrants_for_gaps.md): fourteen scouts read all one
hundred and three staged warrants for the neighbours each names in its Line of Work and Origin table but
does not stage, one hundred and fifty-one candidates surfaced and deduped against the staged set. The
batch reads the registry at one hundred and three. A companion pass fact-checked all six hundred and one
Origin-table citations and found the collection sound bar two coinage misattributions, corrected under
separate cover. Struck before slating, the mine's synthesis proposing each while a staged misfit already
carries it by canonical name, the concordance catching all: risk compensation (The Spent Margin, now
staged and struck from the held list above), the race to the bottom (The Lowest Bar), Brooks's law (The
Ninth Woman), institutional isomorphism (The Done Thing), Rose's prevention paradox (Not Worth My While),
pluralistic ignorance (The Trip to Abilene), the tragedy of the horizon (The Horizon Problem), the Icarus
paradox (The Success Trap), and the family scapegoat and triangulation (The Identified Patient).
Reaffirmed held, the mine re-surfacing candidates the slate already triaged and the reasons standing:
preference falsification, the security dilemma, the iron law of oligarchy, normalization of deviance and
the shifting baseline, the Red Queen, and Muller's ratchet. The survivors below are slated, tiered P1 and
P2, and worked from the top.

- [x] [P1] The Keynesian Beauty Contest (Keynes, The General Theory, 1936, ch. 12): when a payoff depends on matching the crowd rather than on the thing itself, each agent stops valuing fundamentals and forecasts the average forecast of the average forecast, so price and choice track opinion about opinion and detach from value, a self-referential regress that breeds bubbles and fads with no manipulator, distinct from Information Cascade's sequential inference from predecessors' actions read as signals and The Stag Hunt's two-equilibrium coordination in that it is simultaneous higher-order belief with no signal sequence and no fixed equilibrium, only the regress; the costly discipline is anchoring on value while the mispricing runs against you; staged as **The Beauty Contest**
- [x] [P1] Statistical Discrimination (Arrow, "The Theory of Discrimination", 1973; Phelps, 1972): when individual quality is unobservable each agent rationally prices a person by their group's average as a proxy, and the disadvantaged group's rational under-investment in a signal the market will not reward makes the proxy come true, a self-confirming prior authored by no bigot, distinct from The Market for Lemons's pooling of unlike units of one good that drives the good out of trade in that it pools unlike individuals by group and is self-fulfilling, the assumed average becoming the produced average, and from The Head Start's compounding lead in that the trap is a belief that manufactures its own confirmation; the costly remedy is external, subsidised signalling that the same rational updating undercuts; staged as **The Proxy**
- [x] [P1] Publication Bias (Sterling, 1959; Rosenthal, "The File Drawer Problem", 1979): significant positive results are written up, accepted, and cited while nulls and failures stay in the drawer, so the published record and every meta-analysis built on it overstate effects and manufacture a consensus the full evidence would not support, each author, reviewer, and editor rationally preferring the novel and significant, distinct from Metric Fixation's gamed target and The Streetlight Effect's search where it is easy in that no target is set and no one searches the lit patch, the filter falling on outcome-significance after the fact and the distortion living in the aggregated literature; the costly remedy is pre-registration and publishing nulls, slow and against every party's reward; staged as **The File Drawer**
- [x] [P1] Legibility and the Loss of Métis (James C. Scott, Seeing Like a State, 1998): a central authority makes a domain governable by imposing a standard, legible grid on it, and the grid erases the local, tacit, practical knowledge, the métis, that actually made the system work, so the simplification that lets the centre see and manage the domain is the same act that hollows out its function, distinct from The Abstraction Ladder's undeliberate shedding of specifics up a reporting chain and Requisite Variety's provably-too-small repertoire in that the engine is the active destruction of situated knowledge by the drive for legibility itself, the map remaking the territory it could not otherwise read; the costly remedy is holding legibility below the point where it displaces the métis it cannot capture; staged as **The Straight Rows**
- [x] [P2] Berkson's Paradox (Berkson, 1946; the collider-bias literature): conditioning on a common effect of two independent causes opens a spurious, often negative association between them, so selecting a sample by an outcome both causes influence manufactures a correlation that does not exist in the population, distinct from The Survivors' survivorship attrition and The Bigger Picture's Simpson reversal in that the artefact is a collider opened by selection on a shared effect, neither units lost from a sample nor strata pooled over a confound; the costly remedy is reasoning about the selection that built the sample, not the sample alone; staged as **The False Trade-Off**
- [x] [P2] The Dollar Auction (Shubik, "The Dollar Auction Game", 1971): in a contest where the loser as well as the winner pays their last bid, each further bid is rational to escape the larger loss of quitting, so two parties escalate past the value of the prize and on toward ruin, each step sound and the whole a trap, distinct from Good Money After Bad's sunk-cost persistence by one actor and The Winning Bid's winner's curse from misestimation in that the engine is the all-pay structure, the loser's forfeit, that makes escalation rational with no sunk cost and no misjudged value; the costly remedy is a precommitment not to enter, the entry being the only safe exit; staged as **The Dollar Auction**
- [x] [P2] Bootleggers and Baptists (Yandle, "Bootleggers and Baptists", 1983): a rule endures when a coalition that wants it on principle and a coalition that profits from it privately back the same regulation for opposite reasons, so a policy no honest cost-benefit would keep becomes politically immovable, the moralist supplying the public case and the profiteer the quiet muscle, distinct from The Iron Triangle's closed triad of mutual exchange and Regulatory Capture's single agency bent from within in that the engine is a tacit two-sided alliance of principle and profit around one rule, neither party needing to coordinate with or even acknowledge the other; the costly remedy is naming the profit behind the principle, against both coalitions at once; staged as **Bootleggers and Baptists** (kept as its own title)
- [x] [P2] Diminishing Returns on Complexity (Tainter, The Collapse of Complex Societies, 1988): a society meets each problem by adding complexity, which solves it at rising cost and falling marginal return, until the complexity that sustains the society costs more than it yields and can no longer be afforded, and the system simplifies by collapse, distinct from Limits to Growth's binding stock limit and The Brittle Optimum's optimised-away slack in that the engine is the declining marginal return on problem-solving complexity itself as a standing strategy, each layer rational when added and the accumulation unpayable; the costly remedy is deliberate simplification before the return goes negative, forgoing capacities the complexity bought; staged as **The Last Layer**
- [x] [P2] The Samaritan's Dilemma (Buchanan, "The Samaritan's Dilemma", 1975): a benefactor who cannot credibly commit to withholding aid induces the recipient to under-provide for themselves, so guaranteed rescue manufactures the dependence it means to relieve, a time-inconsistent compassion, distinct from Moral Hazard's hidden action under a shifted cost and Time Inconsistency's monetary commitment in that the engine is the helper's own inability to commit to not helping and the recipient's rational under-precaution in response, the aid fully observed and no cost transferred to a third party; the costly remedy is a credible toughness that must sometimes mean not helping the very person it would help; staged as **Someone Will Catch Me**, whole-piece The Soft Heart, cut to the helper's commitment failure, named clear of Moral Hazard, Time Inconsistency (The Vow/Vowing not The Binder), and Shifting the Burden (The Unbuilt not The Weaner), with the Time-Inconsistency distinction cut to the collapse-versus-recurring-dependence axis
- [x] [P2] Schelling's Tipping (Schelling, "Dynamic Models of Segregation", 1971; Micromotives and Macrobehavior, 1978): mild individual preferences over the mix of one's neighbours aggregate, through a tipping threshold, into stark segregation no one intended or preferred, so a weak taste held by each sorts the whole past a point of no return, distinct from The Tyranny of Small Decisions's self-regarding choices summing to an aggregate no one set and Information Cascade's herding on observed action in that the engine is the aggregation of weak same-kind preferences across a tipping threshold into a sorted macro-state, the outcome discontinuous in a smooth change of taste; the costly remedy is holding the mix off the threshold, against each agent's mild and lawful preference; staged as **The Sort**
- [x] [P2] The Optimizer's Curse (Smith & Winkler, "The Optimizer's Curse", 2006): choosing the option with the highest estimated value selects for the option with the largest upward estimation error, so the realised value of the chosen option disappoints even when every estimate is individually unbiased, the disappointment a property of selecting the maximum of noisy estimates, distinct from The Winning Bid's winner's curse in a competitive auction and Overfitting's fit to a sample's noise in that the trap is a single decider selecting across alternatives on noisy but unbiased estimates, no rival bidder and no fitted model; the costly remedy is shrinking estimates toward the prior before choosing, forgoing the boldest apparent bet; staged as **The Best on Paper**
- [x] [P2] The Tyranny of Structurelessness (Jo Freeman, "The Tyranny of Structurelessness", 1972): a group that refuses formal structure does not abolish power but drives it into an informal, self-selected, unaccountable elite, harder to check than a named hierarchy because it can deny it holds any power at all, distinct from Vetocracy's surplus of formal veto points and Pournelle's Iron Law's capture by the organisation-devoted in that the engine is the refusal of structure itself concentrating power off the record, the informal elite forming with no design and answerable to no one; the costly remedy is formalising the structure and losing the openness the structurelessness was adopted to protect; staged as **No One in Charge**
- [x] [P2] The Cobweb Cycle (Ezekiel, "The Cobweb Theorem", 1938; Kaldor): when production lags price and producers plan on today's price, supply overshoots when price is high and undershoots when it is low, so a single market oscillates boom to bust in a self-perpetuating cycle, each producer's plan sound on the price they see, distinct from The Bullwhip Effect's amplification of a demand wobble along a multi-link supply chain in that the engine is one market's price-expectation with a production lag, the oscillation endogenous to the lag and not amplified along a chain; the costly remedy is planning on expected rather than current price, forgoing the signal in front of each producer; staged as **Chasing the Price**

The warrant-mine batch is worked from the top: its twelve targets are now all staged and struck, The
Keynesian Beauty Contest as **The Beauty Contest**, Statistical Discrimination as **The Proxy**,
Publication Bias as **The File Drawer**, Legibility and the Loss of Métis as **The Straight Rows**,
Berkson's Paradox as **The False Trade-Off**, The Dollar Auction and Bootleggers and Baptists (both kept
as their own titles), Diminishing Returns on Complexity as **The Last Layer**, Schelling's Tipping as
**The Sort**, The Optimizer's Curse as **The Best on Paper**, The Tyranny of Structurelessness as
**No One in Charge**, and The Cobweb Cycle as **Chasing the Price**, taking the house to one hundred and
fifteen misfits. That block exhausted, the next-highest target, Impression Management (P1), is found already
staged as **The Front** and is retired as covered, so the slate took the highest genuinely unstruck target
remaining, The Einstellung Effect (Luchins, P2), now staged as **The Good Move**, taking the house to one hundred
and sixteen. The Fiscal Ratchet (Peacock & Wiseman, P2) is then staged as **The High-Water Mark**, cut to the
commitment-floor displacement, named clear of Sandbag's output-quota ratchet, and hedged from an iron law to the
documented tendency, taking the house to one hundred and seventeen. Tolerance (Solomon & Corbit; Brickman & Campbell,
P2) is then staged as **More to Stand Still**, cut to the escalation-to-stand-still vector, named clear of the clinical
term and of Shifting the Burden's Weaner/Weaning, and hedged to the documented partial adaptation, taking the house to
one hundred and eighteen. The Samaritan's Dilemma (Buchanan, P2) is then staged as **Someone Will Catch Me**, cut to the
helper's commitment failure, named clear of Moral Hazard, Time Inconsistency, and Shifting the Burden, taking the house
to one hundred and nineteen. With that the explicit ranked list is exhausted: every P0, P1, and P2 target is struck, and
the season now runs on reserve promotions. The first is the Inverse Care Law (Julian Tudor Hart, The Lancet, 1971),
promoted and staged as **The Driest Wells**, cut to the supply-follows-reward-not-need structure and named clear of The
Head Start, taking the house to one hundred and twenty. The Priest-Klein selection hypothesis (Priest & Klein, 1984) is
then promoted and staged as **Only the Close Ones**, cut to the settlement-selects-the-sample engine, named clear of The
Survivors' passive attrition, and hedged to the fifty-percent tendency, taking the house to one hundred and twenty-one.
Both ready reserves now spent, the first of the flagged-for-cut reserves, the Standard-Form Term Degradation of
adhesion contracts (Schwartz & Wilde, 1979), is promoted and staged as **Nobody Reads It**, cut hard to the
rational-search-cost clearing-failure and never the behavioural-inattention version, named clear of The Market for
Lemons's exit spiral (an informed-minority failure on a non-salient term, not an adverse selection on a hidden quality,
the market intact and clearing while its print slides) and of The Lowest Bar, and hedged to the informed-minority
tendency under stated conditions rather than an iron law, taking the house to one hundred and twenty-two. With that the
flagged reserve queue is exhausted: the Overdiagnosis Popularity Paradox (Raffle & Gray, Screening: Evidence and
Practice; Welch, Overdiagnosed) is promoted and staged as **It Saved My Life**, cut to the popularity loop where the
overdiagnosed become the grateful advocates whose demand inflates the very screening that overdiagnosed them, so a
programme grows more beloved the more it overdiagnoses and its support tracks the harm and not the benefit, named clear
of First Do Harm's iatrogenic harm, The Survivors' passive-attrition inference read from a filtered sample (here the
survivors act, becoming the constituency that grows the programme, a feedback loop and not a misread residue), and Metric
Fixation's gamed survival statistic (here the survival rate is honestly computed and honestly misleading), and hedged to
the documented tendency where overdiagnosis is substantial, taking the house to one hundred and twenty-three. Both
flagged-for-cut reserves now spent, the season turns to the held-pending pair, and the first, Drift into Failure
(Rasmussen, "Risk Management in a Dynamic Society", 1997; Dekker, Drift into Failure, 2011), is promoted as the next
birth, to be cut to the gradient-driven migration of the operating point: cost and effort pressures move a system's
working point toward the unmarked boundary of safe operation, each local step to work a little cheaper or a little easier
rational, until the point crosses an edge no one could see and an accident reveals where it was. It is to be named clear
of The New Normal's cognitive reclassification of an unchanged act (the point migrates under pressure, no perception of
the act need shift), The Brittle Optimum's statically optimised-away slack (a dynamic migration of the working point, not
a one-time trimming), and The Spent Margin's risk compensation for a realised safety gain, cut to Rasmussen's migration
model and hedged to the documented dynamic. Behind it, Drift into Failure (Rasmussen, 1997; Dekker, 2011) is promoted and staged as **The Unmarked Edge**, cut to the
gradient-driven migration of the operating point toward the unmarked boundary of safe operation, named clear of The New
Normal's cognitive reclassification (the point migrates under pressure, no perception need shift), The Brittle Optimum's
static optimised-away slack, and The Spent Margin's risk compensation, its cast vocabulary chosen deliberately off The
New Normal's own safety-org roles, and hedged to a tendency under pressure rather than a fate every system crosses,
taking the house to one hundred and twenty-four. With the held-pending pair down to one, Muller's Ratchet (Muller, "The
Relation of Recombination to Mutational Advance", 1964; Felsenstein, "The Evolutionary Advantage of Recombination", 1974)
is promoted and staged as **No Way Back Up**, cut to the irreversible-click-and-recombination-remedy vector: in a finite
population that copies without mixing, the least-loaded class, the genotype carrying the fewest deleterious mutations, is
lost by chance and cannot be reconstituted without recombination, so the minimum mutation load ratchets up one
irreversible click at a time, no selection for the mutations and no agent choosing the decline. It is named clear
of Model Collapse's recursive-resampling erosion of the distribution tails (there the tails thin as a model retrains on
its own output; here a discrete fittest class is lost by drift and the specific remedy is recombination), and clear of
the one-way ratchet Nobody Reads It already stages (a market-discipline failure, not a chance loss of a fittest class),
cut to the drift-loss-and-recombination engine, kept from titling any element a ratchet, and hedged to the documented
population-genetic tendency, taking the house to one hundred and twenty-five. With Muller's Ratchet the last held-pending
reserve is spent and the ranked slate exhausted, so a fresh research sweep was run to refill it.

A five-field research sweep at the one-hundred-and-twenty-five reconcile, run to refill the exhausted slate. Five scouts
swept physics and materials science, political science and social choice, cognition and decision, biology and medicine,
and information and control systems, each reading REFERENCES.md and the live registry.json and deduping against the
staged set by canonical name and against the structural test, that the pattern must run in the system regardless of which
agent is inside it. The sweep corrected one standing error and cleared several near-misses. The correction: The Balloon
Effect stages Reppetto's crime displacement, a homonym, not the control-theoretic waterbed effect, so Bode's sensitivity
integral is open ground after all. Struck before slating, the scouts' dedup catching each as already carried by name or
as an existing cited source: the flypaper effect, Olson's distributional coalitions, and fiscal illusion (all cited
inside The High-Water Mark's warrant); the hidden-profile common-knowledge effect (cited inside The Colour of the Bike
Shed); Bak's self-organised criticality and Perrow's tight coupling (inside The Brittle Optimum); Kingman's queueing
utilisation (No Idle Hands); Ashby's requisite variety (The Simpler Lever); Sagan's redundancy paradox (Belt and Braces);
and antimicrobial resistance as a commons (The Commons). Rejected as below the house's evidential standard: Dunning-Kruger,
ego depletion, and decision fatigue (each resting on contested or failed-replication findings) and choice overload (a
near-zero meta-analytic effect). A maintenance note carried forward: REFERENCES.md is stale, last built 2026-07-04 and
missing the two dozen misfits staged since, and wants a rebuild so the concordance again covers the whole run. The
survivors below are slated, tiered P1 and P2, and worked from the top.

- [x] [P1] Immunopathology, the defence does the damage (Zinkernagel & Doherty, _Nature_, 1974, Nobel; Tisoncik et al., 2012): the immune response escalates in blind proportion to pathogen load, and the positive-feedback sum of locally-appropriate responses destroys the host's own tissue past what the pathogen would have done, the defence itself the disease, distinct from First Do Harm's iatrogenesis in that no external remedy and no new site is involved, the same defence overshooting the same threat in the same tissue, and from Alarm Fatigue's under-reaction in that it is the mirror over-reaction; to be anchored to the Zinkernagel-Doherty T-cell result and hedged past the looser modern cytokine-storm usage; staged as **In Its Own Defence**, cut to the defence that wrecks the host with the same weapons it clears the invader and amplifies past the threat with no fast brake, named clear of First Do Harm (external remedy, new site) and Alarm Fatigue (the mirror under-reaction), the proportional-scaling premise homed to Medzhitov's resistance and the Zinkernagel-Doherty result kept for the overkill, the amplification hedged to no fast brake rather than no brake and the weapons' harm cast as indiscriminacy not misidentification, held to a tendency under stated conditions
