- [x] [P1] The Evolution of Virulence (Anderson & May, _Parasitology_, 1982; Ewald, 1983; Frank, _QRB_, 1996): within a host, a pathogen lineage that replicates faster transmits better and outcompetes gentler co-infecting lineages, and because replication and host damage are mechanically coupled, selection drags virulence to whatever the transmission trade-off allows regardless of the host's or the parasite's own long-run good, distinct from Fit to Fail's crossing of a viability threshold into collapse in that an equilibrium virulence simply settles worse than an achievable alternative with no collapse needed, and from The Commons in that the engine is a trade-off curve fixed by biophysics, not withdrawal from a depletable stock; hedged to a tendency under the trade-off with real mixed-transmission exceptions; staged as **Sick Enough to Spread**, cut to the harm-is-the-price-of-spread structure where selection settles virulence at the intermediate level that transmits best, a durable equilibrium sicker than the host would choose, named clear of Fit to Fail (equilibrium virulence settling worse, no collapse), The Commons (within-host competition kept a secondary amplifier, no depletable stock), The False Trade-Off, The Peace It Keeps (external parasite, not a defecting part), and In Its Own Defence (parasite's offence, not host's over-reacting defence), held to a tendency under the trade-off with Ewald's transmission ecology as the oblique remedy
- [x] [P1] Antagonistic Pleiotropy, the evolution of senescence (Williams, _Evolution_, 1957; Hamilton, _JTB_, 1966): a gene that raises fitness early, when most of the cohort is alive and selection is strong, is favoured even at the cost of decline late, when extrinsic mortality has thinned the cohort and selection is weak, so ageing is the summed cost of many locally-adaptive early trade-offs and selection is blind to a price it never has to pay, distinct from Fit to Fail's extinction risk and Seed Corn's economic present-for-future in that the engine is the age-structured decline in the force of selection; Kirkwood's disposable-soma resource variant noted as a distinct later cut, not blended in
- [x] [P1] The Condorcet Paradox and Arrow's Impossibility (Condorcet, 1785; Arrow, _JPE_, 1950, Nobel; McKelvey, _JET_, 1976): every voter's own ranking is transitive and sincere, yet the majority relation over three or more options can cycle with no stable winner, so whoever sequences the votes, not any biased judge, decides what the group prefers, distinct from Vetocracy's surplus of veto points and The Anticommons's fragmented ownership in that nothing blocks action, it proceeds but is incoherent and agenda-exploitable; hedged past single-peaked preferences (Black), a tendency in multidimensional choice and not a universal; staged as **Whoever Asks Last**, taken out of order to break the biomedical run, cut to the incoherent-whole vector that the group reaches a decision but no coherent preference stands behind it, the whole-piece The Made Majority and the release valve Black's single-peaked median folded into the multidimensional place The Grid, named clear of Vetocracy (blocked-but-coherent), The Trip to Abilene (a group that cannot see what it wants, not one with no what-it-wants to see), The Tyranny of Small Decisions, and The Sort, and with the agenda-setter renamed The Presider off that neighbour's Convener, kept sincere throughout and clear of Gibbard-Satterthwaite
- [x] [P1] Fatigue Failure and Cumulative Damage (Wöhler, S-N data, 1860s; Miner, _J. Applied Mechanics_, 1945; Paris & Erdogan, 1963): every load cycle stays safely below yield, so the part passes every single-instant strength check, yet each cycle leaves an invisible increment of micro-damage that sums until a crack nucleates and runs to sudden fracture at a load carried a thousand times before, distinct from The Brittle Optimum's removed slack meeting a shock in that no slack was ever cut and no shock is needed, the part failing under the exact load it was built for just enough times; hedged to a strong tendency with real Weibull scatter, not a fixed threshold; staged as **The Load It Always Carried**, cut to the invisible-accumulation vector no staged misfit holds, a load safe on every single-cycle check destroying the part by the sum of its repetitions, named clear of The Brittle Optimum (no slack removed and no shock, repetition alone), The New Normal (physical damage, no judgment reclassified), Seed Corn (no agent deliberates the trade), and the each-choice-sound-sum-fatal decision family (no choice inside the mechanism, only the passed check blind to the tally), with Wöhler's below-yield fatigue and endurance limit, Palmgren and Miner's linear cumulative damage, and Paris and Erdogan's crack-growth law credited and carried
- [x] [P1] Bode's Sensitivity Integral, the control-theoretic waterbed (Bode, 1945; Åström & Murray, _Feedback Systems_, 2008): for any stable feedback loop the integral of log-sensitivity over frequency is conserved, so tuning a controller to suppress error in its working band necessarily amplifies sensitivity to disturbance somewhere else in the spectrum, the bulge the price of the suppression and not a mistake, distinct from The Balloon Effect's crime displacement (a homonym, no shared spine) and The Simpler Lever's requisite-variety state matching in that this is a frequency-domain conservation law; to be titled clear of "waterbed" and "balloon" so it does not read at a glance as the staged Balloon Effect; staged as **The Calm You Pay For**, cut to the conservation vector no staged misfit holds, a loop that pushes its sensitivity below one where the disturbances live and, by the conserved log-sensitivity integral, is forced above one elsewhere, the calm bought in band paid for as an amplifying rise out of band and dearer still for an unstable plant, named clear of The Balloon Effect (a behavioural displacement, not a conservation law), The False Trade-Off (a real conserved trade-off, not a selection artefact), The Simpler Lever (a conservation constraint no variety removes, not a repertoire shortfall), and The Spent Margin (a rise by theorem, not a margin spent back), with Bode's sensitivity integral and Freudenberg and Looze's right-half-plane and bandwidth constraints credited and carried
- [x] [P1] Administrative Burden, sludge (Herd & Moynihan, _Administrative Burden_, 2018; Sunstein, _Sludge_, 2021): each verification step, form, and waiting period is added for a defensible reason, fraud prevention or eligibility accuracy, but the accreted learning, compliance, and psychological costs deter the very people a programme was meant to serve, so take-up falls below eligibility with no one intending to exclude anyone, distinct from The Paper Trail's provider-side liability documentation in that this is claimant-side access friction with the opposite failure, the qualified going unserved; to be cut to the earnest accretion of well-meant steps, never the weaponised-sludge villain version the literature also records; staged as **Too Much to Ask**, cut to the earnest claimant-side friction that deters the eligible and falls hardest on the neediest, named clear of The Paper Trail (provider-side defensive documentation, the opposite failure), Nobody Reads It (an unread notice, not an access friction), and The Tyranny of Small Decisions (a selection on the neediest, not an unwilled aggregate), the three costs homed to Moynihan, Herd, and Harbage, the take-up gap to Herd and Moynihan, and the regressive incidence to Sunstein, the deliberate-sludge barrier left aside

- [x] [P1] The Homevoter Hypothesis (Fischel, _The Homevoter Hypothesis_, 2001): a homeowner's largest asset is the house, so each rationally votes at the local level to restrict nearby development that could depress its value, individually prudent portfolio defence summing across a metro into a regional housing shortage no one voted for as a goal, distinct from The Sort's mild-preference tipping in that the actor is the incumbent owner and the lever the zoning ballot, and from The Lowest Bar's mobile base playing jurisdictions off each other in that the base is stationary and the newcomer is the one excluded; to be cast as sincere equity protection, not NIMBY malice; staged as **The Only Basket**, cut to the portfolio-defence structure where an owner votes locally to insure an undiversifiable home and the sum of sound defences chokes regional supply, the cost falling on newcomers with no vote, named clear of The Sort (stationary incumbent and the zoning ballot, not a mobile chooser), The Lowest Bar (the base stationary and the newcomer excluded, not a mobile base courted), and The Tyranny of Small Decisions (the asset-insurance motive and the disenfranchisement of the cost-bearers, not a merely unmanaged sum), with the homevoter hypothesis and zoning-as-insurance homed to Fischel and the supply-and-price effect to Glaeser and Gyourko, the newcomer cast as a persona and the disenfranchisement carried by the piece The Boundary
- [x] [P1] Metastable Failure and the Retry Storm (Bronson et al., "Metastable Failures in the Wild", 2021; Nygard, _Release It!_, 2007; congestion collapse, Nagle, RFC 896, 1984; Jacobson, 1988): a transient blip fails some requests, well-behaved clients retry exactly as they should, and the retry load itself now exceeds capacity, so the system stays overloaded and failing long after the trigger is gone, a self-sustaining bad equilibrium held up by nothing but everyone's correct retry logic, distinct from The Bullwhip Effect's multi-stage signal amplification in that no upstream chain is needed and from No Idle Hands's single-node utilisation in that the load is retry-fed bistability; the costly remedy circuit breakers and backoff-with-jitter, the older spine (Nagle, Jacobson) steadying the recent primary source; staged as **Held Under**, cut to the self-sustaining-overload vector no staged misfit holds, a transient trigger tipping a sound system into a failing state that its clients' own correct retries then hold under, the failure outliving its cause and clearing only when the loop is broken by force at the cost of the real work shed, named clear of The Bullwhip Effect (a multi-stage chain amplifying a demand signal, not a single loop feeding on its own output), No Idle Hands (a steady-state congestion curve with no memory, not a stuck bad equilibrium that persists after its trigger is gone), and The Bank Run (a bistable collapse run on belief, each depositor fearing what others might do, not on pure retry mechanism with no fear anywhere in the loop), with the metastable-failure framework homed to Bronson, Charapko, Aghayev, and Zhu, the field study and amplification to Huang, Magnusson, and colleagues, congestion collapse and its cure to Nagle and Jacobson, and the retry storm and circuit breaker to Nygard
- [x] [P2] Thermal Runaway (Semenov, 1928; Frank-Kamenetskii, _Diffusion and Heat Exchange in Chemical Kinetics_, 1939): heat loss rises linearly with temperature and heat generation exponentially, so the operating point is provably stable up to a critical threshold, past which ordinary ageing or a normal load spike flips the sign of the feedback and heating runs away, a hard bifurcation at a fixed critical point, distinct from The Unmarked Edge's gradual gradient-driven migration in that this is an instantaneous physical bifurcation independent of any cost or effort gradient; scope hedged to chemical, electrochemical, and electrical systems rather than many independent agents; staged as **Faster Than It Can Cool**, cut to the sign-flipping-feedback vector no staged misfit holds, an exponential heat source against a linear sink whose genuinely stable operating point sits a determinate distance from a hard critical threshold that ordinary ageing alone brings down to meet it, past which the stabilising feedback reverses sign and the system runs away with no shock and no fault, named clear of The Unmarked Edge (a soft gradient migration pulled by an incentive slope, not a hard physical bifurcation), The Load It Always Carried (a hidden tally of accumulating damage, not a feedback sign-flip with no wear), and Held Under (capture by a second stable state sustained by a loop, not a loss of all equilibrium), with the exponential-source-against-linear-sink tangency homed to Semenov, the distributed critical parameter to Frank-Kamenetskii, and the lithium-ion characteristic temperatures and margin ageing to Feng, Ouyang, and colleagues
- [x] [P2] Fiber-Bundle Load-Redistribution Cascade (Daniels, _Proc. Royal Society A_, 1945; Pradhan, Hansen & Chakrabarti, _Rev. Mod. Physics_, 2010): when one member of a parallel load-bearing set fails, its load redistributes to the survivors under a rule that looks safe member by member, yet because member strengths are distributed the transfer pushes the next-weakest over its own limit and past a critical fraction the whole bundle avalanches, distinct from The Brittle Optimum's removed slack and Belt and Braces's added redundant components and common cause in that the members are already parallel and independent and the cascade is pure sequential load transfer driven by strength variance; scoped to the local-load-sharing rule where the abruptness holds; staged as **Onto the Survivors**, cut to the redistribution-cascade vector no staged misfit holds, a redundant bundle of sound parallel members whose sharing of a failed member's load onto the survivors raises every share and, past a critical fraction, carries the failure through the whole set to complete rupture, named clear of The Brittle Optimum (removed slack meeting a shock, not an intact redundancy cascading), Belt and Braces (a common cause taking the redundancy at once, not independent members failing one at a time), and The Load It Always Carried (a hidden cumulative tally in one part, not near-instant load redistribution across many sound members), with the probabilistic bundle, equal load sharing, and the critical load homed to Daniels, and the redistribution avalanche and the local-load-sharing running front to Pradhan, Hansen, and Chakrabarti
- [x] [P2] Shrouded-Attribute Pricing under Present Bias (Gabaix & Laibson, _QJE_, 2006; DellaVigna & Malmendier, _AER_, 2006): firms that price contracts around consumers' documented present bias out-compete transparent rivals, because a firm that unilaterally unshrouds loses the myopic segment to one that re-shrouds, so a stable competitive equilibrium extracts a myopia tax with no swindler and no fool, distinct from Time Inconsistency's intrapersonal promise broken in that here two agent classes settle an undefected equilibrium with nothing promised, and flagged for a careful cut against Nobody Reads It's informed-minority term-drift so the present-bias vector is kept clear of the search-cost one; staged as **No One Gains by Telling You**, cut to the stable-exploitation-equilibrium vector no staged misfit holds, competing firms posting a low salient base and a shrouded high add-on to present-biased buyers, and an unshrouding that fails to pay because a debiased buyer with a close substitute avoids the add-on rather than rewarding the honest firm, so shrouding stands as a stable equilibrium and the myopia tax flows from the myopic to the sophisticated with no swindler and no fool, named clear of Time Inconsistency (an intrapersonal broken plan within one self, not a two-sided market equilibrium) and Nobody Reads It (an informed-minority search-cost drift that more reading would discipline, not a curse of debiasing in which more information cannot, the cost knowable and the bias in the weighing not the reading), with the shrouded-attribute equilibrium and the curse of debiasing homed to Gabaix and Laibson and the field evidence of present-biased contract choice to DellaVigna and Malmendier
- [x] [P2] Rational Ignorance of the Voter (Downs, _An Economic Theory of Democracy_, 1957; Somin, 2013): since one vote almost never decides an election, the expected return to becoming informed is near zero, so each voter rationally invests nothing in political knowledge and the electorate decides on cues rather than information it could hold, distinct from Nobody Reads It's contract-term search cost in that no market or drafter is involved and the object is the electorate's basis for democratic choice, flagged for the family resemblance so the near-zero-pivotality vector is cut clear; hedged past expressive-voting theory and the turnout paradox; staged as **Cheaper Not to Know**, cut to the negligible-return vector no staged misfit holds, a single ballot with a near-zero chance of deciding, so the expected return to becoming informed is near zero against a real cost, and the rational citizen stays ignorant and votes on cues while the electorate governs itself half-blind at no voter's fault, named clear of Nobody Reads It (a contract-term search cost in a market with a drafter that more reading would discipline, not a near-zero pivotality with no market and no discipline) and Everybody's Business (a volunteer's dilemma where one actor suffices and the failure is diffusion, not a near-zero return where informing oneself secures nothing), scoped to the not-acquiring decision and hedged clear of the turnout paradox, expressive voting, and rational irrationality, with the near-zero pivotality and the rational-ignorance calculus and cue-voting homed to Downs and the measured depth of political ignorance and its cost to democratic competence to Somin
- [x] [P2] The Illusion of Explanatory Depth (Rozenblit & Keil, _Cognitive Science_, 2002; Fernbach et al., _Psychological Science_, 2013): people hold confident, extreme policy positions on a felt sense of mechanistic understanding that unravels only when they are asked to trace the mechanism, not merely to give reasons, so a polity's discourse runs on unexamined conviction and polarises with no one lying, distinct from Second Nature's curse of knowledge in that this is a solo overestimate of one's own causal grasp and not a dyadic transfer failure; to be cut to the policy-mechanism form and staged on the general confidence gap with politics as the field case; staged as **I Know How It Works**, cut to the felt-grasp-larger-than-the-actual vector no staged misfit holds, a sense of understanding a mechanism that runs far deeper than the mechanism actually held, an illusion specific to explanatory how-it-works knowledge that survives because ordinary argument asks for reasons and a side and never for the step-by-step how, so confident and even extreme positions rest on an understanding that is not there and polarise as each side trades justifications that never test it, exposed only by a demand to trace the mechanism and not to justify, named clear of Second Nature (a solo overestimate of one's own causal grasp, not a dyadic curse-of-knowledge transfer failure) and The Cargo Cult (a grasp that feels complete and is not, not a ritual form copied without its mechanism), with the illusion of explanatory depth and its specificity to how-it-works knowledge and the collapse of the self-rating on tracing homed to Rozenblit and Keil, and the reasons-confirm-while-mechanism-collapses asymmetry and the discourse of unexamined conviction to Fernbach, Rogers, Fox, and Sloman, the moderation-of-position effect carried hedged as the more contested layer over the reliable collapse of the certainty itself, the confident holder and the mechanism-asker and the reason-trader and the collective cast as the personas The Knower, The Questioner, The Opponent, and The Public

Computers_, 1990; Mars Pathfinder, 1997): a low-priority task holds a resource a high-priority task needs, a medium-priority task preempts the low one for unrelated reasons, and every scheduling decision is individually correct while the composition starves the highest-priority work, a scheduling-and-locking interaction no staged misfit touches; the remedy priority-inheritance protocols, and to be given a human-scale metaphor so it carries the dramatic register; staged as **Outranked from Below**, cut to the rank-does-not-pass-through-a-held-resource vector no staged misfit holds, one machine that serves the highest-ranked ready work at every step and never breaks its rule, yet starves its highest-priority work without bound because the lowest task holds a resource it needs and a middle-ranked task, needing nothing and holding nothing, keeps taking its rightful turn ahead of the frozen holder, so a priority order honoured decision by decision inverts in the sum, ended only by lending the holder the waiter's rank for the span it holds the lock, named clear of The Tyranny of the Urgent (a ranking misjudged by salience, not a right ranking defeated by a held resource), The Anticommons and Vetocracy (a standing veto and a permanent block, not one holder and an unbounded delay that ends the instant the low task runs), and No Idle Hands (a queue swollen by utilisation, not a starvation that strikes an all-but-idle system), and cut clear of deadlock (a delay, not a mutual-wait cycle), with the formal unbounded-inversion account and the priority-inheritance and ceiling protocols homed to Sha, Rajkumar, and Lehoczky, the Mars Pathfinder watchdog-reset flight case to Reeves as one attested case, and the Mesa antecedent to Lampson and Redell, the remedy carried as a wait bounded rather than abolished and bought with foresight, the high, low, middle, and scheduling seats cast as the personas The Foremost, The Holder, The Middle, and The Dispatcher
- [x] [P2] Bufferbloat (Gettys & Nichols, _ACM Queue_, 2011; Nichols & Jacobson, CoDel, 2012): a router adds a large buffer to avoid dropping packets, locally sensible since memory is cheap, but the oversized buffer hides the loss-and-delay signal congestion control needs to back off, so the protective buffer fills and latency balloons, breaking the feedback it was meant to serve, distinct from No Idle Hands's utilisation math and The Bullwhip Effect's signal amplification in that here the signal is concealed rather than absent or amplified, the three-way cut to be drawn with care; the remedy active queue management; staged as **Too Kind to Drop**, cut to the cushion-hides-the-signal vector no staged misfit holds, a buffer placed before a bottleneck to absorb bursts and avoid drops, made deep because memory is cheap and a dropped packet looks like waste, that swallows the loss-and-delay signal congestion control reads as its cue to slow, so the sender never backs off, the buffer fills into a persistent standing queue, and latency balloons while throughput still reads full, the cushion meant to protect throughput destroying responsiveness, ended only by active queue management dropping or marking packets early to restore the signal, named clear of No Idle Hands (a queue swollen by driving a resource to full utilisation, not a signal concealed by a deep buffer), The Bullwhip Effect (a demand signal amplified up a multi-stage chain, not a signal present and swallowed in one hop), and Held Under (a retry-fed metastable congestion collapse that destroys throughput and persists after its trigger, the near-opposite failure joined only by the active-queue-management remedy), with the oversized-buffer diagnosis and the concealed signal homed to Gettys and Nichols, the controlled-delay active-queue-management remedy and the good-queue and bad-queue distinction to Nichols and Jacobson, the loss-based congestion signal to Jacobson, and the Random Early Detection antecedent to Floyd and Jacobson, the remedy carried as a queue bounded rather than a buffer abolished, the sender and the deep-buffer discipline and the latency-sensitive flow and the active-queue-management discipline cast as the personas The Sender, The Keeper, The Interactive, and The Manager

Triage of the rest of the sweep, held strong but below the line or pending a cut: The Bathtub Curve as a population
mixture (Klutke, Kiessler & Wortman, 2003), a real mixture-of-mechanisms read shelved pending which shelf it sits on
beside the staged paradox cluster; Percolation's connectivity phase transition (Broadbent & Hammersley, 1957), held
against The Brittle Optimum's self-organised-criticality language until the static-percolation-versus-driven-SOC cut is
airtight; Physical Aging in glasses (Struik, 1978), held as too narrow a variant of Fatigue Failure to stand beside it;
the CAP theorem (Brewer, 2000; Gilbert & Lynch, 2002), held as reading more impossibility-theorem than emergent trap and
adjacent to the Two Generals problem; Scale-Free robust-yet-fragile (Albert, Jeong & Barabási, 2000), held as crowding
The Head Start's preferential attachment and The Brittle Optimum's robust-yet-fragile; Selectorate Theory (Bueno de
Mesquita et al., 2003), held as abstract and villain-prone; Original Antigenic Sin (Francis, 1960) and the Hygiene
Hypothesis (Strachan, 1989), held on contested or reframed evidence and Ecological-Trap adjacency; Apparent Competition
(Holt, 1977), held as distinct but the least dramatic; and the Peak-End Rule (Kahneman et al., 1993), held as needing a
speculative market cut. The regulatory-ossification and the medical-autoimmunity readings are held as too close to
Vetocracy and to the already-held Autoimmunity respectively. The seventeen slated above are the sweep's refill, worked
from the top.

The refill is worked from the top: Immunopathology, the first slated, is staged as **In Its Own Defence**, cut to the
defence that destroys what it defends, a response proportioned to a real threat wrecking the host with the weapons that
clear the invader and amplifying past the threat with no fast brake, named clear of First Do Harm (an external remedy in
a new site) and Alarm Fatigue (the mirror under-reaction). Its three-lens review re-homed the proportional-scaling
premise from Zinkernagel and Doherty to Medzhitov's resistance while keeping the Zinkernagel-Doherty finding for the
overkill, corrected the amplification from no brake to no fast brake the feedback outpaces, and recast the weapons' harm
as indiscriminacy rather than misidentification. That takes the house to one hundred and twenty-six. The next slated,
The Evolution of Virulence, waits behind a held target: the somatic clonal evolution of the blood, staged as a system
sickness, is promoted on a distinct cut settled by a research sweep. The daylight from Fit to Fail is not the renewal
that grows the clone, which alone reads as evolutionary suicide and collides, but the level asymmetry: a body is a
truce, a higher-level individual that exists only by holding its own cells' selection down through differentiation,
apoptosis, senescence, and surveillance, so the clone is the resumption of a suppressed lower-level process that
destroys the very suppressor it is a part of, the killer a component of its victim and dying with it, per-organism and
heritable to no host generation. It is cut clear of The Commons too (no agent and no restraint lever, a defector lineage
displacing rather than a shared resource depleting), of In Its Own Defence (immunity is a suppressor whose failure
permits it, not a defence overshooting), and of No Way Back Up (positive selection for a fitter clone, not the
drift-loss of the fittest class). The harm is to be staged as the clone jamming the normal blood's maturation and
poisoning its niche, not evicting it, and the remedy as a trade paid in the renewal the body needs, never as doom, some
leukaemias being curable; hedged past a flat no-cause (radiation, benzene, prior therapy, and inherited and age-related
risk are real) and past selection as the sole author (drift and niche matter). Now staged as **The Peace It Keeps**, the
body a truce and the disease a coup by one of its own cells, the whole-piece The Coup and the spine piece The Truce, its
clone-side cast renamed off Fit to Fail's own Edge and Advantage (to The Gain and The Upstart) and its remedy persona
named clear of the medical siblings' Physician and Clinician (The Attending). Its three-lens review confirmed the
distinctness from Fit to Fail holds on the level asymmetry, corrected a reference slip that had the clone kill by
displacing tissue against the jam-not-evict cut, documented Gatenby's competitive release, and softened the imatinib and
transmissible-cancer claims off an overreach. That takes the house to one hundred and twenty-seven.

To break the run of biomedical births, the next slated target, The Evolution of Virulence, is held one slot and The
Condorcet Paradox and Arrow's Impossibility is taken out of order, staged as **Whoever Asks Last**, cut to the
incoherent-whole vector: every voter ranks honestly and consistently, yet the pairwise majority can cycle, so the group
has no coherent choice of its own and the winner is set by the order of the votes, with no fair rule to supply the
coherence the group lacks (Arrow). The whole-piece is The Made Majority; the trap is staged multidimensional in the place
The Grid so Black's single-peaked median escape is unavailable and forced, and held to a tendency and not every-vote. It
is named clear of Vetocracy (blocked-but-coherent against proceeds-but-incoherent), The Trip to Abilene (a group that
cannot see what it wants, not one with no what-it-wants to see), The Tyranny of Small Decisions, and The Sort, with the
agenda-setter renamed The Presider off that neighbour's Convener, and kept sincere throughout and clear of
Gibbard-Satterthwaite's strategic manipulation. Its three-lens review found the theorem statements exemplary, corrected a
no-hero inversion and a Condorcet overclaim (that the winner is no voter's first choice, false for the canonical cycle),
and confirmed the distinctness holds on all four neighbours. That takes the house to one hundred and twenty-eight.

The slate then returns to The Evolution of Virulence at the top of the P1 line, staged as **Sick Enough to Spread**, cut
to the coupling of replication and damage where selection drags virulence to the intermediate level that transmits best,
a durable equilibrium sicker than the host would choose. Its three-lens review trimmed an overclaim that the parasite
was a net loser at its own fitness optimum in a single-strain model (Lens A), confirmed the no-hero/no-villain framing
holds (Lens B), and verified the five named neighbours (Fit to Fail, The Commons, The False Trade-Off, The Peace It Keeps,
and In Its Own Defence) were cleanly distinguished (Lens C). That takes the house to one hundred and twenty-nine, and
returns the slate to Antagonistic Pleiotropy at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate then returns to Antagonistic Pleiotropy at the top of the P1 line, staged as **Early and Uncounted**, cut to
the age-structured decline in the force of selection: an early effect is weighed against a nearly whole cohort and
rewarded in full, a late effect against whatever remnant extrinsic mortality has left and discounted almost to nothing,
the same fair tally producing both, senescence the summed residue of many such favoured, unbilled trades. It is named
clear of Fit to Fail (no threshold and no collapse, only gradual accrual) and Seed Corn (no agent deliberates the trade,
the asymmetry a fact about when a fair count can reach), with Kirkwood's disposable-soma resource variant credited and
held off entire, the play staging why the gene is retained rather than how the body allocates. That takes the house to
one hundred and thirty, and returns the slate to Fatigue Failure and Cumulative Damage at the top of the unstruck P1
line.

A staging off the slate, from the floor rather than the backlog: The Permission of the Emergency, cut from a
practitioner observation that a household finds three hours to sit and wait out a burst pipe and never forty minutes for
a bath. It is staged to the justification account of idleness (Hsee, Yang, and Wang on idleness aversion and the need for
justifiable busyness, with Bellezza, Paharia, and Keinan on busyness as status, Tonietto, Malkoc, Reczek, and Norton on
leisure spoiled by the sense of waste, and Kaplan's attention restoration as the good that is starved), cut to the vector
no staged misfit holds: rest is entered only under a licence, and a crisis is the readiest one, so the emergency grants
the leave to stop that ordinary rest is never given. Named clear of The Tyranny of the Urgent (task over task within one
actor's attention, the deferred thing high-worth work rather than restorative rest, the crisis licensing a stillness and
not merely out-shouting it), The Firefighter's Medal (an audience crediting the save and a shared capacity that erodes,
where here the permission is granted by the situation itself and nothing spirals), and Moral Licensing (a virtue
permitting a vice, not a disaster permitting a rest). That takes the house to one hundred and thirty-one, and leaves the
slate unmoved, Fatigue Failure and Cumulative Damage still at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to Fatigue Failure and Cumulative Damage at the top of the P1 line, staged as **The Load It Always
Carried**, cut to metal fatigue's accumulation structure: every cycle sits below the yield strength and passes every
instantaneous check, yet each spends an irreversible increment no check reads, the increments summing until a crack
nucleates and runs to a sudden fracture under the exact load the part always carried, the failure authored by the count
and no cycle at fault. It is named clear of The Brittle Optimum (no slack cut and no shock, repetition alone), The New
Normal (a physical damage, not a drift of judgment), Seed Corn (no deliberated trade), and the each-choice-sound-sum-fatal
decision family the house already stages (no choice inside the mechanism, only a load every check correctly passes and a
damage no check can see), with Wöhler's below-yield fatigue and endurance limit, Palmgren and Miner's linear cumulative
damage, and Paris and Erdogan's crack-growth law credited and carried. That takes the house to one hundred and thirty-two,
and returns the slate to Bode's Sensitivity Integral at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to Bode's Sensitivity Integral at the top of the P1 line, staged as **The Calm You Pay For**, cut to the
feedback conservation law: a loop's sensitivity can be pushed below one in the band where the disturbances live, buying
real rejection where it matters, but the integral of the log-sensitivity is conserved, so it is forced above one
elsewhere, the calm paid for as an amplifying rise out of band, worse where the plant is unstable and the conserved total
turns positive. It is named clear of The Balloon Effect (a behavioural displacement of a trade, not a conservation law and
no shared spine), The False Trade-Off (a trade-off the world does have, not an artefact of conditioning on a sum), The
Simpler Lever (a conservation constraint no variety removes, not a repertoire shortfall), and The Spent Margin (a rise
arriving by theorem, not a margin spent back by anyone), with Bode's sensitivity integral and Freudenberg and Looze's
constraints from right-half-plane poles, non-minimum-phase zeros, and delays credited and carried. That takes the house to
one hundred and thirty-three, and returns the slate to Administrative Burden and sludge at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to Administrative Burden and sludge at the top of the P1 line, staged as **Too Much to Ask**, cut to the
access-friction structure: a programme built to serve adds each verification, form, and wait for a defensible reason, but
the accreted learning, compliance, and psychological costs deter the very people it was built for, so take-up falls below
eligibility and the burden falls hardest on the neediest, no gate built and no one refused. It is named clear of The Paper
Trail (provider-side defensive documentation protecting the professional, the opposite failure to the claimant going
unserved), Nobody Reads It (an unread notice, not the accreted cost of entering the interaction at all), and The Tyranny
of Small Decisions (a selection that screens the intended beneficiaries by their capacity to bear the friction, not an
unwilled aggregate), with the three costs credited to Moynihan, Herd, and Harbage, the take-up gap to Herd and Moynihan,
and the regressive incidence to Sunstein, and the deliberate-sludge barrier the literature also records left aside for the
earnest accretion alone. That takes the house to one hundred and thirty-four, and returns the slate to The Identifiable
Victim Effect at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to The Identifiable Victim Effect at the top of the P1 line, staged as **The One in Front of You**, cut
to the allocation structure: a giver responds to the one named, pictured, present victim with real, proportionate
compassion and directs resources there, while an equal or larger statistical, anonymous, future population draws no
feeling and goes underfunded, each act of compassion sound and the sum a neglect of the unseen many that no giver chose,
and the obvious cure failing because reasoning past the face cools the heart that gave without warming it to the number.
It is named clear of Not Worth My While (demand-side allocation driven by identifiability, not a supply-side benefit too
thin per head) and The Availability Cascade (a static, single-decision affective bias, not a media snowball over time),
with the identified and statistical lives credited to Schelling, the donation mechanism and the perverse effect of
deliberation to Small, Loewenstein, and Slovic, and psychic numbing to Slovic, the identified victim and the statistical
many cast as personas and the face and the number as the objects they carry. That takes the house to one hundred and
thirty-five, and returns the slate to The Homevoter Hypothesis at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to The Homevoter Hypothesis at the top of the P1 line, staged as **The Only Basket**, cut to the
portfolio-defence structure: a homeowner whose largest asset cannot be diversified votes at the local level to restrict
the nearby development that might depress its value, a prudent, sincere defence that sums town by town into a regional
housing shortage no one voted for, its cost falling on newcomers who were never at the ballot, and the escape, raising the
decision to a higher table where the excluded count, stripping the owner of the only hedge on an undiversifiable asset. It
is named clear of The Sort (a stationary incumbent and the zoning ballot, not a mobile chooser sorting by preference), The
Lowest Bar (the base stationary and the newcomer excluded, not a mobile base courted), and The Tyranny of Small Decisions
(the asset-insurance motive of an undiversifiable home and the disenfranchisement of the cost-bearers, not a merely
unmanaged sum), with the homevoter hypothesis and zoning-as-insurance credited to Fischel and the supply-and-price effect
to Glaeser and Gyourko. That takes the house to one hundred and thirty-six, and returns the slate to Metastable Failure
and the Retry Storm at the top of the unstruck P1 line.

The slate returns to Metastable Failure and the Retry Storm at the top of the P1 line, staged as **Held Under**, cut to
the self-sustaining-overload structure: a transient trigger, a brief spike or pause, tips a sound system past a threshold
into a failing state, its clients retry the failed requests exactly as well-behaved clients should, and the retry load
itself now exceeds capacity, so the system stays failing long after the trigger is gone, held under by nothing but
everyone's correct recovery, a bad equilibrium that sustains itself and cannot clear on its own correct behaviour because
that behaviour is the load. The escape is not free: something must break the loop by force, tripping a circuit breaker to
fail fast, backing the retries off with jitter, and shedding load until the sum drops below capacity, bought by rejecting
requests that would have succeeded and dropping real users, so recovery is paid for in the real work thrown away to drain
the queue. It is named clear of The Bullwhip Effect (a demand signal amplified stage by stage up a multi-echelon chain,
not a single system whose own clients' retries feed straight back onto it), No Idle Hands (a steady-state congestion curve
with no memory that eases the moment the load does, not a stuck bad equilibrium that persists after its trigger is gone),
and The Bank Run (a bistable collapse whose sustaining loop runs on belief, each depositor withdrawing because others
might, not on pure retry mechanism with no fear, expectation, or belief anywhere in the loop), with the metastable-failure
framework credited to Bronson, Charapko, Aghayev, and Zhu, the field study and amplification mechanisms to Huang,
Magnusson, and colleagues, congestion collapse and its cure to Nagle and Jacobson, and the retry storm and circuit breaker
to Nygard, the trigger cast as the persona The Surge that departs before the failure peaks, the correct client as The
Caller whose right reattempt is the load, and the escape as The Operator who trips the breaker at a price. That takes the
house to one hundred and thirty-seven, and clears the P1 line, returning the slate to Thermal Runaway at the top of the
unstruck P2 line.

The slate returns to Thermal Runaway at the top of the P2 line, staged as **Faster Than It Can Cool**, cut to the
sign-flipping-feedback structure: a system makes heat and sheds it, and because the generation rises exponentially with
temperature while the loss rises only linearly, its operating point is genuinely, provably stable for the whole of its
life, sitting a determinate distance below a hard critical threshold. Ordinary ageing alone, a resistance rising, a
cooling path fouling, an ambient climbing, with no shock and no wear, brings that threshold down to meet the operating
temperature, and at the tangency the stabilising feedback reverses its sign, so past the point the same warming that
always settled now amplifies and the system runs away to destruction with nothing left to cool it. The escape is not
free: holding the margin, derating below the point and adding cooling, costs real capacity, never abolishes the
exponential-beats-linear asymmetry, and cannot stop the point drifting closer as the system ages, a permanent tax against
a threshold that never stops approaching. It is named clear of The Unmarked Edge (a soft gradient migration pulled by an
incentive slope, not a hard physical bifurcation with no agent and no gradient), The Load It Always Carried (a hidden
tally of accumulating damage summed to fracture, not a feedback sign-flip in a system that stays sound and unworn), and
Held Under (capture by a second stable state a loop sustains, not a loss of every equilibrium at once), with the
exponential-source-against-linear-sink tangency credited to Semenov, the distributed critical parameter above which no
steady state exists to Frank-Kamenetskii, and the lithium-ion characteristic temperatures and the ageing of the margin to
Feng, Ouyang, and colleagues, the two opposed laws cast as the personas The Reaction and The Sink and the human who holds
the margin at a cost as The Engineer. That takes the house to one hundred and thirty-eight, and returns the slate to
Fiber-Bundle Load-Redistribution Cascade at the top of the unstruck P2 line.

The slate returns to Fiber-Bundle Load-Redistribution Cascade at the top of the P2 line, staged as **Onto the Survivors**,
cut to the redistribution-cascade structure: a load is carried by many parallel members at once, each holding a share
below its own strength, so the bundle is genuinely redundant and sound and every member checked alone passes. The member
strengths are drawn from a spread, so there is always a weakest, and when the load parts it, its share does not vanish but
falls onto the survivors, raising every remaining share, and since the survivors are drawn from the same spread the raised
share may exceed the next-weakest and part it too. Below a critical fraction of members lost the avalanche of successive
partings is finite and absorbed and the bundle holds at a higher strain, but past that fraction each transfer breaks the
next and the cascade runs to the complete rupture of the whole, from a state an instant before that was holding. The
escape is not free: adding members, widening margins, and breaking the local sharing all cost real material and weight
and none abolishes that a shared load rises on every survivor as members drop, so the redundancy is a distance bought
against a cascade the sharing will always, past a point, carry through. It is named clear of The Brittle Optimum (slack
removed to reach an optimum meeting a shock, not an intact redundancy cascading with nothing removed and nothing struck),
Belt and Braces (redundant components defeated together by a common cause, not independent members failing one at a time
by sequential load transfer), and The Load It Always Carried (a single part accumulating a hidden tally of damage over
cycles, not the near-instant redistribution of load across many sound members), with the probabilistic bundle, equal load
sharing, and the critical load credited to Daniels and the redistribution avalanche and the local-load-sharing running
front to Pradhan, Hansen, and Chakrabarti, the individual member and the surviving members cast as the personas The
Strand and The Survivor and the one who sizes the redundancy at a cost as The Engineer. That takes the house to one
hundred and thirty-nine, and returns the slate to Shrouded-Attribute Pricing under Present Bias at the top of the unstruck
P2 line.

The slate returns to Shrouded-Attribute Pricing under Present Bias at the top of the P2 line, staged as **No One Gains by
Telling You**, cut to the stable-exploitation-equilibrium structure: a good is sold as a low, salient base and a high,
deferred add-on, and competing firms post the low base where the buyer compares and shroud the dear add-on that funds it,
because the lowest visible price wins the sale. The buyer underweights the deferred add-on, not from stupidity but from a
real, documented present bias, so the myopic take the cheap base and meet the add-on and pay more across the whole than an
honest single price, a myopia tax. The one obvious cure fails: a firm that unshrouds, advertising the true total, wins no
one, because a debiased buyer with a close substitute for the add-on avoids the add-on rather than rewarding the honest
firm, so the truth turns myopes into sophisticates who need only the cheap base and not the teller, and the honest firm
bears the cost of education and captures none of the reward. So the shroud holds as a stable competitive equilibrium and
the tax flows from the myopic to the sophisticated who dodge the add-on and ride the cheap base they fund, with no
swindler and no fool. It is named clear of Time Inconsistency (an intrapersonal broken plan within a single self, a
present self betraying a future one, not a two-sided market equilibrium between firms and consumers with nothing
promised) and Nobody Reads It (an informed-minority search-cost drift that more reading would discipline, not a curse of
debiasing in which more information cannot, the add-on cost knowable and even the sophisticated understanding it, the bias
in the weighing and not the reading), with the shrouded-attribute equilibrium and the curse of debiasing credited to
Gabaix and Laibson and the field evidence of present-biased contract choice to DellaVigna and Malmendier, the shrouding
firm and the biased buyer and the transparent rival cast as the personas The Firm, The Myope, and The Honest Rival. That
takes the house to one hundred and forty, and returns the slate to Rational Ignorance of the Voter at the top of the
unstruck P2 line.

The slate returns to Rational Ignorance of the Voter at the top of the P2 line, staged as **Cheaper Not to Know**, cut to
the negligible-return structure: to vote well a citizen must learn the records, the measures, and what the offices do, at
a real cost in time and effort, but the benefit of that learning lands only if the better-informed vote changes the
outcome, and in an election of millions the chance that a single vote decides anything is vanishingly small, so the
expected return to becoming informed, a better result times a near-zero chance of bringing it about, is near zero against
a real cost. So the citizen rationally stays ignorant and votes on a cheap cue, the party, the name, the endorsement, and
an electorate of individually rational citizens governs itself on cues rather than knowledge, half-blind at no voter's
fault. The cure fails on the same arithmetic: the diligent citizen who masters it all casts one vote as unpivotal as any
other and changes no outcome, and exhorting the public to be informed asks each citizen a real cost for a return one vote
cannot make positive. It is named clear of Nobody Reads It (a contract-term search cost in a market with a drafter, where
a larger informed readership would discipline the terms, not a near-zero pivotality with no market and no discipline, even
a fully informed ballot as unpivotal as an ignorant one) and Everybody's Business (a volunteer's dilemma where one actor
would suffice and the failure is diffusion, not a near-zero return where informing oneself secures nothing on its own),
scoped to the not-acquiring decision and hedged clear of the turnout paradox, expressive voting, and rational
irrationality, with the near-zero pivotality and the rational-ignorance calculus and the cue-voting shortcut credited to
Downs and the measured depth of political ignorance and its cost to democratic competence to Somin, the rational citizen
and the diligent one and the collective cast as the personas The Voter, The Diligent, and The Electorate. That takes the
house to one hundred and forty-one, and returns the slate to The Illusion of Explanatory Depth at the top of the unstruck
P2 line.

The house strikes The Illusion of Explanatory Depth, staged as **I Know How It Works**. The trap is a feeling: the sense
of understanding how a thing works, a bicycle, a zipper, a policy, is one of the most convincing feelings the mind
produces, and it is produced whether or not the understanding is there. The felt grasp runs far larger than the actual
one, and the gap is widest exactly for explanatory, how-it-works knowledge, because a grasp of what a thing does is
easily mistaken for a grasp of how it does it (Rozenblit and Keil). What makes it a trap and not merely a quirk is what
conversation asks for. Argument asks for reasons and a side, which are cheap and plentiful and never test the mechanism,
so giving them round after round confirms the felt grasp rather than probing it and can even harden the position, each
justification feeling like more understanding (Fernbach, Rogers, Fox, and Sloman). The one move that would test it, a
demand to trace the mechanism step by step, from enacted policy to promised effect, from crank to wheel, is awkward and
slow and almost never made, so the grasp is never put to the one question it cannot answer. When it is made, the grasp
collapses: the fluency stops partway, the middle simply blank, and the self-rating drops sharply from where it stood a
minute before (Rozenblit and Keil), and a whole public can hold firm and opposed views on mechanisms almost none of its
members could trace, polarising by trading reasons that never touch the understanding beneath, each side sincere and no
one lying. None of it is folly or dishonesty: the felt understanding visits the careful and the expert as readily as
anyone, the confident position is held in good faith, and the discourse of unexamined conviction is authored by no one.
It is cut to the felt-grasp-larger-than-the-actual vector no staged misfit holds and staged on the general confidence
gap with policy as the field case, the one solvent, a demand to explain and not to justify, being exactly the move the
discourse is built not to make. The moderation of the position as the certainty falls is carried hedged throughout as
the more contested layer of the finding, the reliable core being the collapse of the certainty itself. It is named clear
of Second Nature (a solo overestimate of one's own causal grasp, not a dyadic curse-of-knowledge transfer failure across
a gap between two minds) and The Cargo Cult (a grasp that sincerely feels complete and is not, not a ritual form
knowingly copied without its mechanism), with the illusion of explanatory depth and its specificity to how-it-works
knowledge and the collapse of the self-rating on tracing credited to Rozenblit and Keil, and the reasons-confirm-while-
mechanism-collapses asymmetry and the polarising discourse of unexamined conviction to Fernbach, Rogers, Fox, and Sloman,
the confident holder and the mechanism-asker and the reason-trader and the collective cast as the personas The Knower,
The Questioner, The Opponent, and The Public. That takes the house to one hundred and forty-two, and returns the slate to
Priority Inversion at the top of the unstruck P2 line.

The house strikes Priority Inversion, staged as **Outranked from Below**. The trap is a composition of correct decisions.
Set one machine that does one thing at a time under a rule anyone would keep, always run the highest-ranked ready work,
and it does right by the order in nearly every instant. Now let the highest and the lowest work need the same resource,
a lock only one may hold. The low work takes it rightfully and begins a short task; the high work needs it, finds it
held, and blocks, a wait that at this moment is short and bounded. Then a task of middle rank becomes ready, needing none
of it and holding nothing, and the rule correctly hands it the machine because it outranks the low work, so the holder is
frozen mid-task still holding the lock, and the high work, which outranks everyone, waits behind ordinary middle work for
as long as that work runs (Sha, Rajkumar, and Lehoczky). The intermediate task is the element that makes the wait
unbounded, and when the wait runs long enough something downstream gives up on the highest work: on Mars Pathfinder in
1997 a watchdog read a starved bus task as failure and reset the spacecraft, losing data, with nothing actually broken
(Reeves). Replay the sequence and every decision was the right decision, the machine having served the highest ready work
each time, the middle work having taken its rightful turn, the lock held as a lock must be, so the inversion is authored
by no one and the fault, if the word applied, is only that holding a resource carries no rank the rule can read. It is
cut to the rank-does-not-pass-through-a-held-resource vector no staged misfit holds, given a human-scale register of a top
job waiting behind ordinary middle work because the junior who holds the one thing it needs keeps being pulled off, and
the one solvent, lending the holder the waiter's rank for the span it holds the lock so the middle work can no longer cut
in, is carried as a wait bounded rather than abolished and bought with foresight designed in before the timing lines up,
the priority-inheritance and ceiling protocols homed to Sha, Rajkumar, and Lehoczky and the Mesa antecedent to Lampson
and Redell. It is named clear of The Tyranny of the Urgent (a ranking misjudged by salience, where here the ranking is
exactly right and the highest work starves anyway, the middle task not urgent but only higher-ranked than the holder and
ready), The Anticommons and Vetocracy (a standing veto and a permanent block, where here there is one holder, no veto,
and an unbounded delay that ends the instant the low task runs), and No Idle Hands (a queue swollen by driving a resource
to full use, where here the highest work can starve in an all-but-idle system), and cut clear of deadlock (a delay of
correctly ranked work, not a mutual-wait cycle with nothing permanently stuck), with the high, low, middle, and
scheduling seats cast as the personas The Foremost, The Holder, The Middle, and The Dispatcher, and the Pathfinder reset
carried as one attested case and not a claim that every inversion ends in a reset. That takes the house to one hundred
and forty-three, and returns the slate to Bufferbloat at the top of the unstruck P2 line.

The house strikes Bufferbloat, staged as **Too Kind to Drop**. The trap is a kindness that hides a signal. A buffer is
placed in front of a bottleneck link to absorb a burst that would otherwise be lost, and because memory is cheap and a
dropped packet looks like pure waste, the buffer is made deep, a plainly sensible thing to do. But a lost packet, and
the delay a filling buffer adds, is the exact signal end-to-end congestion control reads as its cue to slow down: the
sender speeds up until it sees loss, then backs off. A buffer deep enough never to drop is deep enough to swallow that
cue, so the sender is told nothing, keeps ramping, and fills the buffer to the top, where it stays full, a persistent
standing queue rather than a transient one, and every packet now crosses seconds of backlog while the link's throughput
still reads full (Gettys and Nichols). None of it is a blunder: the buffer was sized large for good reason and the
sender ramps because that is how congestion control works, so the good queue that absorbs a burst and drains has quietly
become the bad queue that fills and stands, on a link no one overloaded. It is cut to the cushion-hides-the-signal
vector no staged misfit holds, and the one solvent, active queue management dropping or marking packets early and on
purpose so the sender is told in time, runs against the cheap-memory instinct that built the trap, must be designed into
the bottleneck ahead of the fact, and for years was so hard to tune it went rarely turned on, bounding the standing
queue rather than abolishing the buffer that still absorbs genuine bursts (Nichols and Jacobson; the Random Early
Detection antecedent, Floyd and Jacobson). It is named clear of No Idle Hands (a queue swollen by driving a resource to
full utilisation, where here a single well-behaved flow bloats an on-average-underloaded link because the feedback is
hidden), The Bullwhip Effect (a demand signal amplified up a multi-stage chain, where here the signal is present and
swallowed in one hop, a suppression and not an amplification), and Held Under (a retry-fed metastable congestion
collapse that destroys throughput and persists after its trigger, the near-opposite failure, throughput preserved and
latency alone ballooning, the two joined only by the active-queue-management remedy), with the oversized-buffer diagnosis
and the concealed signal homed to Gettys and Nichols, the controlled-delay remedy and the good-queue and bad-queue
distinction to Nichols and Jacobson, the loss-based congestion signal to Jacobson, and the earlier active queue
management to Floyd and Jacobson, the sender and the deep-buffer discipline and the latency-sensitive flow and the
active-queue-management discipline cast as the personas The Sender, The Keeper, The Interactive, and The Manager. That
takes the house to one hundred and forty-four.

With Bufferbloat struck, the P2 well is dry: the four-field sweep, the discourse-and-boundary batch, and the reserves
that carried the run to the century and past it are all staged, and no unstruck target remains above this line. The
slate is refilled below by a fresh research-input pass, each candidate run through REFERENCES.md by canonical name and
against the structural test, that the pattern must run in the system regardless of which agent is inside it. The
concordance caught one before slating: Gresham's Law is already staged as Bad Coin, bad money driving out good, so it is
struck, not stacked beside it. The three below survive the check and are slated, worked from the top.

      uninformed for free, so if the price reflected all of it no one would be paid for gathering it and no one would, and
      the price could not reflect it, an equilibrium that must stay noisy enough that gathering still pays, distinct from
      Information Cascade's herding on observed actions and Cheaper Not to Know's near-zero pivotality of the voter in that
      the engine is a price signal that, by revealing costly private research for free, destroys the incentive to produce
      the very information it aggregates; the remedy is the noise or reward that keeps information-gathering paid, and to be
      given a market-native register with a plain metaphor for the paradox; staged as **Priced In**, cut to the
      signal-that-reveals-effort-for-free-destroys-the-effort vector no staged misfit holds, a market price that
      aggregates the informed traders' costly research and reveals it to everyone for free the moment they act, so each
      uninformed trader rationally reads the answer off the price rather than pay to gather it, and a fully informative
      price would leave no return to gathering and so no one to gather, self-defeating, the market resting instead at an
      equilibrium degree of disequilibrium, informative enough to be useful and noisy enough that gathering still pays,
      the noise supplied by traders who on average lose, named clear of Information Cascade (a held private signal set
      aside by imitation, not a signal never gathered because the price reveals others' research for free), Cheaper Not
      to Know (a voter's non-self-referential ignorance with no market and no price to aggregate, not a paradox turning
      on the price's own efficiency removing the incentive that produces it), and The Winning Bid (an auction winner's
      curse falling on the winner through estimate dispersion, not a standing property of a continuous market's price
      with no auction and no single winner), the impossibility result and the equilibrium degree of disequilibrium and
      the noise that must persist homed to Grossman and Stiglitz, the no-hero carried as a permanent incompleteness
      bought by the noise traders' average losses and not a flaw awaiting a fix, the informed and the uninformed and the
      value-blind trader and the price-setting mechanism cast as the personas The Informed, The Uninformed, The Noise
      Trader, and The Market
- [x] [P2] The Financial Instability Hypothesis (Minsky, _Stabilizing an Unstable Economy_, 1986; "The Financial
      Instability Hypothesis", 1992): a stretch of calm rationally lowers the risk premium and raises leverage, so hedge

      and staged elsewhere, the no-hero carried as a fragility built by prudence in the good times and a backstop that
      cushions the fall while validating the risk that feeds the next cycle, the safe, middle, top, and lending postures

      synchronized withdrawal in that the engine is a mechanical synchronization of correct actions on a shared expiry, the

      Under adjacency so the cut is made to the coincidence and not to a self-sustaining loop; staged as **All at Once**, cut
      to the synchronized-coincidence-of-correct-actions vector no staged misfit holds, a popular value cached with a time
      to live so the backend is spared while it is warm, but because the value is popular its expiry is a single shared
      instant, so every concurrent request misses at once and each correctly recomputes from the backend, and the very
      popularity that made caching worthwhile concentrates all the misses into one synchronized spike that overwhelms the
      backend the cache was there to protect, no request out of line and nothing misconfigured, ended only by
      de-synchronizing or collapsing the recomputations, named clear of Held Under (a retry-fed bistable overload that
      persists after its trigger, not a one-shot spike synchronized by a shared clock that subsides once re-cached, a

      each acts from fear of the others, not a mechanical coincidence on a shared expiry with no belief anywhere in the
      loop), with the formal treatment and probabilistic early recomputation homed to Vattani, Chierichetti, and
      Lowenstein and the lease and request-coalescing remedy to Nishtala and colleagues, the thundering herd credited as

      served a little stale, a request made to wait, or a refresh done early, the caller and the store and the origin and
      the remedy discipline cast as the personas The Request, The Cache, The Backend, and The Warden

Triage of the rest of this pass, against REFERENCES.md. Held pending a distinct cut, each with a real spine but a fit
still to find: the Osborne Effect (the pre-announcement of a better product collapsing present demand for the current
one), a documented business trap whose each-customer-rationally-waits engine is distinct but whose organisational spine
is thin and anecdotal, held until a cited treatment anchors it past the single Osborne Computer case. The three slated
above are the pass's refill, worked from the top, and past them a further research-input pass will be wanted, run the
same way, by canonical name through REFERENCES.md.

The house strikes The Impossibility of Informationally Efficient Markets, staged as **Priced In**. The trap is a signal
that reveals the effort behind it for free. A market price gathers what the informed have found out about what a thing
is worth and shows it, in a single number, to everyone who reads it, so a trader who has done no research can read the
answer off the price for nothing. But finding out what a thing is worth costs real effort, and a trader pays that cost
only when the return covers it, so the moment they trade their finding leaks into the price for free, and the better the
price reveals what the informed know, the less the informed are paid to know it. If the price ever reflected everything,
the return to finding anything out would be zero, no rational trader would pay the cost, and then the price would have
nothing to reflect, so a perfectly informative price is self-defeating (Grossman and Stiglitz). None of it is folly or a
swindle: the uninformed who reads the answer off the price is sensible, the informed who quits when the edge is gone is
sensible, the market that moves the price is doing its job, so the impossibility arises with no one cheating and nothing
rigged, the market's very success at spreading information undercutting the making of it. It is cut to the
signal-that-reveals-effort-for-free vector no staged misfit holds and given a market-native register, its plain metaphor
the analyst who stops doing the research once it is all priced in, and the one thing that keeps the finding-out alive is
that the price stays a little wrong, noisy with trades made for reasons unrelated to value, so the informed can hide and
keep some edge, an equilibrium degree of disequilibrium that is not a flaw to fix but the condition of the price working
at all, paid for by the noise traders who on average lose. It is named clear of Information Cascade (a held private
signal set aside by imitation, not a signal never gathered because the price reveals others' research for free), Cheaper
Not to Know (a voter's non-self-referential ignorance with no market and no price to aggregate, where here the price's
own efficiency removes the incentive that produces it), and The Winning Bid (an auction winner's curse falling on the
winner through estimate dispersion, not a standing property of a continuous market's price with no auction and no single
winner), with the impossibility result and the equilibrium degree of disequilibrium and the necessary noise homed to
Grossman and Stiglitz, and the informed, the uninformed, the value-blind trader, and the price-setting mechanism cast as
the personas The Informed, The Uninformed, The Noise Trader, and The Market. That takes the house to one hundred and
forty-five, and returns the slate to The Financial Instability Hypothesis at the top of the unstruck P2 line.

The house strikes The Financial Instability Hypothesis, staged as **The Long Calm**. The trap is a calm that breeds the
crash it looks least like. Give an economy a good long stretch of stability, loans repaid, defaults rare, prices
climbing, and everyone who took a risk rewarded for it, and a rational borrower reads that record and concludes,
correctly on the evidence, that the caution the last crisis taught is now overpriced, the danger it guarded against a
decade absent. A rational lender reads the same record and eases terms for the same reason. So the financing structure
migrates, unit by unit, from hedge finance, where income covers interest and principal both, to speculative finance,
where it covers only the interest and the principal must be rolled over, to Ponzi finance, where it covers neither and
only the rising price sustains it, and each step is a sound response to a genuine calm (Minsky). The fragility hides in
the calm, because everything that would test the leverage, a credit squeeze, a price fall, is exactly what the long calm
has not produced, so the structure grows most brittle precisely as it looks most secure, and a small shock, an ordinary
rise in rates or a shortfall, is then enough to keep one refinancing-dependent unit from rolling its debt, force it to
sell, and bring the whole of the accumulated fragility apart: stability itself destabilizing, the shock small and
interchangeable and the fragility, built by years of sound decisions, doing the work. None of it is folly or fraud: the
borrower and the lender each read a real record and responded soundly, and the collapse is authored by no one. It is cut
to the calm-breeds-the-fragility vector no staged misfit holds, and the fixes are hard for the reason the trap is:
leaning against the boom means restraining credit while everything looks fine and everyone is paid to have taken risk,
and the backstop that softens the fall validates the risk-taking after the fact and feeds the next cycle. It is named
clear of Debt-Deflation (the bust-side spiral of falling prices raising the real weight of debt, whereas this is the
boom-side build-up that makes the spiral possible, Minsky taking Fisher's debt-deflation as the shape of the descent),
The Brittle Optimum (a trimmed physical or organisational buffer meeting an external shock, whereas here the fragility
is accumulated leverage that tips from within, a Ponzi unit simply failing to roll), and The New Normal (a drift in what
is perceived as tolerable, whereas here the balance sheets objectively become more leveraged and refinancing-dependent),
with the hypothesis and the three financing postures homed to Minsky, the Minsky moment label to McCulley, and the
downward spiral to Fisher's debt-deflation staged elsewhere, and the safe, middle, top, and lending postures cast as the
personas The Hedged, The Speculator, The Ponzi, and The Lender. That takes the house to one hundred and forty-six, and
returns the slate to The Cache Stampede at the top of the unstruck P2 line.

The house strikes The Cache Stampede, staged as **All at Once**. The trap is a coincidence of correct actions. An
expensive value is cached with a time to live, so while it is warm every request is handed the cheap copy and the
expensive backend is spared, and the more callers converge on the one hot value the more the cache absorbs. But the
stored copy has one expiry, the same instant for every caller because they all read the one entry, so the moment it goes
stale every concurrent request misses at once, and each does exactly what a cache miss is defined to do, recompute the
value from the backend and write it back. Not one is wrong: a single miss recomputing is the whole correct mechanism.
But the misses are as many as the value was popular and as simultaneous as the expiry was shared, so the recomputation
that is nothing one at a time arrives as one synchronized flood that strikes the backend the cache existed to protect,
hardest at the moment the cache stops shielding it (Vattani, Chierichetti, and Lowenstein). It is authored by no one:
the cache served and expired as a cache should and every request kept the contract exactly, so the harm is in no action
but in their coincidence, the popularity that fills the cache and the shared clock that empties it building the stampede
between them. It is cut to the synchronized-coincidence vector no staged misfit holds, and the fixes all break the
coincidence rather than the correctness, scattering the refreshes off the shared cliff or collapsing them to a single
recompute the rest wait on (Nishtala and colleagues), each de-synchronizing rather than computing less and each bought
with a value served a little stale, a request made to wait, or a refresh done early, and built in ahead of time. It is
named clear of Held Under (a retry-fed bistable overload that persists after its trigger, whereas this is a one-shot
spike synchronized by a shared clock that subsides once the value is re-cached, a severe stampede tipping into a
persistent state being the two mechanisms composing, not this one alone) and The Bank Run (a self-fulfilling panic where
each acts from fear of what the others will do, whereas here no request believes anything or watches any other and the
synchrony is mechanical, a shared expiry timestamp, each request acting identically alone), with the formal treatment
and the probabilistic early recomputation homed to Vattani, Chierichetti, and Lowenstein, the lease and request
coalescing to Nishtala and colleagues, and the thundering herd credited as the antecedent family, and the caller, the
store, the origin, and the remedy discipline cast as the personas The Request, The Cache, The Backend, and The Warden.
That takes the house to one hundred and forty-seven.

With The Cache Stampede struck, the P2 well is dry again: Grossman and Stiglitz, Minsky, and the cache stampede are all

- [x] [P1] The Hold-Up Problem (Klein, Crawford & Alchian, "Vertical Integration, Appropriable Rents, and the

      transaction-cost and property-rights spine; staged as **Over a Barrel**, cut to the specific-investment-exposed-to-opportunism
      vector no staged misfit holds, a relationship-specific asset worth far more inside the deal than outside it sunk under

      the deal is never made, named clear of Moral Hazard, The Market for Lemons, and Good Money After Bad, the quasi-rent
      and the appropriation homed to Klein, Crawford, and Alchian, the fundamental transformation from many bidders to one
      to Williamson, and the incomplete-contract underinvestment to Grossman and Hart, the Fisher Body and GM case held
      lightly as contested, the load-bearing harm carried as the ex-ante underinvestment and not the ex-post transfer, and
      the exposed party, the party who reopens the terms, the alternatives that empty once the asset is specific, and the
      remedy of one ownership cast as the personas The Investor, The Buyer, The Rival, and The Owner
- [x] [P2] The Principle of Minimum Differentiation (Hotelling, "Stability in Competition", _Economic Journal_, 1929;
      Downs, _An Economic Theory of Democracy_, 1957, the median-voter theorem): competitors selling to a spread-out market

- [x] [P2] The Two-Sided Cold Start (Rochet & Tirole, "Platform Competition in Two-Sided Markets", _Journal of the
      European Economic Association_, 2003; Caillaud & Jullien, 2003): a platform is worth joining on each side only once
      the other side is there, so buyers wait for sellers and sellers wait for buyers, and each side's rational wait keeps

      cross-side-coordination-at-launch vector no staged misfit holds, a platform worth joining on each side only once the
      other side is there, so each side rationally waits for the other and the cross-side value never forms and a platform

      antecedent to Katz and Shapiro, the failure carried as one of two equilibria selected by expectations and not an

Triage of the rest of this pass, against REFERENCES.md. Held pending a distinct cut, each with a real spine but a fit
still to find: the Lucas Critique (Lucas, 1976), whose breakdown of estimated relationships when they are used for
policy reads more as a methodological caution than a locally-rational-sums-to-globally-bad behavioural trap and brushes
Metric Fixation's gamed measure, held until a cut is found that keeps it structural and clear of Metric Fixation; the
Pygmalion and Golem effects (Rosenthal & Jacobson, 1968), whose self-fulfilling expectation shaping a person's
performance is close to The Proxy's self-fulfilling statistical-discrimination loop, held until the interpersonal
expectation channel is cut clear of the group-proxy one; and the Liquidity Trap (Keynes, 1936; Krugman, 1998), whose
demand shortfall at the zero lower bound is adjacent to The Paradox of Thrift, held until the monetary-impotence sliver
is cut clear of the fallacy of composition already staged. The three slated above are the pass's refill, worked from the
top, and past them a further research-input pass will be wanted, run the same way, by canonical name through
REFERENCES.md.

The house strikes The Hold-Up Problem, staged as **Over a Barrel**. The trap is a value that only a tight fit can create
and only the tight fit exposes. A party can make the most of a deal by fitting an asset closely to this one relationship,
building the plant beside the factory or cutting the tool to the spec, but the closer the fit the less the asset is worth
anywhere else, so once it is sunk its value inside the relationship far exceeds its value outside, and that difference,
the quasi-rent, sits there to be taken (Klein, Crawford, and Alchian). The counterparty can reopen the terms and ask for
a larger share, up to the whole quasi-rent, and the investor's only alternative is to lose everything the specific asset
is worth here, so it yields. No one is deceived and no promise is broken: the squeeze presses only what the incomplete
contract left unnamed, a contingency it could not have written, so the counterparty threatens nothing before the
investment and breaks nothing after. What disciplined the terms was competition, the other buyers and suppliers whose
standing offers set what a fair deal was, and the sinking of the specific asset is exactly what empties that competition,
since no rival can bid on a plant built for one factory; Williamson names this the fundamental transformation, a deal
that began with many bidders becoming, the moment the asset is specific, a bargain of one against one. The harm is not
the transfer but what the transfer forecloses: the investor, foreseeing the squeeze as clearly as the counterparty does,
builds general rather than specific, or invests less, or not at all, and the most valuable version of the deal is never
made, the loss invisible because the thing that would have caused it was never built (Grossman and Hart). The fixes all
buy back the security the specificity spent, bringing both sides under one ownership so there is no separate party left
to hold up, writing longer contracts, or posting hostages, and each is costly, trading the market's sharp incentives for
the firm's dulled ones or paying to bind a promise nothing else would hold. It is named clear of Moral Hazard (a hidden
action taken because the actor does not bear its full cost, whereas here every action is open and the exposure is a sunk
asset, not a concealed choice), The Market for Lemons (a hidden quality that unravels a market before any deal, whereas
here nothing is concealed and the trap springs after the investment, not before it), and Good Money After Bad (a sunk
cost driving further investment into a failing course, the opposite direction, whereas here the sunk asset drives
