holds at least three different Johnsons under one bare key, Wendell Johnson in The Abstraction Ladder, the
Johnson of Balogun and Johnson in The Frozen Middle, and the Johnson of Cuellar, Keil and Johnson in Watermelon
Reporting, with a fourth arriving if the debris candidate is authored. Undeclared surnames collate into one key
and can only raise a spurious overlap, which fails loudly, so this is the safe direction and nothing is at risk.
Declaring it would cost two incumbent cell fixes, since two of the three name no given name.

Struck at two hundred and fifty nine, and both of the findings recorded when the flag was tested held up in the
build rather than only on paper.

The corrected cut carried the play, and the failed one would not have. Written to irreversibility, the warrant
would have been arguing that a known mechanism is worse here, which reads as a bigger instance of Held Under
and would have been right to fail a reader. Written to the loop having no behaviour in it, the distinction does
work in three directions at once, against Held Under whose clients can back off, against The Commons whose
users can govern themselves, and against Onto the Survivors where a fixed load is redistributed rather than a
population grown. One vector, three neighbours, which is what a load-bearing cut looks like.

And the ordering inversion was confirmed in practice rather than by reasoning. The declaration landed first and
the citation of Donald Kessler resolved on arrival, giving three resolved Kesslers and zero unresolved. Had the
misfit gone first it would have failed the namesake wall the day it landed, which is what the register
predicted from reading the check rather than from watching it happen. The rule stands as written: cells first
where the surname is undeclared, declaration first where it is already declared, and the question that decides
which is whether `scholarPolicy.homonyms` already holds the name.

What is left on the slate is two P2s, both held: Shifting Baseline Syndrome on the Prevalence-Induced Concept
Change adjacency, and the lockup cliff on its anticipation cut. The same treatment applies, and the Kessler
result is the reason to apply it properly: test the cut, and expect the test to correct it rather than confirm
it.

Struck at two hundred and sixty, and the flag test corrected the cut for the third time running, which stops
being a coincidence and starts being what the procedure is for.

It corrected it twice on this candidate. First by **narrowing**: the literature splits generational amnesia,
where knowledge of the earlier state does not pass between cohorts, from personal amnesia, where an
individual's own sense of the past updates. The second is a within-observer adaptation and is
Prevalence-Induced Concept Change's, so the play stages the first and declines the second outright. That is the
same move Kept From Burning made in refusing the wildland-urban edge, and it is now twice that refusing a real
vector because a neighbour owns it is what made a mandated line true rather than asserted. Worth naming as a
technique: **where a candidate's literature contains two mechanisms and the house holds one, the play takes the
other and says so.**

Second by **finding a neighbour the line never named**. The flag pointed at Prevalence-Induced Concept Change
and the distinction there held, on the dynamics of the comparison class, continuous there and frozen at first
contact here. The one nobody anticipated was Nobody Stopped Speaking It: both turn on something that passes by
first-hand acquisition and is not replaced by a description of it, which is a shape the house had already
staged this month. The line is that there transmission is an act somebody performs and a parent chooses, while
here there is no act to omit, since first-hand experience of a state that no longer exists cannot be handed
over at any price, and the casualties differ, the good itself there against the ability to register its loss
here.

That second correction is worth more than the first, because it points at a failure mode in triage rather than
in this candidate. **A one-paragraph triage compares a candidate against the misfits it reminds the triager
of.** The neighbour it missed was three weeks old and shares no field, no literature and no vocabulary with
fisheries science: nothing about a language shift resembles a stock assessment until the mechanisms are laid
side by side, and mechanisms are what authoring compares. The three flag tests have now missed a neighbour
each, and in every case the missed one was the sharper.

The namesake decision rule ran in the normal direction here and completes its pair. Pauly was undeclared, so
cells first in the misfit lane and declaration second, which is this one, twenty three declared surnames and
zero unresolved. Kessler was already declared and inverted. One question decides it and both answers have now
been exercised.

What is left on the slate is one line: the [P2] lockup cliff, still held on its anticipation cut and never
tested. Everything else slated since the two hundred and fifty reconcile has been staged.

Struck at two hundred and sixty one, and the slate emptied. The line had been on it longer than any other and
carried its own kill switch: stage it only on the anticipation puzzle, or it is All at Once with a calendar and
is not staged. The kill switch is what made the test cheap, because it named the incumbent to beat and the
beating had to be mechanical rather than tonal. All at Once runs on **simultaneity**, and the proof of that is
that jittering a time-to-live dissolves it. Lockups are now released in tranches, and each tranche runs the
lockup mechanism entire, because a tranche's shares cannot be borrowed before the tranche exists. A remedy that
kills one and leaves the other running is a cleaner separation than any amount of prose about how the two feel
different.

**A slate line that names the incumbent it must beat is worth more than one that names the concept.** Every
other line on this register describes a candidate; this one described a test, and the test could be executed in
one paragraph rather than argued. Whoever writes the next batch of lines should write the incumbent into them.

And the fourth flag test missed its neighbour, in the same direction as the other three. The line pointed at
All at Once and the distinction held. The one nobody anticipated was **Fair Warning**, which is the candidate's
exact inverse on the same variable: there an announced future date reaches the present and does harm by
reaching it, so earlier and more credible is worse, while here the announcement is at least as early and at
least as credible and reaches nothing, because the party who would act on it holds no asset to act with until
the date arrives. Four for four, and in every case the missed one was the sharper. The triage failure named at
two hundred and sixty holds: triage compares concepts, and authoring compares mechanisms.

The namesake pass found the largest collision yet. **Miller** carried five different people: Wendi in Don't
Look, Dale in Moral Licensing, Geoffrey in Only the Close Ones, Franklin in Treated and Counted, and Edward M.
arriving with the new misfit. Geoffrey had been resolved incidentally when Kessler was declared, since the two
share a cell, which is worth noticing on its own: **resolving one surname resolves whichever others sit beside
it**, so the cells-first pass is cheaper than the count of unresolved occurrences suggests. Three cells, one
misfit-lane pull request, then this declaration: twenty four declared surnames, zero unresolved. Six surnames
have now been declared in three days and every one of them was already carrying two or more people.

**The third sweep, and what it returned.** Run at two hundred and sixty one with the slate at zero. The method is the one the previous two used: read the
field distribution across every warrant, then look past every field it covers rather than deeper into the
covered ones. The distribution stands at 63 measurement and cognition, 54 markets, 37 safety and risk, 29
organisations, 28 collective action, 26 ecology and public health, 19 groups and candour, 5 Senge archetypes.
The fields with no misfit in them at all are education, forecasting, geology, library science, food systems,
sport, audit and art conservation. Three candidates survive, two are held with their cut written, and the
strikes are recorded so nobody re-derives them.

- [x] [P0] The Forecaster's Dilemma (Lerch, Thorarinsdottir, Ravazzolo & Gneiting, "Forecaster's Dilemma: Extreme Events and Forecast Evaluation", Statistical Science, 2017; Denrell & Fang, "Predicting the Next Big Thing: Success as a Signal of Poor Judgment", Management Science, 2010; Gneiting & Raftery, 2007): a proper scoring rule is proper only over all cases, and conditioning the evaluation on the realised outcome, which is the only review the public ever performs and is the right thing for it to care about, is incompatible with the assumptions that make the rule proper, so the strategy that wins the conditional evaluation is to forecast extremes more often than one believes them and the calibrated forecaster is discredited by construction, worst where the signal-to-noise ratio is lowest and forecasts matter most; to be cut hard to the propriety result and never to a complaint that the press is unfair, with the costly remedy already in the literature, proper weighted scoring rules requiring the evaluator to fix a weight function in advance and to be the sort of party who reads a weighted score; named clear of Metric Fixation, where a target is gamed by the agent and here nothing is gamed and the evaluator's own selection is the mechanism, of The False Trade-Off, whose collider opens a spurious association where the object here is a scoring rule's incentive property, and of The Firefighter's Medal, where the non-event is invisible and here the ordinary case is perfectly visible and simply not looked at; Denrell is already carried three works deep by Once Bitten, which is the expected direction and fires nothing, but the fourth work should be added knowingly, staged as **Right About the Big One**: the flags held all the way through, the first candidate of the run whose pre-authoring notes survived contact with the drafting unamended, and what they did not name was Clear From Here, which is the same room after the same storm and turned out to be the sharpest neighbour in the file; the reversal that decided it is that a perfectly unbiased evaluator, with no hindsight and no error in the sums, produces the same ranking, so the defect is arithmetic and survives the removal of every psychological cause
- [x] [P1] Nutrient Dilution Under Elevated CO2 (Loladze, "Hidden shift of the ionome of plants exposed to elevated CO2", eLife, 2014; Myers et al., Nature, 2014; Chunwu Zhu et al., Science Advances, 2018): the quantity rises, the concentration per unit falls, and the instrument is denominated in the quantity, so nobody chooses the carbon dioxide, the breeder selects for yield because yield is what feeds people, and food security is counted in calories because famine kills by calories, and every measure reports improvement while what a calorie carries declines; to be cut to that denominator vector and named clear of Best Day for It, whose value factor falls as quantity rises in the same shape from an entirely different domain and literature, and of Metric Fixation, which needs a gamer; flagged twice, first that no live scientific dispute was confirmed at the sweep, only heterogeneity by crop, by nutrient and by experiment type, so the bound must be established at authoring rather than asserted, and second that Chunwu Zhu makes a third Zhu and the surname must be declared in the same pass, staged as **A Bigger Harvest Every Year**: the bound the line demanded was established rather than asserted and it is real, since much of the early evidence came from chambers and glasshouses with many individually non-significant results and research groups challenged whether it transferred to the field, which is why the play rests on the meta-analytic and open-air evidence, stages a direction and never a figure, and stages no health projection at all; Chunwu Zhu was left out, so the Zhu declaration is still owed and the pair is still undeclared
- [x] [P1] The Lucas Critique (Lucas, "Econometric Policy Evaluation: A Critique", 1976): a relation estimated under one policy regime embeds that regime's expectations, so using it to choose a new policy invalidates it, with no agent gaming anything and every agent behaving optimally and honestly; this is the authoring table's fourth case rather than a free slate, a distinct mechanism the incumbent has annexed, since Goodhart is a measure corrupted by pressure on an agent and Lucas is a model invalidated by its own use, so **Metric Fixation must be narrowed before this is built** and that cost is on the line rather than discovered at authoring, staged as **True Until It Was Used**, and the narrowing the line demanded was done in the same pull request, since a misfit may touch the incumbent it collides with and both are misfit lane: Metric Fixation now states in its own warrant that its mechanism needs an agent with an interest in the number and a way to move it, so there is pressure to relieve and a proxy to change, which is narrower than Goodhart's sentence is usually stretched to cover; what the build added is that nobody games anything here, the household revising because the question it answered changed and would revise identically in a world containing no economists, and that the record could not have warned anybody, since a sample under one standing arrangement contains no variation in the arrangement and so no coefficient to estimate on it; the dissent is real and is carried, Ericsson and Irons having surveyed the literature citing Lucas and found virtually no evidence substantiating the critique, which is why the play stages the structure and never a magnitude

Held, each with the cut that would bring it back. **Desirable difficulties and the fluency trap** (Bjork &
Bjork; Kornell & Bjork), where the metacognitive cue steering study is inverted, is held against I Know How It
Works until it is cut hard to the inversion itself, that the cue is negatively coupled to retention **by
construction** because the difficulty producing retention is the same difficulty lowering fluency; as stated it
reads as the illusion of explanatory depth applied to study methods, and it brushes The Skill to See It and The
Scaffold Left Up as well, and three close incumbents is one too many for a slate. **The Paradox of Enrichment**
(Rosenzweig, 1971; May, 1972), where raising the resource base destabilises the system it improves, is held
against More Mouths Than Meat's lag and Room to Grow's overcompensation: the same model family is not the same
mechanism, the oscillation coming from delay there and from raised carrying capacity here, but the cut must be
written before the slate, and the field's own puzzle, that the paradox is seldom observed, is the bound the
play would have to carry.

Struck before slating, the sweep's dedup catching each as already staged: the Matthew effect in reading is The
Head Start; teaching to the test is Metric Fixation; the cover jinx is The Bounce; mark-to-market
procyclicality is The Weather We Make's endogenous-risk feedback; issuer-pays audit independence is Good Enough
to Hold; digital format obsolescence is already held on this register as too fine to stage; the winner's curse
in genome-wide association studies is The Winning Bid with The Best on Paper; diagnostic momentum is Already
Explained; and the tornado false-alarm ratio is Alarm Fatigue. Induced seismicity was examined and dropped for
want of a clean no-villain cut rather than for an incumbent.

And a namesake recorded rather than acted on, in the safe direction. **Zhu** carries two people already,
Timothy Zhu in Held Under's metastable-failures paper and Meng Zhu in The Tyranny of the Urgent, both bare and
undeclared, which can only raise a spurious overlap and so fails loudly rather than passing quietly. It joins
the Johnson note. The nutrient-dilution line above would make it three, and its line says so, which is the
whole point of writing a namesake into the slate rather than leaving it for the pull request.

**A brief triaged at two hundred and sixty three, on the ontogenetic decline of divergent thinking.** The
brief arrived anchored on Land and Jarman's NASA figures, popularised by Ken Robinson, and the triage went the
other way from the usual one: the anchor is the weakest element in it and the mechanism worth staging is an
aside three sections down. Worth naming, because a triage that reads a brief top-down meets the popular claim
first and the structural trap last: **the strongest thing in a research brief is rarely its anchor.**

- [x] [P0] The Reliability Constraint on Assessment (Guilford, "Creativity", American Psychologist, 1950; Messick on validity; the constructed-response rater-variation literature and the NAEP scoring procedures; the Torrance scoring manuals): a score must be reproducible by a different marker to be defensible, because reproducibility is what makes it appealable, auditable and fair to the candidate with no advocate, and originality is precisely the property on which competent markers do not agree, since a response two markers scored identically original would by that fact be conventional; so the instrument that removes favouritism is the instrument that cannot reward the divergent, and the way divergent thinking was made markable at all was to replace "is this original" with "how rare is this answer in the reference sample", which moves the standard out of the response and into a corpus; the psychometric literature states the bind in its own voice, that constructed response is favoured for ecological validity and is restricted in standardised testing because of rater variation; to be cut hard to that definitional bind, that the measured property is defined by the disagreement disqualifying it from measurement, and never to a complaint that schools kill creativity, with the costly remedy carried at its price, portfolio and rubric assessment being real, in use, and reintroducing exactly the marker variance fairness was built to remove, at a rater-training cost falling hardest where budgets are thinnest; named clear of Metric Fixation, where a target is gamed and here nothing is gamed and the binding constraint is reliability rather than pressure, of The Straight Rows, where the simplification serves administration and what is lost is metis while here it serves fairness to the assessed individual and what is lost is a mode of that individual's own cognition, and of The Youngest in the Room, which stages the same institutional fact of age batching on an entirely different mechanism; flagged that Kyung Hee Kim would be a second Kim beside the house's Daniel H. Kim and must be declared in the same pass if cited, staged as **Marked the Same by Anyone**: the cut the line demanded held, and the spine turned out to be older and harder than the line anticipated, since Spearman's 1904 correction for attenuation makes consistency a **ceiling** on validity rather than a virtue traded against it, which removes the move every argument about assessment rests on; the sharpest thing the build added was that the valuable judgements and the reliability-destroying ones are the same set and not merely correlated, because a script two competent markers score identically without hesitating did not need either of them, so an instrument cannot suppress its inconsistency without suppressing its expertise; the nearest neighbour is Right About the Big One, three days old, both staging a scoring instrument whose guarantee carries a condition nobody carries with it, there the sample breaking a good rule and here the requirement of agreement binding before a single script is scored; and the Kim flag was not needed, since the play rests on Spearman, Guilford, Torrance and Messick and never on the decline literature at all
- [x] [P1] Certification Requires Individuation (the academic-integrity literature; Ringelmann and the social-loafing work, cited to hold the line): a certificate vouches for a person, so the work behind it must be attributable to that person alone, which makes one act collaboration in every workplace and cheating in every examination hall; an institution preparing people for distributed work must forbid distributed work at exactly the moment it measures them, and the forbidding is correct rather than perverse, since an unattributable result certifies nobody and the certificate is what the candidate came for; to be cut to the attribution requirement and never to a complaint about examination culture, and named clear of Social Loafing, which is the **reason** individuation is needed rather than a sibling of what it costs, so the pair is sharp rather than overlapping: one stages why individual contribution must be observable and the other what making it observable costs, staged as **One Name on the Paper**, with the concept renamed at authoring to Certification and the Attribution Requirement once the cut settled; the no-hero came back documented rather than argued, since individual weighting factors from peer rating are the standard remedy and the member who contributed least saw least of what anybody else did, so the informant is thinnest exactly where the splitting has to happen; Social Loafing is named as the premise rather than as a sibling, one saying why individual contribution must be made visible and this saying what making it visible removes

Held, with the cut that would bring it back. **The concurrent evaluative filter**, where generation and
evaluation run together so novel associations are pruned before they consolidate, drawn from the dual-network
work on default-mode and executive-control coupling and from the cognitive-inhibition literature, is held
against The Good Move's primed solution blocking the search and The Scaffold Left Up's guidance that helps a
novice and hinders an expert. What holds it is not an incumbent but an unestablished step: that schooling
**trains** the simultaneity is a hypothesis in that literature rather than a finding, and a misfit resting on
it would be staging a mechanism nobody has shown. It returns if the training step is demonstrated.

Struck before slating, and one of them for a reason this register has not had to use before. **The Land and
Jarman figures are struck as unusable rather than as already staged**: there is no published study, no NASA
record of one, no reference given anywhere, and the commonly quoted percentages do not match the single
paragraph on page 153 of the trade book they are attributed to. A house that writes credit-not-claim and holds
warrants five and six sources deep cannot anchor on a number whose provenance is a citation loop, and the fact
that it is among the most repeated statistics in education is the reason to write the strike down rather than
a reason to soften it. **The framing is struck too**: "schooling kills creativity" names a culprit and a
victim, which is the one thing the house forbids, so it is not a candidate at any tier however well it plays.
The rest were struck as already staged: the overjustification half is Paid to Stop Caring and A Reason Too
Many, twice over; the age-batching half is The Youngest in the Room. And **Kim (2011) is struck as a spine and
kept as a row**: it is real, peer-reviewed and large, nearly three hundred thousand tests between 1968 and
2008, and it reports a decline across **birth cohorts**, which is a secular trend, where the brief reads it as
an ontogenetic decline within individuals; it is contested on its empirical decisions and statistics besides.
Two different claims wearing one sentence is the failure mode to watch for in any brief that mixes a
longitudinal design with a cross-sectional one.

**A brief triaged at two hundred and sixty four, on male flight from higher education.** The second brief in
a row whose headline framing has to be struck and whose usable material survives underneath it, which is now
a pattern worth expecting rather than a coincidence. The outcome here is not a new play at all, and that is
the finding: **three of the four moves in the authoring table are not "build one", and this brief lands on the
third.**

The tipping half **is The Sort**, in a new domain. The brief reaches for white flight as its analogy, and
white flight is the phenomenon Schelling's model was written about, so mild individual preferences over
composition producing complete sorting is not a mechanism the house lacks: it is the incumbent, and the
occupational case is the same engine on a different substrate. So the move is the authoring table's third
row, extend the warrant with an Origin row and cast nothing.

- [x] [P1] Extend **The Sort**'s warrant with Anne E. Lincoln, "The Shifting Supply of Men and Women to Occupations: Feminization in Veterinary Education" (Social Forces, 2010), which takes the tipping model out of neighbourhoods and into occupational choice on the veterinary applicant pool of 1975 to 1995; a misfit-lane change to `misfits/the_sort/REFERENCE.md` and no cast, since a mechanism shown in two domains is worth more to the incumbent than a second play would be worth to the house; **cite her three factors and not one**, since the brief compresses her to "the number of women in the classroom" and her paper gives the 1972 federal amendment ending discrimination against female students, the deterrence of male applicants, and the rising supply of women earning bachelor's degrees, of which the third is a supply-side fact that would produce much of the pattern with no preference over composition at all, so citing her while dropping two thirds of her finding is the move the brief accuses others of making; done, and a second row went with it, the profession's own veterinary workforce forecasts onto **Chasing the Price**, whose evidence had been Hanau's hog cycle and four theory papers and now carries a professional labour market and a published forecast that was correct, ignored, and fifteen years ahead

Struck. **The framing is struck, for the reason the divergent-thinking brief's was**: "avoid the feminine"
names a disposition, a culprit and a victim, and a play built on it would be describing a wrong rather than a
structure. That is a rule about what the house can stage and not a verdict on whether the claim is true, and
the distinction is worth keeping crisp, since the strike would be identical if the claim were established
beyond argument.

**The devaluation half is struck as a spine, and the first version of this entry was wrong about why.**
Levanon, England and Allison, "Occupational Feminization and Pay" (Social Forces, 2009), run fixed-effects
models on 1950 to 2000 census data and find substantial evidence for devaluation, that feminisation lowers
pay, and only scant evidence for the queuing view that low pay draws women in. That was written up here as a
scepticism that did not survive contact. It should have been written up as a contest with a competitor
missing.

**There is a third theory with the same observational signature and it was not in that contest.** Bergmann's
occupational crowding (1974) says that an increase in the supply of labour into a field lowers its wages, and
under it the female share is a marker of who the entrants were rather than a cause of anything. Devaluation
against queuing is a test between two gendered stories; crowding is not gendered at all, and a design that
separates share from headcount is what would settle it. Whether the paper controls for occupational size could
not be verified from here, since both it and the working-paper literature around it are unreachable behind
this environment's egress proxy, so the register records the question rather than an answer.

**And in the veterinary case the two are the same event.** The 1972 federal amendment removed a legal barrier,
which is by construction an outward shift in labour supply, and Lincoln's own third factor is the rising number
of women earning bachelor's degrees. Nothing separates "more women" from "more entrants" in that period without
a design built to do it. Stronger still, **the profession forecast the decline from supply before the
feminisation completed**: a workforce analysis from the middle of the 1980s, reported in the profession's own
later retrospective of its workforce studies, projected demand for small-animal and large-animal services
growing by roughly a third and a quarter across 1980 to 2000 against supply growing by about three quarters,
and predicted private practitioners' real incomes falling to around three quarters of the 1980 level. No
gender appears in that forecast. The primary was not reachable from here and the figures are carried at the
strength of a secondary report.

**Mandel (2018, Demography) makes the finding less stable than the first version of this entry allowed**, too:
re-examining 1960 to 2015, the negative association declines to insignificance by 2015 and reappears only once
education is controlled. So "stronger than expected" was too strong, and the strike stands on firmer ground
than the one it was first given. It is still not a misfit: the mechanism
needs an evaluator whose valuation responds to who does the work, which is a bias with an agent however
diffuse it is, and the nearest thing the house holds is The Proxy. A play here would need a villain or would
have to pretend it did not have one.

Struck as already staged, the rest of the brief's machinery: herding on an observed choice is Information
Cascade; the coordination problem underneath is The Stag Hunt; the credential as a sorting signal is Too Dear
to Fake; and the compounding of an early advantage is The Head Start.

**And the supply reading relocates the whole brief onto incumbents rather than opening a gap, which is why the
correction strengthens the strike instead of reversing it.** Read as supply, the veterinary case is Chasing
the Price: a training pipeline answering a shortage that had gone by the time it delivered, forecast a decade
and a half ahead by the profession's own analysts and arriving anyway, which is the cobweb with a workforce
study attached. It sits directly beside Ready on the First Day, staged the same week on the pipeline being
longer than the period of the thing it aims at, and the vet case would make a fair Origin row for one of the
two if either is extended. Read as composition it is The Sort. Read as bias it is The Proxy. Three readings,
three incumbents, and no gap anywhere in it.

A triage move worth keeping, since it is what produced the correction. The thing that broke the devaluation
reading was not a paper: it was one person's memory of two vets in one town, and of the same town twenty years
later with the same animals in it and many more vets. **A local observation that holds the denominator fixed
discriminates better than a summary of a study that does not**, because same town and roughly the same animals
is a controlled demand side by construction, which is precisely the control the published contest was missing.
So the move is to ask what stayed the same, and it is cheap enough to ask of every candidate.

Held, with the cut written, and it is the only un-staged shape in the brief. **The recruitment that broadcasts
the composition**: an institution watching one group's enrolment fall has one lever, which is to recruit that
group, and any visible attempt to pull it publishes the composition that is deterring them, so the remedy
announces the condition it is meant to cure. The shape is very close to The Fix Tells Them Where, where the
repair is identical to a description of the flaw, with different objects. It is held because **no literature
was found for it**, and a misfit resting on a shape the author noticed rather than on a body of work is the
thing the Land and Jarman strike was written about a day earlier. It returns if the recruitment-signal bind is
demonstrated somewhere.

And a note on the surname scan, which is now run before every candidate. `Lincoln` and `England` both returned
hits in the index and **neither is a scholar**: the first is the Lincoln Institute and the second the New England
Journal of Medicine, and both sit in a **Key Work** cell rather than a Source cell, which is the detail worth
having: the scan reads the whole row, so a publisher or a journal named after a person or a place looks
exactly like a scholar even though the science build would never key it as one. It costs one look
each and it is worth writing down, because a scan whose hits are institutions trains the reader to skim it.

**A discipline note, written against myself.** Those three lines sat struck-in-fact and unstruck-on-the-page
for a day, while other work was chosen off the same slate. The rule in `CLAUDE.md` is one line long, take the
next misfit from the top and strike what you stage, and the half that gets skipped is always the second half,
because striking is the only step in the sequence that produces nothing. **A register that lags is worse than
no register**, since the next author trusts it: an unstruck line is not a neutral omission but a live
instruction to build something that already exists, and it is exactly the failure the concordance had when it
fell thirty seven misfits behind and the reason both indexes are generated now. The register cannot be
generated, because a discard and its reason are judgements, so it is held by habit instead, and the habit
needs the strike to happen in the pull request that stages the thing rather than in the next one.

**A correction to the discipline note above, and the lane rule is the reason.** That note said the strike must
happen in the pull request that stages the thing. **It cannot.** A misfit rides `misfit/<topic>` and this
register is `management/**`, which is governance, so the guard refuses the two together and is right to: the
whole point of the lane is that content and its governance move separately. The only exception the house
already carries is a management **order** under `management/orders/**`, which is a rider and folds onto the
lane of the change it drives, and this file is not an order.

So the workable habit is the next-best one: **open the governance pull request alongside the misfit rather
than after it**, in the same sitting, so the strike is written while the reason for it is still in front of
whoever is writing. That is what this one does, and it carries the Spearman declaration with it because the
same misfit forced that too. A prescription that the gates forbid is worse than none, since it reads as
permission to skip the step when the guard refuses it, and the note as first written would have taught exactly
that.

The declaration itself ran in the normal direction and completes quickly. **Spearman** is Mark in No Idle
Hands, from Hopp and Spearman's Factory Physics, and Charles arriving with the attenuation bound. The
incumbent cell was fixed in the misfit lane in the same pull request that added the new citation, which is the
cheapest the cells-first order ever gets: one pass, no separate pull request, because the misfit that creates
the collision is also allowed to touch the incumbent it collides with. Twenty five declared surnames, zero
unresolved.

**The strike lagged a third time, so it stops being a prescription and becomes a check.** Twice now this
register has answered a lagging strike by telling the next author to try harder, and the second of those had
to be corrected because it prescribed something the lane rule forbids. A third note would be the tell that the
house has not worked out how to compute something it keeps writing sentences about.

The register cannot be generated, since a discard and its reason are judgements and no inversion of a warrant
supplies them. **One part of it can be checked.** A slate line opens with its candidate's concept name and a
staged misfit declares its concept in frontmatter, so an open line naming a concept the house already holds is
a line that should have been struck, and `node tests/science_overlap.mjs --slate` says so. It is a wall rather
than an advisory, because a concept in the house makes the line stale with no reading required.

**It catches about half, and the half it misses is the more interesting one.** The line is written before the
misfit exists, and the concept is often renamed during authoring once the cut settles: the Lucas line kept its
name and was caught by the check as it was written, and Certification Requires Individuation became
Certification and the Attribution Requirement and was not. No key fixes that, because the drift is the author
changing their mind about what the thing is, which is the whole of what authoring does. So the check has
recall of a half and **no false positives at all**, which is the trade the house has taken before: the shape
of a wall is decided by which error it must never make, and this one must never tell an author to strike a
line that is still live.

Also worth writing down, since the pattern is now three for three: the lag is not carelessness in any
particular sitting. Striking is the only step in the sequence that produces nothing, it happens in a different
lane from the work that earns it, and it competes with the next candidate at exactly the moment the next
candidate is most interesting. Any one of those is survivable and the three together are not, which is why
this needed a gate rather than a resolution.

**The fourth sweep, run at two hundred and sixty seven with the slate at zero.** Same method: read the field
distribution across every warrant, then look past every field it covers rather than deeper into the covered
ones. The distribution stands at 65 measurement and cognition, 55 markets, 37 safety and risk, 30
organisations, 28 collective action, 27 ecology and public health, 20 groups and candour, 5 Senge archetypes.
The third sweep listed eight fields with no misfit in them at all; three of those have since been staged, and
the ones still empty are geology, library science, sport, audit and art conservation. Three candidates survive,
one is held, and the strikes are recorded.

- [x] [P0] The Hirshleifer Effect in Insurance (Hirshleifer, "The Private and Social Value of Information and the Reward to Inventive Activity", American Economic Review, 1971; Arrow, 1963, which the house already carries as canon in Moral Hazard and may therefore be cited): insurance is the sale of a pooled ignorance, so a risk everybody can measure precisely is a risk nobody needs to pool, and each improvement in the precision of measurement removes some of what there was to insure; the party who wanted the test wanted it for good reasons, the insurer who prices on it is doing its job, and the casualty is the person whose risk is now known and high, who is uninsurable for a fact about themselves that no conduct of theirs produced; to be cut hard to the **symmetric** case, where the information is available to both sides and the pool dissolves anyway, and never to a story about an insurer hiding behind a test; **named clear of The Market for Lemons**, which is the nearest incumbent and stages the asymmetric case, the seller knowing what the buyer does not, and which carries Rothschild and Stiglitz's spine so that work is unavailable here, a constraint that sharpens the boundary rather than weakening the candidate; the costly remedy is a legal bar on using the information, which works, is in force in several countries, and reintroduces the asymmetry the incumbent stages, so the two misfits are the two halves of one bind and should say so; flagged that the axis must not be `risk-pool-members`, which Onto the Survivors declares with a positive sign, since the quantity here is the precision of the measurement rather than the size of the pool
- [x] [P1] The Two Requirements on Civil Time (the leap second; the ITU debate and the 2022 decision to abandon leap seconds by 2035; the smear as practised): civil time must track the rotation of the Earth, which is irregular and slowing unevenly, and must also be a uniform count with no discontinuities in it, because that is what every computer and every interval measurement needs; both requirements are correct, they are incompatible in principle, and one quantity has to satisfy both; to be cut to that incompatibility and never to a complaint about standards bodies, with the remedies carried at their real prices, since the leap second breaks software that assumed monotonic time, abandoning it lets civil noon drift from the sun forever, and smearing the second across a day makes a second briefly not a second; named clear of Exactly One Kilogram, where the object defining a unit cannot by construction be wrong so the drift is undetectable from inside, while here nothing drifts undetected and the difficulty is that two requirements are both known and both binding, and of Both Take the Crown, whose CAP result forces a choice among three properties during a partition, where this is two properties in permanent conflict with no partition and no event to trigger it
- [x] [P1] Archival Appraisal (Schellenberg, Modern Archives, 1956; Jenkinson; the appraisal-theory literature after Cook and Booms): an archivist must select, because keeping everything makes nothing findable and cataloguing is the binding cost rather than storage, and the criterion available is a description of what present researchers ask, applied to a record every future question will have to be asked of; the selection is irreversible, and the error is structurally invisible, since nobody ever learns what the discarded box would have answered; to be cut to the deliberate criterion and its invisible counterfactual, and named clear of The Survivors, whose sample is thinned by **passive** attrition nobody chose, where here somebody chooses, by stated criteria, in good faith, and the record is better for it in every way anybody can check
- [x] [P0] There Is No Neutral Default (Thaler and Sunstein, _Nudge_, 2008; Johnson and Goldstein, "Do Defaults Save Lives?", Science, 2003; the choice-architecture critique literature): somebody must set the initial state of every form, toggle and screen, and there is no null setting, because presenting no default is itself an architecture and the order the options are listed in moves the outcome, so the seat cannot abstain from steering and every result is partly authored by a decision that had to be taken by somebody with no way to take it neutrally; to be cut to the **unavailability of the null option** and never to a story about a designer nudging anybody, with the remedies at their prices, since active choosing raises the abandonment rate and is itself a design, randomising the default distributes the steering rather than removing it, and a default set by measured preference is set by the preferences of whoever was measured; named clear of An Angle and a Count, where two requirements conflict and a seat must break one, since here there is no conflict between requirements and the difficulty is that no position on the question is neutral; **Johnson is a namesake owed**, the house holding the orbital-debris Johnson in Nobody Has to Launch Again against Eric J here, while Thaler and Sunstein are the same people the house already carries on other work and owe nothing
- [x] [P0] The Observer's Paradox (Labov, _Sociolinguistic Patterns_, 1972; the vernacular-elicitation methods; the Hawthorne dispute after Levitt and List): the data wanted is behaviour as it is when unobserved, and the only way to obtain it is to observe, so the act of measuring is the act that removes the quantity being measured; nobody is deceived and nobody games anything, which is the whole of the boundary with Metric Fixation, since the speaker is not responding to a measurement but to a person being present; to be cut to the **method consuming its own object** and never to a complaint about researchers, with the remedies carried honestly, since concealed recording obtains the data and is unethical and in most places unlawful, long acquaintance reduces the effect without removing it and buys the reduction with the observer becoming part of the setting, and the emotionally engaging question works by making the observation matter less rather than by removing it; named clear of The Same Print Twice, where the observer is contaminated by context and the repair is to withhold it, since here the observer is unbiased and the observed is what moves; the Hawthorne effect to be carried as a **contested** illustration and never as the spine, the re-analyses having found little in the illumination data, and the house already citing the bank wiring room in Sandbag, which is a different work
- [x] [P0] The Control Right and the Concentrated Exposure (Hansmann, _The Ownership of Enterprise_, 1996; Blair and Stout, "A Team Production Theory of Corporate Law", Virginia Law Review, 1999; Jensen, "Value Maximization, Stakeholder Theory, and the Corporate Objective Function", 2001, as the no-hero): corporate law gives control to shareholders on the argument that they bear the residual risk and therefore hold the sharpest incentive to maximise total value, and the diversified shareholder holds a fraction of a percent of this firm and similar fractions of hundreds of others, while the employee holds the whole of their firm-specific human capital in this one firm and the supplier who retooled holds a plant, so **the party that decides is the party structurally least exposed to the firm ceasing, and the parties with concentrated exposure have no vote**; to be cut to that inversion and **never to shareholders against stakeholders**, which names a culprit and a casualty and is the framing this candidate has to survive rather than adopt, since diversification is prudent and is what makes equity investable at all, specialising is what makes an employee valuable, and a board answerable to one claimant is what makes it answerable to anybody; the no-hero is unusually strong and must carry both halves, Jensen that a firm cannot maximise two things so a manager accountable to everybody is accountable to nobody, and Hansmann on why employee-owned firms under-invest and cannot be wound up when they should be, which together make the alternative to a single objective not a better objective but none; **named clear of Seed Corn**, and the test is already run and recorded, since removing that incumbent's engine leaves this standing: a private firm with no quarterly reporting and no near-term bonus still faces a five-year mandate that values the year-five price, and value appearing in year twelve is worth nothing to it **even if perfectly known**, so nothing is measured, nothing is rewarded and the divergence survives; also named clear of Metric Fixation and The Best on Paper; the axis must not be a horizon quantity, which is Seed Corn's family, since the quantity here is the **concentration of the deciding party's exposure** and not the length of anybody's view; Friedman is the same Milton the house already holds in Debt Deflation if the 1970 essay is cited, so nothing is owed there, though Milton and the Jerome of the statistical-learning citation are a latent undeclared pair of the cheap kind, two cells and both certain
- [x] [P1] Common Ownership (Azar, Schmalz and Tecu, "Anticompetitive Effects of Common Ownership", Journal of Finance, 2018; the contesting literature after Rock and Rubinfeld and O'Brien): one index fund holds every airline, so competing hard moves value between its own holdings rather than creating any, and the pressure to compete weakens without anybody instructing anybody; to be cut to the **absence of an instruction** and never to a story about a fund telling a firm what to do, since the index fund is a genuinely good product that made diversification cheap for ordinary savers, the managers may receive no signal at all and simply face a proprietor who is indifferent between them and their rival, and there is no meeting, no agreement and no communication anywhere in the mechanism; **named clear of anything cartel-shaped**, since a cartel requires an agreement and its remedy is to detect and punish one, where here there is nothing to detect; the empirics are **fiercely contested** and the dispute is the bound rather than a weakness, carried the way True Until It Was Used carries Ericsson and Irons, so the play stages the structure and never a magnitude
- [x] [P1] Second-Order Free-Riding (Oliver, 1980; Yamagishi, "The Provision of a Sanctioning System as a Public Good", 1986; Heckathorn on collective sanctions): a public good needs enforcement against defectors, enforcement costs the enforcer and benefits everybody equally, so enforcement is itself a public good and under-supplies for the same reason the first one did, and the sanction on the non-enforcer is a third public good with the same property; to be cut to the **recursion with no base case** and never to a tale of apathy, since declining to punish is exactly as rational as declining to contribute and the person who declines is the same person harmed by everybody else declining; the escape to be carried at its real price, since the recursion terminates only where enforcement is cheap enough to be worth one person's while or is itself rewarded, which is an institution rather than a solution and has to be paid for by the same group; named clear of The Vaccination Free-Rider, which stages the first order alone, and of Everybody's Business, where a duty is diffused across bystanders rather than declined by each on a sound calculation
- [x] [P2] The Optimiser Finds the Lever (the online controlled experiment literature, Kohavi et al; the dark-pattern taxonomies of Brignull and of Mathur et al, cited to name the observed output and not to carry the spine): a design is searched by continuous experiment against a metric that is **genuinely achieved**, so the search cannot distinguish a product that got better from a design that found a lever in the user's cognition, because both raise the number identically and neither is a degenerate result; nobody authors a manipulation and one is selected; **to be tested for annexation before any authoring**, since The Letter of the Law already stages proxy-and-search, and the distinguishing claim to be established or abandoned is that the found design is not degenerate and carries **no tell**, an ordinary interface a reasonable person would have drawn, where specification gaming produces a visibly absurd result: detectable-on-sight against undetectable-on-sight; if the test fails, the move is an Origin row on the incumbent and not a play; Narayanan is a namesake owed if Mathur et al is cited, the house holding the finance Narayanan in Seed Corn against Arvind here
- [x] [P0] Excavation Consumes Its Own Evidence (Lucas, _Critical Approaches to Fieldwork_; Renfrew and Bahn on method; the preservation-in-situ debate and the Valletta convention; rescue archaeology under development): a site can be read once, because reading it is taking it apart, and the discipline's technique improves monotonically while the site decays, is built over and is looted, so digging later is always better methodologically and worse materially and there is no moment at which both curves favour acting; to be cut to the **now-against-later trade** and never to a complaint about anybody's dig, since the excavator records more carefully than any predecessor and still destroys what they record; **named clear of Kept for the Questions We Had**, and the test is already run: remove that incumbent's engine, a criterion built from the present's questions, and this survives, because even an excavation with no criterion at all destroys, and the question here is **when** rather than **what**; the no-hero to carry preservation in situ, which is the discipline's own answer, is right, and means the knowledge is never obtained, and rescue digging, which is real archaeology performed to a bulldozer's timetable
- [x] [P1] The Instrument Calibrated Where It Is Least Needed (the palaeoclimate proxy literature; Briffa and the divergence problem; the calibration-period question generally): a proxy is calibrated against instruments over the only span in which both exist, and is then used over the span where no instrument does, so the validation covers precisely the period the reconstruction is not for, and whether the relation holds outside it cannot be checked by any means the method contains; to be cut to the **calibration window being the only window** and never to a charge about anybody's reconstruction, since the calibration is the only one available and using it is better than using nothing; **to be tested for annexation against True Until It Was Used before authoring**, since that misfit stages a relation estimated under one arrangement and invalidated by the use of it, and the distinguishing claim to be established or abandoned is that **nothing here is used to change anything**, the relation simply may not extend and no act invalidates it; if the test fails, the move is an Origin row and not a play; **Cook is a namesake owed** if Edward Cook the dendroclimatologist is cited, the house having declared Richard and Terry
- [x] [P1] Barred From the Test That Would Settle It (the sham-surgery literature; Moseley on arthroscopic knee surgery; the ethics of surgical placebo controls): a drug is tested against a placebo as a matter of course and a procedure often cannot be, because the control requires cutting somebody open to no purpose, so an entire class of intervention is held to a weaker standard of evidence than another for reasons that have nothing to do with how well any of it works; to be cut to the **evidentiary standard set by what is ethically testable rather than by what needs testing**, and never to a story about surgeons resisting scrutiny, since the ethical bar is right and the surgeon wants the answer too; named clear of Nothing Known Against It, where nobody has looked and looking would fix it, since here looking is **barred** and the bar is correct; and named clear of Not While You Are Watching, where the barred method is barred and the target is defined by the observer's absence, since here the target is ordinary efficacy and what distorts is the **comparison across classes** rather than the measurement of any one
- [x] [P2] The Type Specimen (the codes of zoological and botanical nomenclature; the holotype as the name-bearer; cases where a type turns out to be a hybrid, a juvenile or an aberrant individual): a species name is anchored to one physical object, and if that object is later shown to be unrepresentative the name stays attached to it anyway, because a name that moved when the biology moved would not be a name; to be cut to **stability chosen over accuracy, deliberately and with the reasons stated**, since the codes are the work of people who considered exactly this and decided correctly; **to be tested for annexation against Exactly One Kilogram before authoring**, and the distinguishing claim to be established or abandoned is that there the artefact cannot be wrong **by construction** and the drift is undetectable from inside, where here the specimen can be shown atypical, everybody can see it, and the name is held to it on purpose: an undetectable error against a visible one knowingly accepted; if the test fails, the move is an Origin row and not a play
- [x] [P0] The Comparative Method's Horizon (the comparative method as historical linguistics practises it; Swadesh and the rate of lexical replacement; Ringe on chance resemblance; Lyle Campbell as the dissent against long-range comparison): regular sound correspondences are the evidence that two languages share an ancestor, and they erode at a rate set by ordinary language change, so beyond roughly eight to ten thousand years genuine cognates become indistinguishable from chance resemblance and from borrowing; to be cut to **the thing that makes a family exist being the thing that erases the proof of it**, since divergence is what creates a family and the same divergence consumes the evidence of it, and never to a charge that anybody is being unrigorous, because the long-range comparativists are working where the instrument has no resolution rather than misreading a signal; the region beyond the horizon to be staged as one where the method returns **nothing either way** rather than one where anybody is wrong, which is what makes the claims there unfalsifiable in both directions; **named clear of A Hundred Years to Vouch for a Thousand**, where a fitted relation is extrapolated outside the window it was established in, since here nothing is fitted and nothing extrapolated and what fails is a signal falling below a noise floor, and of **Taken Apart to Be Read**, where the reading destroys, since here nobody destroys anything and time does it whether or not anybody looks; **Campbell is a namesake owed**, the house holding Donald T in Metric Fixation against Lyle here, one cell and both certain; Nichols would be a second and is avoidable, staged as **Erased by What Divided Them**: the cut the line demanded held, and the build sharpened it in one place the line had not reached, which is that the erosion has **two** engines and not one, since lexical replacement removes the words a correspondence could sit in and sound merger removes the distinctions the correspondence is made of, and only the first is what Swadesh measured; the symmetry turned out to be the load-bearing sentence rather than the noise floor, because a referee who writes that the evidence does not support the conclusion has written the only defensible thing and a field hears a refutation, so the misfit is not that the deep proposals are wrong but that neither they nor their denial can be recorded; the neighbours the line named held and a fourth was found in the writing, **Nothing Known Against It**, which is the same shape run one way where this is symmetric, and the house's other language misfit, **Nobody Stopped Speaking It**, shares neither a scholar nor a work; **Greenberg** was a surname hit and is not a namesake owed, the house holding Steven in On Good Authority and Joseph the linguist being avoidable on the merits, since the long-range proposals are cited as a class of claims rather than through his work; Campbell is owed and is the cheap kind, one cell and both certain; **and that count was wrong, which is the finding the pass produced**: the declaration turned up **five** further unresolved cells and **three** people, Donald T in Metric Fixation, More to Stand Still, The Bounce and The Cobra Effect, W M with Capen and Clapp in The Best on Paper and The Winning Bid, and Lyle here, so a namesake reported cheap on a pre-authoring scan can be seven cells deep; the reason it cannot be counted in advance is that an **undeclared surname collates**, so the index shows one scholar with several works, which is the expected case the house has already written down as owing nothing, and nothing in that output distinguishes one person with six works from three people with six between them, which means **the declaration is the instrument** rather than the last step, and the count of cells owed is only knowable after it; **and the cosmology pass completed the rule from the other side**, since it carried three surnames at once and they did not run the same way: Scott and Schwarz were undeclared and owed cells before the declaration, while White was **already declared** with three forms and owed the Martin form before the misfit, because a declared surname is under the wall now and an undeclared one is not, so the misfit citing Martin White cannot land until the form exists and the pre-authoring probe correctly reported **zero** cells owed on White, which is the one case where a count taken before the pass is trustworthy; the practical consequence is that a namesake pass spanning both lanes runs **cells first**, since a bare cell is valid without the declaration and the declaration is not valid without the cell, and `khai-guard advise` named the opposite order, correctly for lanes and wrongly for this, because it orders lanes and not dependencies
- [ ] [P1] The Sample of One Universe (cosmic variance; the largest angular scales of the microwave background; the one observable sky): for the largest structures there is exactly one realisation to look at, so the uncertainty on any measurement of them has a floor that no instrument, no exposure and no future technology can lower, because the limit is set by there being one thing rather than by how well anybody can see it; to be cut to **a floor set by the number of objects rather than by the quality of the instrument**, and never to a complaint about the state of cosmology, since the field named this itself, quantified it exactly and reports it as a separate term; **to be tested for annexation against Overfitting and against A Hundred Years to Vouch for a Thousand before authoring**, and the distinguishing claim to be established or abandoned is that nothing here is fitted, extrapolated or held back, the quantity being simply unrepeatable; if the test fails, the move is an Origin row and not a play
- [ ] [P2] The Only Instrument Is the Report (pain, nausea, fatigue and mood as measured in trials and in clinics; the response-shift literature; the incomparability of individual scales): the quantity that matters is private by definition, so the only access to it is a number the person says, and there is no external referent by which one person's scale could be compared with another's or with their own a year later; to be cut to **a measurement with no referent outside the reporter** and never to a suggestion that anybody is exaggerating, since the report is honest and is the best evidence that exists; **to be tested for annexation against Not While You Are Watching before authoring**, since that misfit stages a target reachable only by an access that alters it, where here the access does not alter the state but is a different kind of object from it; **and the claim to establish or abandon is that randomisation does not repair it**, since randomisation makes group means comparable in expectation and is a genuinely good answer, so the candidate lives or dies on whether the individual decision and the within-person change score survive that concession; if they do not, the move is an Origin row and not a play

Held, with the cut written. **Mandatory auditor rotation**, where the independence that makes an audit
trustworthy is bought by removing the client-specific knowledge that makes it competent, is held not against a
single incumbent but against a **shape the house has just taken three times**: Ready on the First Day, Marked
the Same by Anyone and One Name on the Paper all stage one property whose two effects cannot be separated. A
fourth on the same shape in one week would be a mould rather than a cut, which is what Cut to Fit forbids
whatever the domain. It returns when the house has moved on, or when somebody finds the vector that is not
one-property-two-effects.

Struck before slating, the sweep's dedup catching each as already staged: talent identification in sport is The
Proxy compounded by The Head Start, on the cutoff The Youngest in the Room already holds; the cadastral
monument that legally is the boundary even when it has moved is Exactly One Kilogram's object that cannot be
wrong; restoration that removes the evidence of an object's history is Held Still to Keep It; rare harms
findable only after approval at scale are Nothing Known Against It's absence of data read as clearance; and the
paleoseismic record shorter than the recurrence interval it estimates is As Full As I Remember's series whose
zero is already a reduced state. Induced seismicity was examined for the second sweep running and dropped again
for want of a clean no-villain cut, which is now worth recording as a probable dead end rather than a
recurring maybe.

**And the strike now rides the lane of the thing it strikes.** The gate written in the previous pass fired for
the first time on a live misfit branch, and it failed the misfit rather than the author: it demands that a
staged concept's line be struck, and the register is `management/**`, which the misfit lane may not touch. So
the gate as shipped was half a fix, and it made every misfit pull request red. The other half is that
`management/plan_fill_the_season.md` is now `shared` in `branchScope`, beside `registry.json`,
`docs/SCIENCE.md` and `REFERENCES.md`, which are shared for the same reason: the change that stages a thing is
the only change that can compute what the file should say about it. This removes the structural cause of the
lag rather than the symptom. The strike had lagged three times, two prose notes had told the next author to try
harder, and neither note could have worked, because the edit they asked for could not be made from the branch
they were read on. A misfit may now strike its own line, and record its own discards, in its own pull request.

**The first strike to ride a misfit lane.** Nothing Left to Pool struck its own line above, in the pull request
that staged it, which is what the previous pass made possible. The line had set three constraints and all three
survived contact: the cut held to the symmetric case, where everybody may read the same result and the pool
dissolves anyway, so no insurer hides behind a test; the boundary with The Market for Lemons came out sharper
than a merely adjacent one, because **the remedy for this misfit creates that one**, a bar on using the result
restoring a pool by putting the applicant in possession of what the underwriter may not have, which makes the
two the halves of one bind rather than variants of one mechanism; and the axis is `risk-information-precision`
rather than `risk-pool-members`, since the quantity acted on is how precisely the risk is measured and not how
many people are in the pool. The unavailability of Rothschild and Stiglitz, whose spine The Market for Lemons
carries, cost nothing and did the boundary work for free: a constraint that forces the distinction into prose
is worth more than the citation it withholds.

**Hirshleifer declared, and a heuristic corrected.** The house's Hirshleifer was David, in Information Cascade;
Nothing Left to Pool brought Jack, his father, so the surname is now declared and the index keys them apart.
Worth recording because it cuts against the rule the earlier passes wrote. Those found four collisions on
common surnames and concluded that a common surname is more likely than not to be taken already, which is true
and is why `--check` takes the full string. It does not run the other way. Hirshleifer is rare and was taken
anyway, because a rare surname is rare by belonging to a family, and academic families produce academics: the
property that makes a name look safe is the property that concentrates it. So the surname scan is
unconditional, not reserved for names that look crowded.

**The civil time line, staged as An Angle and a Count.** Struck in its own pull request, the second to ride its
own lane. The line's two named boundaries both held and the second one paid better than expected: Exactly One
Kilogram inverts this misfit rather than merely sitting beside it, since there the defining object cannot be
checked and here both quantities are checked continuously and still cannot both be satisfied, so the pair is a
measurement that cannot be wrong against a measurement that cannot be right. Both Take the Crown held on the
absence of a trigger, as written. The neighbour the line had not anticipated was **Right Apart, Wrong
Together**, which is the closest mechanism in the house, two correct things destroying something by being
brought together, and the distinction came from the remedy rather than from the setup: galvanic corrosion is
cured by insulating the metals, and the equivalent move here already exists, since atomic and Earth timescales
are published separately and the difficulty survives, because civil time is the one people write down. Worth
recording as a triage move: **where two misfits look alike at the setup, run the incumbent's remedy on the
candidate and see what it fails to reach.** That is the same test as removing the incumbent's engine, applied
from the other end, and it was quicker here because the setups were genuinely similar.

**Nelson declared, and a third outcome named for the surname scan.** The house's Nelson was Leif, in Reasonable
Either Way and The File Drawer; An Angle and a Count brought Robert A, so the surname is declared and the index
keys them apart, at 27 declared and none loose. The pass is worth recording for what else it turned up. That
misfit's scan hit **two** surnames and only one was a namesake: Kamp came back held, and the house's Kamp is
Poul-Henning, who coined bikeshedding, which is the same person who wrote the leap second's practitioner
argument ten years later. One scholar across two works is expected and is most of the index, so nothing was
owed.

Naming that outcome matters, because the scan has three and the register had only written two. A surname hit is
**the same person on another work**, in which case nothing is owed and declaring a homonym would be the error,
since it would split one person across two keys and defeat the wall it was meant to serve; or **two people**,
in which case the cells are resolved in the misfit's own lane and the form is declared in governance; or two
people on one work, which cannot happen and would be a shared-work finding if it appeared. So the scan does not
answer the question by itself: it locates a cell to read, and reading it is what decides the move. An author
who declares on the strength of the hit alone will eventually declare a homonym for one person, which fails
quietly rather than loudly and is the one direction the gates cannot see.

**And the slate is down to one line.** Archival Appraisal is all that remains open, so a sweep is due behind it.

**The appraisal line, staged as Kept for the Questions We Had, and the slate is now empty.** The line's named
boundary against The Survivors held and turned out to be the misfit's own strongest paragraph: passive attrition
can be corrected for, because the process that removed the sample can be modelled and a rate estimated, and a
reasoned selection cannot, because the reason **was** the mechanism. Worth keeping as a general form: where an
incumbent stages a loss, ask whether the loss is correctable in principle, since two misfits can share a shape
entirely and differ on whether anybody could ever undo them.

The candidate was tested against the shape this register held mandatory auditor rotation off for, one property
whose two effects cannot be separated, since selection both makes a record findable and makes it unable to
answer later questions. It is not that shape, and the test is worth recording. That trade is the **setup** here
rather than the mechanism: what carries the play is that the decision destroys the evidence by which it could
be judged, so the error class is undetectable in principle and the profession's own honest review confirms the
decision whatever the decision was. A misfit whose distinguishing vector survives when the trade-off is granted
to both sides is not on the held shape. **Ask what is left when the two effects are conceded.**

The pass also turned up a live undeclared namesake with a silent failure in it, recorded with the declaration
that follows.

**Cook and Greene declared, and the quiet direction found at last.** Kept for the Questions We Had brought Terry
Cook against the house's Richard, and Mark Greene against a **Greene that was already two people**: David the
psychologist in A Reason Too Many and Paid to Stop Caring, and David L the transport economist in Jevons
Paradox. That pair had sat undeclared and invisible, which is the safe direction and cost nothing, but writing
the declaration exposed something that is not safe. The build resolves a cell to the **first** declared form
matching `given === form` or `given.startsWith(form + " ")`, so `["David", "David L"]` keys `David L Greene` as
`Greene (David)` and merges two people, while `["David L", "David"]` is correct. Both orderings pass the
namesake wall, because under the wrong one nothing is left bare: every cell resolves, to the wrong person.

That is the failure the whole namesake apparatus exists to prevent, arriving through the apparatus itself, so
it now has a wall of its own and a `--forms` mode beside it. The general shape is worth more than the fix: **a
gate that reports a clean count is evidence about the question it asks and about nothing else.** The namesake
wall asks whether an occurrence is unresolved. It was never asked whether a resolution is the right one, and it
answered zero either way for as long as it has existed.

**The fifth field sweep, refilling an empty slate, and it changed where the yield is.** Six candidates were
drawn the way the previous four passes drew them, from named effects across thin fields, and **five were
already staged**: the ecological trap is The Ecological Trap, the Abilene paradox and pluralistic ignorance are
one misfit, the volunteer's dilemma is The Bystander Effect, the competency trap is inside The Innovator's
Dilemma, and second-order vaccination free-riding is The Vaccination Free-Rider. That hit rate is the finding.
At 270 the **named** effects are largely taken, so a sweep that goes looking for one is mostly running the dedup
it should have run first, and the two candidates that survived this pass came from somewhere else entirely:
from the **methodological constraints of disciplines**, the things a field cannot do rather than the things it
has named. The observer's paradox and second-order free-riding are both of that kind. Draw from there next.

**Two folded in from a reading of Hacking the American Mind and Evil by Design.** Both books' headline framings
are struck for the reason the male-flight and divergent-thinking briefs were struck: they are organised on a
deliberate manipulator, Evil by Design literally on the seven deadly sins, and a designer who chose to deceive
is a wrong rather than a misfit. Underneath, the house turned out to be well covered: escalation and tolerance
is More to Stand Still, attention as a business is You Are the Product, unread standard terms is the
informed-minority misfit, reward crowding out motive is A Reason Too Many and Paid to Stop Caring, and present
bias, endowment, choice overload and availability cascades all have plays. Cialdini is in the index only as a
backdrop row in Must Be a Catch and carries no spine. What survived is slated above: the unavailability of a
neutral default, and the optimiser that finds the lever, the second carrying its annexation test as a
precondition rather than as a note.

**And one strike worth more than the slate lines.** _Knowing does not disarm_ looked like the sharpest gap in
that territory and the sharpest thing the books get wrong, since naming a bias does not stop it working, the
way the arrows stay unequal after they are measured. It is not a gap. It appears in **eight** warrants already,
because it is the house's standing **No Hero** line: knowing the mechanism completely still leaves the seat
where it was. Staged as a play it would be a mould stamped from the restriction every misfit already carries.
Worth generalising, since this will recur: **a sentence that appears across many warrants is the house's own
convention and not an unstaged concept**, and the test is cheap, because the house's conventions are greppable
and a real candidate is not.

**And this pass corrected the fix that made it possible.** Giving the register a lane it could ride was done by
declaring it `shared`, and `shared` is the wrong primitive: it is for **build artefacts**, `registry.json`,
`docs/SCIENCE.md`, `REFERENCES.md`, which are regenerated by a change and are never the whole of one. A shared
path therefore owns no lane, so the moment a pass touched the register **alone**, which is exactly what a
sweep does, the guard refused to compute a branch and was right to. The primitive that already meant what was
wanted was sitting in the same block: a **rider**, which `management/orders/**` has always been, rides the lane
of the change it travels with and homes to `governance/` when committed by itself. Both directions verified
before this landed.

Worth keeping as a form, because the first fix passed every check it was tested against: **a change that only
ever appears alongside something else is not tested for appearing alone.** The strike always rode a misfit, so
the misfit lane was the only case exercised, and the case that failed was the one nobody had needed yet.

**The neutral-default line, staged as It Has to Start Somewhere, and a namesake deliberately not declared.** The
line's named boundary against An Angle and a Count held and sharpened into a pair worth remembering: that misfit
is a **conflict without a resolution**, two correct requirements on one quantity, and this one is a **decision
without an abstention**, one requirement and no neutral way to meet it. Two neighbours the line had not
anticipated both came from the same question, what survives when the obvious cause is granted. Loath to Part
holds the endowment effect, which explains part of why a setting sticks: grant a population with no attachment
to the status quo whatever and the form must still be printed in some arrangement, so the endowment effect
covers the adhesion and none of the necessity. The Third Option holds the decoy effect, which needs an option to
be **added**, where here the option set is unchanged and the condition it arrives in does the work.

**And the scan produced a hit that is better left alone, which is new.** Johnson is already four people in the
index, Nicholas L in Nobody Has to Launch Again, Wendell in The Abstraction Ladder, and two more left bare in
The Frozen Middle and Watermelon Reporting; Eric J here makes five, second only to Miller. It stays undeclared,
and the reason is the wall's own shape: declaring a surname obliges every occurrence of it to be resolved, and
two of those cells carry no given name that this pass can supply with confidence. **A declaration that cannot be
made confidently is worse than none**, because the wall then converts an honest gap into a guessed attribution
written into the index, where leaving it undeclared collates two people into one key and can only raise a
spurious overlap, which fails loudly. The safe direction stays safe precisely because it demands nothing. Welch
and Samuelson are in the same position and are recorded here for the same reason: Welch is Ivo in Information
Cascade and H Gilbert in Better in Both and It Saved My Life, and Samuelson is Paul in The Loud Few and The
Paradox of Thrift against William in The Winning Bid.

The pass also ran the three readings of a surname hit four times and returned **the first reading** on all four:
Thaler, Sunstein and Laibson are the same people the house already holds on other work, and so is the William
Samuelson of the status quo literature and of The Winning Bid. Nothing was owed on any of them. That is the
reading the register wrote down last week as the one most likely to be got wrong, and it is worth noting that
it is also the most common.

**The observer's paradox line, staged as Not While You Are Watching, and a work the check could not see.** Both
