boundaries the line demanded held: Metric Fixation is clean because nothing strategic happens here, the speaker
gaining nothing by any register and responding to a person rather than to a measurement, and The Same Print
Twice turned out to be the same joint from the other side, the observer moving there and the observed moving
here, with a procedure that fixes one and none that fixes the other. The line's instruction to carry Hawthorne
as contested rather than as the spine was right for a reason it had not anticipated, recorded below.

**The pre-authoring check cleared a work the house already holds.** `--check "Roethlisberger & Dickson ::
Management and the Worker"` returned clear, and Sandbag cites the same publication as "the Hawthorne bank wiring
room observations (1939)". Those are one book. The work key compares strings, so a work cited under its formal
title in one misfit and under a descriptive name in another does not collide, and the wall cannot see it. This
is not a defect to gate: normalising descriptions to titles is a judgement, and a check that tried would guess.
It is the same lesson the surname scan taught in a different place, and it is worth stating once in general:
**the checks locate cells to read and never decide, so an author who does not know the literature is not
protected by running them.** The move here was to cite neither, since the Hawthorne evidence is contested
anyway, and to name Sandbag in the warrant as holding that body of work for another mechanism, which discharges
the overlap in prose where a gate could not.

Levitt was declared the cheap way: the house held **Barbara** in The Success Trap's organizational learning row
and **Theodore** in its marketing myopia row, both in one misfit, so the misfit that brought **Steven** resolved
both cells on its own lane in its own pull request. Worth contrasting with the Johnson hit of the previous pass,
which stays undeclared: the difference is not the number of people but whether the author can attribute every
existing cell with confidence, and here both sat in one file and both were unambiguous. **A namesake is cheap
when its cells are few and certain, and the count is not what decides it.** Milroy and List were the first
reading again, the same people the house already holds on other works, and owed nothing.

**Levitt declared, at 30 surnames.** Barbara in The Success Trap's organizational learning row, Theodore in its
marketing myopia row, Steven arriving with Not While You Are Watching. Both incumbent cells sat in one file and
both were unambiguous, so the misfit resolved them on its own lane and this pass only writes the declaration.
Set beside the Johnson hit two passes ago, which stays undeclared with five people in it, the pair makes the
rule legible: **what decides whether a namesake is cheap is not how many people share the surname but whether
every existing cell can be attributed with confidence.** Johnson has more people and two cells nobody here can
attribute; Levitt had three people and every cell certain. The count is the wrong number to look at.

**Johnson declared at last, and the hold was the right call.** Five people now key apart: Eric J in It Has to
Start Somewhere, Nicholas L in Nobody Has to Launch Again, Wendell in The Abstraction Ladder, Gerry in The
Frozen Middle and Roy D in Watermelon Reporting, at 31 declared surnames with none loose and no unreachable
forms. The two bare cells were attributed off the papers' own author lines rather than from recollection, and
the check earned itself: confidence going in was high on Gerry and only moderate on Roy D, the moderate one is
the one that needed looking up, and the recollection that came with it placed him at the wrong institution
while getting the name right. **A memory that is right about the name and wrong about the affiliation feels
exactly like a memory that is right**, which is the whole reason the hold was correct and the reason to look
rather than to declare.

So the sequence is worth writing down as the standard shape for an expensive namesake: **hold it undeclared,
which costs nothing; record it here so it is not rediscovered; and declare it only when somebody has actually
read the author lines.** Two passes ago this surname was recorded as too uncertain to declare, and that entry
is what made this pass a lookup rather than a rediscovery.

**A triage of shareholder value against company value, and of long term against short term, which turned out to
be two questions with opposite answers.** The horizon question is **taken and taken well**: Seed Corn holds it
with the canonical literature, Narayanan, Stein, Graham with Harvey and Rajgopal, Laverty and the practitioner
mechanics, and its engine is measurement, that when only the near term is measured and rewarded managers spend
the far term to deliver it. Starting Tomorrow, The Success Trap and Boundary Critique sit around it. A horizon
candidate now would be an Origin row on that incumbent at best, so none is slated.

The ownership question is the opposite. **Not one scholar of the corporate governance literature is in the
index**: no Jensen, Meckling, Hansmann, Blair, Stout, Berle, Means, Rappaport, Bebchuk, Azar or Schmalz, and
every spine checked came back clear. For a house of 272 with 56 misfits in the markets field that is a
conspicuous hole, and it is the reason both lines above are slated ahead of what was already standing: an
entirely unrepresented field is a better reason to build than a good candidate in a crowded one.

**The framing had to be struck before anything could be slated.** Shareholders against stakeholders names a
culprit and a casualty, and takes the same strike as the male-flight brief and the reading of Evil by Design.
What survives underneath is structural and is written into the two lines: which party holds the control right
and how concentrated that party's exposure is, and what a proprietor indifferent between two rivals does to the
pressure between them.

**Two held, with the cut written.** _The price is the only quoted quantity_, that continuation value has no
price where exit value does, so every decision needing justification is justified in the quoted one, is held as
Seed Corn's **you get what you measure** wearing a different hat, and probably Metric Fixation's as well; it
returns if somebody finds a version where nothing is being measured. And _the norm mistaken for a rule_,
Stout's claim that American law does not in fact require share-price maximisation while everybody acts as
though it does, is a fine structure resting on a **contested reading of law**, and a misfit whose spine is a
disputed legal claim is thinner than one whose spine is a structure; it returns if the reading settles or if
the same shape is found somewhere the law is not in dispute.

**The control-right line, staged as Nobody Votes for the Plant, and the opposed pair it turned up.** The
boundary the line demanded against Seed Corn held on the test the line already carried, so nothing was
re-derived. But the misfit's nearest neighbour was not on the list, and it is in another literature entirely:
**The Only Basket**, which holds Fischel's homevoter and had no axis. The two act on one quantity, the
diversification of whoever holds the decision right, and their outcomes move in opposite directions. There the
household's whole wealth is in one undiversifiable asset **and it holds the local ballot**, so it defends that
asset and the cost falls on people who were never at the ballot; here the parties who cannot spread their stake
hold no vote at all and the party that does is diversified by duty. Both now declare `owner-diversification`
with opposite signs, and both name each other and say what sets the sign: **whether the concentrated exposure
and the decision right sit in the same hands.**

That is the fifth declared opposed pair and the first found by **building** rather than by the check, which is
worth reading carefully rather than as a win. The check iterates misfits that declare an axis, and The Only
Basket declared none, so the pair was invisible to it and would have stayed invisible however often it was run.
The thing that surfaced the pair was writing the neighbours section, which is the cheap wall the house already
has, and it found in one pass what a family sweep had not: **the two misfits share no scholar, no work, no
field convention and no vocabulary, and the only thing they have in common is the shape of the quantity.** So
the lesson is not about the checker but about coverage: an undeclared misfit is not merely unchecked, it is
unfindable, and the ratchet's grandfathered set is where the remaining pairs are.

Worth keeping as a general form too, since it generalises past this pair: **a lever with a failure at each end
is two misfits, not one.** Moving a vote toward an exposure and away from it are the same act, and the house
now stages both ends of it. Where a candidate looks like a complaint about too much of something, ask what the
misfit at the other end of the same lever would be, and whether the house already holds it.

**The common ownership line, staged as The Same Name on Both Registers.** The line's cut to the absence of an
instruction survived, and the objection it had to survive was the strongest one available: if nobody tells
anybody anything, how does the incentive ever arrive. The answer is a channel that carries no message. A
remuneration committee measuring an executive against their own results rather than the rival's argues its case
on fairness to one person, an argument that is sound and predates the whole question, and the same design
removes whatever reward there was for taking share from the competitor. **A transmission built for unrelated
reasons is the difference between a channel and a message**, and it is what lets the play answer the objection
without importing an instructor.

Three boundaries, one of them not anticipated by the line. Nobody Shops Around is the nearest mechanism in the
house, since the Diamond paradox also stages competition failing among firms that never communicate, and the
distinction is where to look: there the failure is on the **buyer's** side and cheap comparison repairs it,
where here comparison is free and the fares are published. The Race to the Bottom is the same variable from the
other end of its range, competition as the engine of a failure rather than its absence. And Nobody Votes for
the Plant, built the pull request before, needed holding apart carefully since both run on a diversified owner:
that one is **vertical**, what a barely exposed holder under-weights about one firm's continuation, and this
one is **horizontal**, what a proprietor of both is indifferent about between two firms. A fund holding four
hundred companies in four hundred unrelated industries produces the first and not the second, so the axes are
declared apart rather than shared.

Dennis was the cheap namesake again by the rule two passes ago: the house holds Brian in Safety in Numbers,
this misfit brings Patrick, one cell and both certain, resolved on the misfit's own lane. Salop was the first
reading, the same Steven the house already carries in Nobody Reads It, and owed nothing.

**Dennis declared, at 32 surnames, and a caution about routing around one.** Brian in Safety in Numbers against
Patrick in The Same Name on Both Registers: one cell, both certain, the cheap kind by the rule set two passes
ago. Third consecutive cheap namesake, and the rule is holding.

The caution is about a hit that never became a declaration. That misfit's scan also raised **Rock**, the house
holding Kevin in Sold to Find the Price against the Edward Rock of the common ownership commentary, and no
declaration was owed because the citation was not made: the dissent row went to Dennis, Gerardi and Schenone
instead. That was the right call **on the merits**, since a direct rebuttal in the same journal on the same
industry is a stronger dissent than a general antitrust commentary, and it would have been the choice with no
namesake anywhere in view. But it is worth writing down that the two reasons look identical from outside, and
only one of them is legitimate: **routing around a namesake is sound when the substitute is better on the
merits and is a corruption of the warrant when the substitute is merely cheaper.** The test is whether the
citation would still have been chosen had the surname been free, and an author who cannot answer that has
already been moved by the wrong thing. This register earlier refused to drop Greene and Meissner for exactly
this reason and paid three cells for it, which is the standing precedent.

**The second-order line, staged as And Then Who Makes Them.** Both boundaries the line named held, and one of
them sharpened into a test worth keeping. Borrowed Immunity stages the first order alone, so this misfit takes
that as its setup and begins where the incumbent ends. Everybody's Business needed more care than expected,
because a bystander who says nothing and a witness who says nothing look identical from outside: the
distinction is that a bystander's responsibility is **diffused** and is repaired by naming somebody, where here
nothing is diffused and everybody knows exactly whose turn it was. **Naming a bystander works; naming an
enforcer transfers the private cost onto the named person and hands them the same reason to decline.** That is
the decisive difference and it is a remedy test rather than a setup test, which is the move this register wrote
down when An Angle and a Count was held against Right Apart, Wrong Together.

The unanticipated neighbour was **The Commons**, and the relation is not adjacency but dependence. Ostrom's
design principles answer the tragedy with monitoring and graduated sanctions, and those are exactly what this
misfit is about: the monitoring has to be done by somebody, the sanction applied by somebody, and the cost of
being that somebody is what the principles assume is met rather than supply. So the incumbent's remedy is this
misfit's subject, which is a relation the house has not had before and is worth naming: **where an incumbent's
answer requires an act, ask what that act costs whoever performs it, and whether the answer assumes the cost
away.**

No namesakes owed. Ostrom was the first reading, the same Elinor the house already carries in The Commons on
another work, which is now the commonest outcome of a surname hit by a wide margin.

**The optimiser line, tested and resolved as an extension rather than a play, which is the answer its own slate
line demanded.** The line was written with the annexation test as a precondition and not a note, so the test was
run first, against The Letter of the Law, and it failed. **The house's decisive test is to remove the
incumbent's engine and see whether the failure survives**, and the incumbent's engine is a proxy objective that
is not the intent. Remove it: suppose a metric that perfectly captured whether the person was served, and run
the same continuous experimentation against that. The search would find designs that genuinely serve people.
What makes it fail is exactly that conversion is a proxy for being served, which is the incumbent's engine
operating and not a second one.

The **no tell** claim, which the line had named as the thing to establish or abandon, is abandoned. It is real
as an observation, since a dark pattern looks like an interface a reasonable person would have drawn where a
boat spinning in circles does not, but it is a property of the **domain** rather than of the mechanism.
Detectability of the output is not what distinguishes two failures; it is what distinguishes two settings a
single failure occurs in. Worth keeping as a general form: **a candidate whose only difference from an
incumbent is how the result looks is a domain, not a spine.**

What the domain **did** supply is worth more than the play would have been. The incumbent's warrant was written
entirely out of the AI safety literature, and its own boundary against Metric Fixation claimed the engine is a
powerful and often non-human **optimiser**, positioning the misfit against a person displacing a goal. The
online experimentation case shows the mechanism running with **no optimiser at all**: a selection over thousands
of independent human judgements, none of them gaming anything, converging on the same degenerate maxima. So the
incumbent's own claim was too narrow, the extension corrects it, and the misfit now sits between a person
displacing a goal and a machine topping an objective rather than only at the second. An Origin row that
**narrows an incumbent's over-claim** is a better outcome than a play that would have restated it, and it is the
first time an extension has corrected the warrant it was extending rather than merely widening it.

Narayanan is recorded and not owed. The row is an event row on the merits, since the claim is about
experimentation as a practice rather than about one paper, so the Source cell carries no surname and the
taxonomies sit in the Key Work cell. But Arvind Narayanan is an author of the Mathur paper and the house holds
M P Narayanan in Seed Corn, so anybody who later moves that citation into a Source cell inherits a namesake.
Written down here so it is met rather than discovered.

**The sixth field sweep, drawn where the fifth pass said the yield was, and the seam paid at about one in two.**
The fifth sweep found the house's named effects largely taken and pointed the next author at **the
methodological constraints of disciplines**, the things a field cannot do rather than the things it has named.
That instruction was followed here and it worked, which is worth recording as much as any of the candidates:
six territories examined, four slated, one held, one struck on dedup. That is a much better rate than the
fifth pass got from named effects, and the seam should be worked again before anybody goes back to hunting
effects with names.

**The dedup strike was the instructive one, because it landed twice.** Legal doctrine develops only through
cases somebody could afford to bring, so the body of law is a selection of disputes rather than of questions,
and there is no mechanism for developing doctrine on what nobody litigated. The house holds that twice over,
as **The Priest-Klein Selection Hypothesis** and as **The Repeat Player Advantage**, and a candidate this
author thought was a fresh field turned out to be a well-covered one. The check that found it was a concept
scan of `REFERENCES.md` and it cost one command, which is the standing argument for running the concordance
before the literature.

**Held, with the cut written.** _Clinical equipoise dissolving as the trial runs_, where the licence to
randomise depends on genuine professional uncertainty and the accumulating evidence removes that licence before
it becomes definitive, is held against **Truncation Bias in Stopped Trials**: the incumbent stages stopping
early and the overstatement it produces, which is most of this candidate's ground. It returns if somebody
separates the **ethical** dissolution of equipoise from the **statistical** consequence of acting on it, since
only the second is staged.

**Struck, and for a reason the previous pass wrote down.** The _last speaker_ problem, that a language with one
fluent speaker cannot be documented in conversation because conversation needs two, is a **domain** rather than
a spine: what happens there is Not While You Are Watching, elicitation producing a register addressed to the
linguist, sitting on top of Language Shift. The form applied is the one written into the register when the
optimiser line failed its test: **a candidate whose only difference from an incumbent is where it happens is a
domain, not a spine.** If it earns anything it is an Origin row on the observer's paradox, and the register
should say so rather than let the next author rediscover it as a candidate.

**Two of the four are slated with an annexation test as a precondition rather than a note.** That device has now
been used three times and has been decisive twice: it resolved the optimiser line as an extension and it is
what made the excavation line defensible, since the test against Kept for the Questions We Had was run in the
sweep and is written into the line rather than left to whoever builds it. Worth keeping as the default shape for
any candidate whose nearest neighbour is already in the house: **write the test into the line, not into a note
beside it.**

**The excavation line, staged as Taken Apart to Be Read, and a false clear from the surname scan that turned out
to be a defect in the index.** The line's Kept for the Questions We Had test had been run in the sweep and held,
which is the second time writing the test into the line rather than beside it has paid: nothing was re-derived
and the boundary paragraph was written from the sweep's own words. The two neighbours the line had not
anticipated were both about severity rather than kind. Not While You Are Watching is the same joint one degree
milder, since there the observed **survives** altered and can be recorded again where here it does not survive
at all. Held Still to Keep It inverts the curator's difficulty: there preservation **changes** the thing and the
change is legible, here preservation changes nothing and reveals nothing, so what cannot be established is not
how the site was altered but whether it is there.

**The scan said Lucas was clear and it was wrong.** The house has held Robert E Lucas since True Until It Was
Used, and the Source cell read `Robert E. Lucas Jr.`, so the build took the last token as the surname and keyed
him under **Jr**. He was not findable under Lucas by any scan, and citing Gavin Lucas would have put two people
in the index with no collision raised and no gate fired. One other cell had the same defect, `John C. Bailar
III` keyed under **III**. Both are repaired in the misfit's own lane, which creates the Lucas namesake honestly
and hands it to the governance pass that follows.

This is the quiet direction for the third time, and the three instances now make a pattern worth stating
plainly: **the namesake apparatus fails silently whenever the surname is not the last token of the Source
cell.** A declared form ordered wrongly, a surname resolved to the wrong person and a suffix taken for a surname
are three faces of one thing, which is that the index key is computed from a cell written by an author and
nothing checks that the computation found a person. Each was caught by eye and none by a gate. The gate that
would have caught this one is narrow and is owed with the declaration: **no index key may be a generational
suffix**, which is a closed list and costs nothing to assert.

**Lucas declared and the suffix walled, at 33 surnames and 21 tests.** Gavin in Taken Apart to Be Read against
Robert E in True Until It Was Used, both certain and one cell each, so the cheap kind again. The wall is the
narrow one the previous pass said was owed: **no index key may be a generational suffix**, verified in both
directions against the real shape of the defect, which is a bare `Jr` key and not a `Lucas Jr` one. The first
attempt at that verification injected the wrong shape and passed, which is worth a line of its own: **a gate
tested against a defect you have reconstructed from memory is tested against your memory.** The real key was
still in the previous build and could have been read instead of imagined.

The three walls now standing on the scholar key are one shape seen three times, and the register should stop
treating each as a surprise. `--forms` catches a declared form ordered so that another form absorbs it.
`--namesakes` catches a declared surname left bare. `--suffixes` catches a key that is not a name at all. What
they have in common is that **the index key is computed from a cell an author wrote and nothing checks that the
computation found a person**, and in each case every other gate passed, the counts read clean, and a person
found it by eye. A fourth will look like those three: quiet, arithmetically fine, and wrong about who somebody
is. The place to look is anywhere the surname is not the last token of the Source cell.

**The calibration line, staged as A Hundred Years to Vouch for a Thousand, and its annexation test passed
cleanly.** True Until It Was Used runs on **use**: an act invalidates a relation, and the invalidation arrives
afterwards. Remove that engine and this stands entire, because nobody uses a reconstruction to change the
climate of 1300 and the question of whether the relation extends exists whether or not the series is ever read.
That is the third time a test written into the line rather than beside it has decided the matter before any
files existed, and the second time it has come out in the candidate's favour.

**The spine turned out to be the discipline's own most uncomfortable result, which is a better warrant than the
one the line anticipated.** The line was written around the structural point, that a proxy is validated over the
span it is not needed for. The literature supplies something stronger: the divergence problem, where
high-latitude density series that had tracked temperature for centuries stopped tracking it after about 1960.
So the single stretch in which the stability of the relation was examinable against an instrument is the
stretch where it did not hold, and the people who found it published it themselves. **A field that has already
found and published the worst version of its own difficulty gives a warrant its spine for free**, and it is
worth looking for that before settling on a structural argument: the structural claim was going to be the
spine, and the empirical one is both sharper and better attested.

Two neighbours the line had not named. Overfitting is the failure the held-back verification exists to catch,
and it catches it, so the standard repair is applied in full and aimed elsewhere: the success of a correct
guard is not a false comfort anybody manufactured. The Streetlight Effect looks identical from outside and
differs on whether an elsewhere exists: there the light is merely convenient and the reproach is available,
here there is no second overlap and its location was fixed by when the thermometer was invented. **Where a
candidate resembles a misfit about looking in the easy place, ask whether a hard place exists at all.**

No namesakes owed, and three were avoided on the merits rather than by routing round them. Mann, Bradley and
Hughes would have raised Hughes, Cook would have needed a third form and Jones a declaration, and none of the
three is the spine: the divergence result is, and the famous reconstruction belongs to a different controversy
about method that this misfit does not stage. The test from the Rock caution applies and passes: those
citations would not have been chosen with the surnames free either.

**The sham-control line, staged as A Lower Bar Set by a Higher One.** Both boundaries the line named held, and
the Not While You Are Watching one needed the sharper cut the line had only gestured at: there the target is
**specified** by the observer's absence, so covert recording is barred as a route to something that could not be
had cleanly anyway, where here the target is ordinary efficacy, is perfectly well defined, and is measurable by
a design that exists and is understood completely. What distorts is not the measurement of any one treatment
but **the comparison across two classes**, one of which can afford its control arm and one of which cannot. That
is the misfit's own distinguishing vector and it was found by pushing on the neighbour rather than on the
candidate.

The unanticipated neighbour was **The Bounce**, and the relation is the one this register named two passes ago
when And Then Who Makes Them met The Commons: **dependence rather than adjacency**. Regression to the mean is
one of the three reasons the post-operative improvement carries no information, and its standard remedy is a
control group, which is exactly what this misfit is about not having. So the incumbent names the hazard and
this play stages why the repair for it cannot be applied to a whole class of treatment. That relation has now
appeared twice, and it is worth stating as a form: **where an incumbent's remedy is the thing a candidate
cannot obtain, the two are not competitors and the candidate should say so plainly.**

A caught error worth recording, because the gate would not have caught it either. The warrant first named the
neighbour as **Regression to the Mean**, which is the concept and not the house title: the title is The Bounce.
The neighbour wall requires a real house title anywhere in the file, and the warrant already named two others,
so it would have passed with a neighbour paragraph pointing at nothing. **A misfit can satisfy the neighbour
wall and still misname a neighbour**, and the wall cannot see it, because it tests presence rather than
correspondence. Nothing here needs a gate, since the concordance is one command away, but the next author
should know that naming a neighbour by its concept reads exactly like naming it by its title and fails
silently.

No namesakes owed, and one first reading worth noting because it was so close: Horng and Miller are **both**
already in the house, together, in Treated and Counted on a different paper, and Franklin Miller is already a
declared form. So the pair arrived pre-resolved and the scan cost nothing.

**The type-specimen line, staged as The Name Does Not Move, and widened in the building.** Its annexation test
against Exactly One Kilogram passed as written and produced the sharpest sentence in the warrant: there the
artefact **is** the unit, so the drift is undetectable from inside, where here a reviser measures the holotype
against two hundred specimens, publishes, and is believed the same afternoon. **One misfit stages an error that
cannot be detected; this one stages an error that is detected, published, agreed and then knowingly left in
place.** The remedies confirm it, which is the check worth keeping: theirs needed a new definition of the
kilogram, and no definition helps here.

**The line named one anchor and the building found two.** It was written around the type specimen alone, and
the codes anchor twice, to an object and to a date, and the second is the sharper instance: **priority** removes
discretion as completely as the type does, which is what makes it work and what leaves it unable to tell a
binomial in universal use from a description nobody has cited since 1830. Both are one mechanism with two
instruments rather than two mechanisms, since both anchors are chosen for being uncontestable rather than for
being right, so the play carries both without breaching Cut to Fit. Worth recording as a question to ask at
build time: **when a slate line names one instrument of a mechanism, look for the others before casting**, since
a play built on one of two anchors would have been thin and would have read as complete.

The unanticipated neighbour was **Bug for Bug**, which is the closest mechanism in the house and separates on
**who chose**: there the constraint is somebody's earlier mistake, faithfully preserved because too much was
built on it, and a clean world would not have it; here nobody made a mistake and the constraint was written down
on purpose by people who had considered exactly this case. That is a distinction the house has not drawn before
and generalises: **two misfits can share the shape of stability-over-correctness and differ entirely on whether
the thing being preserved was ever an error.**

No namesakes owed. The warrant is built on codes, commissions and an alternative system rather than on
individuals, which is the second time an event-heavy Origin table has been the honest form rather than a dodge,
and the first time it has been so because the field's authorities genuinely are documents.

**The slate is empty again**, four lines struck in four passes, and the sixth sweep's seam is still the standing
instruction for the seventh.

**The seventh field sweep, on the same seam, and the seam is thinning.** The sixth pass worked the
methodological constraints of disciplines and paid about one in two; this one worked the same ground and paid
about one in three, with three slated out of eight territories examined. That is still better than the fifth
pass got from named effects, so the instruction stands, but the trend is worth writing down rather than
discovered again: **the seam is real, is not inexhaustible, and each pass leaves the next one a smaller field.**

**Two of the three strikes were held twice over, which is the signal worth reading.** Warning under uncertainty,
where a body must call an evacuation on a signal that usually means nothing, is Alarm Fatigue **and** The
Forecaster's Dilemma. Successful prevention being invisible, where a measure that works produces no event to
point at, is The Firefighting Trap **and** Rose's Prevention Paradox. A single incumbent means a territory is
occupied; **two incumbents mean it is covered**, and a candidate that lands on a doubly held territory was
drawn from a part of the map the house has already worked thoroughly. Where the dedup returns two, the lesson
is about the direction of the search rather than about the candidate.

**And the third strike is the form working at a distance.** Conservation biology cannot run a controlled trial
on the last population, so the species whose management most needs evidence get the least-evidenced management.
That is **A Lower Bar Set by a Higher One**, built two passes ago: the structure is identical and only the
barrier differs, extinction risk in place of harm to a volunteer. Struck as a **domain rather than a spine** by
the rule the optimiser line established, and it is the first time a candidate has been struck against a misfit
this register itself built rather than against an inherited one. If anybody wants it, it is an Origin row on
that misfit and not a play.

That points at something the register should do and does not: it records new misfits by **title and reasoning**,
and the strike above only worked because the author happened to remember the structure. **A candidate is struck
against a mechanism, not against a name**, so a register that lists titles is helping less than it could. Not a
change to make in a sweep, and worth carrying to whoever next has cause to touch the file's shape.

**Two of the three lines carry an annexation test as a precondition**, and one of them carries something
stronger: the private-report line names the concession that will decide it, that randomisation makes group means
comparable in expectation and is a good answer, so the candidate lives or dies on whether the individual
decision survives granting that. **Writing the concession into the line, and not only the test, is the sharper
version of the device**, because it names what the author must beat rather than only what they must be clear of.
